[\[5a.3\]]de las byung na/_/sgrub pa brjod pa don med do/_/rtag phyir gso byed min pa la/_/de ni lhan cig byed ma yin/_/gang dag yod na 'byung mthong la/_/med na nam yang ma yin pa/_/de yi rgyu gzhan nyid rtog na/_/khyed la ji ltar thug med min/_/byed pa po nyid bkag pa'i phyir/_/kun 
[\[5a.4\]]shes nyid kyang gsal [#]bar ni/_/rtogs bya de yi stobs nyid kyis/_/kun shes nyid du 'thad phyir ro/_/ji skad bshad pa'i nyes pa yis[#]/_/sgrub rnams sun phyung ma gyur kyang /_/'on kyang 'khrul pa mthong ba'i phyir/_/byed pa po gcig ma grub na/_/thams cad shes nyid ci la [#]brten/_/
[\[5a.5\]]de sgrub phyir ni sgrub smras pa/_/rgyal dpog pa rnams la mdzes so/_/de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i rab tu byed pa las dbang phyug brtag pa ste gnyis pa'o//_//de ltar rang bzhin dbang phyug dag_/so sor rgyu nyid bkag pa'i phyir/_/gnyis ka lhan cig pa 'di yang /_/skye ba can

[\[5b.1\]]'gyur/_/gang tshe mun pa'i nus pa ni/_/shas chen po dang sbyor ba na/_/de tshe 'di ni 'gro ba kun/_/'jigs [#]par byed pa yin zhes grag[# ]/de ltar rdul dang snying stobs sogs/_/dngos [#]rnams 'di yi lhan cig byed/_/rim pa kho nar 'byung 'gyur te/_/de phyir 'bras bu rim med 
[\[5b.2\]]yin/_/'dir brjod bya ste 'di dag ni/_/gcig byed tshe na srid dam gcig_/sgrub pa'i mthu ste yod dam med/_/gal te yod na 'byin tshe gzhan/_/gnyis pa yang ni yod par 'gyur/_/de bzhin gzhan zhig yod na yang /_/gnyis pa gzhan du thal bar 'gyur/_/de yi ngo bo gzhan 
[\[5b.3\]]gyis ni/_/gzhan gyi [#]byed po ma yin pas/_/'di yi rang bzhin 'di nyid ni/_/de yi bye brag ga la yod/_/de yi nus pa dang bral na/_/gzhan tshe de yi skyed pa por/_/mi 'gyur nus pas stong pa'i phyir/_/nam mkha' yi ni me tog bzhin/_/nus pa shas chen ngo bo yang /_/gal 
[\[5b.4\]]te de tsam [(]kyi [,]gyi [)]rgyu na/_/rgyu yi ngo bo rtag nyi'i [#]phyir/_/dus kun du ni de yod 'gyur/_/gzhan dag gis gzhan mi 'dod pa/_/de phyir gzhan las kyang de min/_/res 'ga' 'byung ba nyid kyi phyir/_/de ni rang dbang can yang yin/_/rang las byung na rgyu nyid min/_/rang la byed pa 'gal phyir ro/_/
[\[5b.5\]]dngos po rnams ni ltos pa yis/_/res 'ga' ba nyid srid pa yin/_/'di ltar rang gi bdag nyid la/_/rang dngos bya ba 'jug mi 'dod/_/bdag nyid grub par gyur pa yang /_/gnas skabs 'di la grub phyir ro/_/bdag gi de nyid ma grub pa/_/'ga' la byed pa 'gal nyid med[#]/_/

[\[5b.7\]]rgyur mi smra/_/pad+ma'i ge sar la sogs pa'i/_/sna tshogs gang gis byed 'gyur ba'am/_/rma bya'i mdongs la sogs pa yis/_/sna tshogs pa ni [#]gang gis sprul/_/dper na tsher ma la sogs pa'i/_/rno sogs [#]rgyu med can nyid de/_/res 'ga' 'byung phyir de bzhin du/_/sdug bsngal 
[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]la sogs rgyu med nyid/_/rjes su 'gro ldog ldan pa yi/_/mtsho skyes ge sar la sogs pa/_/gnas skabs khyad par ldan pa yi/_/sa bon 'dam dang chu sogs can/_/mngon sum mi dmigs pa dag gi_/rgyu ni nges pa nyid yin na/_/gang tshe 'di dag gi rgyun 
[\[6a.2\]]ni/_/gzhan gang yin zhes 'dri bar byed/_/yul dang dus kyi nges pa dag_/ji ltar dngos po rnams la yod/_/gal te 'di yi de rgyu min/_/de dag kun du kun 'byung tshe/_/gang du gang tshe 'ga' zhig la/_/de las gzhan pa spangs nas ni/_/nges pa nyid du 'byung 'gyur ba/_/de dag 
[\[6a.3\]]de la ltos pa yin/_/de ltos de ltar 'byung ba yin/_/ltos pa 'bras bu nyid du brjod/_/mngon sum gyis kyang de ltar 'byung /_/des na [#]'di la rgyu dag 'grub/_/yang na de la zhes byar sbyar/_/de phyir rang bzhin smra ba 'di/_/mngon sum gyis ni rab gnod 'gyur/_/mngon sum dang ni mi 
[\[6a.4\]]dmigs pas/_/rgyu yi ngo bo nges phyir ro/_/rgyu yod par ni sgrub don du[#]/_/tshad ma yod nyid ma gyur mod/_/de lta na yang tshad med pas/_/don med nyid du nges pa min[#]/_/gang phyir don ni yod pa yi/_/tshad ma khyab byed dang rgyu min/_/tha dad par ni yod phyir dang /_/'khrul phyir de las 
[\[6a.5\]]byung phyir ro/_/dngos gang de lta'i rnam pa min/_/de nyid dang ni ldog pa las/_/gzhan ldog par ni nges pa ru/_/mi rtogs [#]'brel ba med phyir ro/_/kun gyis ma mthong the tshom dang /_/rang gis [#]ma mthong 'khrul pa ste/_/'bigs byed ri phug ngur pa sogs/_/ma mthong bar yang 
[\[6a.6\]]yod phyir ro/_/gal te rgyu med can nyid du/_/bsgrub phyir gtan tshigs mi sbyor na/_/tshad ma med par mi 'grub ste/_/de yi phyir na phyogs mi 'grub/_/gal te de grub phyir gtan tshigs/_/rab tu sbyor ba de lta na'ang /_/grub pa de rgyus bskyed nyid phyir/_/khyed kyi phyogs kyi 'grub mi 
[\[6a.7\]]'gyur/_/de ltar shes byed gtan tshigs dang /_/de gsal byed pa'i yang de ni/_/grub pa'i rgyu mtshan nyid gyur pa/_/bsgrub bya'i shes byed yin par bshad/_/de phyir byed po [#]'di nyid ni/_/shes par byed pa'i [#]rgyur 'dod do/_/bsgrub [#]bya'i skyed byed ma yin phyir/_/byed pa por ni mi brjod 
[\[6b\]]
[\[6b.1\]]do/_/de phyir dngos po gzhan dag kyang /_/rgyu bcas nges las skyed phyir ro/_/khyed kyi bsgrub bya'i don yul can/_/shes pa bzhin du sgrub [#]'byung phyir/_/de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i rab tu byed pa las 'gro ba rang bzhin du smra ba brtag pa ste bzhi pa'o//_//skye 'jig med par yang 
[\[6b.2\]]dag ldan/_/sgra yi rang bzhin tshangs pa mchog_/gang de yongs su gyur pa ni/_/dngos po'i grong nyid rab shes bya/_/de ltar gang dag brjod pa la'ang /_/de la brjod bya ba 'di ni/_/rang gi sgra yi ngo bo ni/_/btang nas sngo sogs nyid thob bam/_/de lta min na gang gi tshe/_/dang po'i phyogs 
[\[6b.3\]]rten de yi tshe/_/yi ge nyid dang bral 'gyur te/_/sngar yod dag ni 'jig phyir ro/_/ji ltar de ma thag pa'i phyogs/_/de la snga [#]sogs myong ba'i tshe/_/'on pa la yang gsal por ni/_/sgra yi bdag nyid myong bar 'gyur/_/gang gis gtso bor 'jug pa yis/_/thams cad sgra yi ngo 
[\[6b.4\]]bor gnas/_/sgra yi ngo bo ma btang na/_/yongs su 'gyur bar brjod phyir ro/_/sngon po la sogs gcig nyid du/_/phal pa min par rnam gnas na/_/de rig [#]pa yi dus su ni/_/ji ltar 'di rig yod ma yin/_/'di reg med na sngo sogs kyang /_/rab tu myong bar mi 'gyur te/_/

[\[7a\]]
[\[7a.1\]]bzhin na/_/de lta na yang 'gyur med phyir/_/de las rim par [#]'byung ba med/_/rang bzhin gcig po de nyid las/_/gzhan gzhan ngo bor 'byung min na/_/don gyi ngo bor yongs gyur par/_/de la ji ltar brjod par bya/_/de yi ngo bo min las ldog_/'ji ba'i ngo bo nyid

[\[8a.1\]]'gyur/_/ba rgya ba lang [#]rang bzhin gyi/_/snal ma'i rgyur ni [#]mi 'dod de/_/gang phyir srog chags za 'dod las/_/kha chu yi ni dra ba byed/_/gal te ci [#]zhig ltar 'jug na/_/'di yi 'byung ldan ci 'dra zhig_/gang gi phyir na nya ba yang /_/ma dpyad las la 'jug mi 'gyur/_/

[\[8b.1\]]gis shes pa bzhin/_/rnam pa gzhan du [#]mtshams sbyor ba/_/rgyu med par ni skye mi 'gyur/_/bdag ces bya ba'i tshig 'di ni/_/blo dang dbang sogs tshogs pa las/_/tha dad par ni rjod byed yin[#]/_/rab grags mi las tha dad na/_/gang phyir tshig gcig nyid 'dod phyir/_/gang yang

[\[10a.1\]]can gyi ni/_/sems pa'i ngo bo nyid du 'dod/_/sems pa'i blo yi mtshan nyid can/_/dper na sbrul 'khyil gnas skabs na/_/ldog cing de ma thag tu ni/_/drang po'i gnas skabs byung na yang /_/sbrul nyid du ni rjes 'jug 'gyur/_/de bzhin bdag ni rtag tu ni/_/

[\[10b.2\]]rtogs [#]bya'i phyir/_/da ltar [#]rig par byed po bzhin/_/shes byed [#]de yang khar [#]rtsang yod/_/shes byed phyir dang de nyid phyir/_/khar [#]rtsang shes pa po bzhin no/_/shes pa de dag bsgrub bya nyid/_/rgyud gcig dang 'brel [#]shes pa po'i/_/nga shes shes pa nyid kyi phyir/_/khar [#]rtsang de ring yod pa

[\[15a\]]
[\[15a.1\]]can kun 'jig pa la/_/thams cad du ni ltos pa med/_/'jig pa'i rgyu rnams rnam kun du/_/[(]da [,]de [)]la cung zad byed med phyir/_/'di ltar 'jig par byed pa'i rgyu/_/dngos la tha dad med pa yi/_/'jig pa'i byed por mi rigs te[#]/_/dngos po rang rgyu las skye phyir/_/
[\[15a.2\]]'di la sgrib [#]sogs kyang mi rig_/'jig pa [#]zhes bya'i [#]dngos po yi/_/dngos po'i 'jig byed ces bya [#{]brdzun[,]rdzun[}]/_/gzhan nyid la sogs rnam rtog dag_/don de la yang 'jug phyir ro/_/dngos po'i ngo bo med bdag nyid/_/'jig pa zhes bya'i med [#]gzhan can/_/'jig byed ce na de la yang /_/
[\[15a.3\]]byed pa rig pa [#]ldan ma yin/_/med pa yang ni 'bras nyid na/_/myu gu sogs bzhin dngos nyid du/_/thal 'gyur bskyed bya'i ngo bo ni/_/rgyu yi [#]nus pas skyed [#]phyir ro/_/de ltar dngos med sgrub sgo nas/_/byas yin ma yin dgag rten phyir/_/gang yin de la tha dad sogs/_/slar yang 
[\[15a.4\]]rnam rtog 'jug par 'gyur/_/'on te bya ba bkag pa [#]'di/_/dngos nyid byed pa ma yin nyid/_/de lta na yang rgyu med grub/_/byed po min na rgyu nyams phyir/_/'o na dngos po yod dus su/_/'jig pa 'di yod ma yin nyid/_/sngon min yun ring phyis [#]kyang min/_/dngos po'i de ma thag tu 'di/_/
[\[15a.5\]]de lta na yang dus nges phyir/_/'di ni rgyu ldan regs [#]pa yin/_/ltos med pa la res 'ga' dang /_/'brel pa sun [#]ni phyung ba yin/_/dngos po'i de ma thag tu ni/_/'byung ba'i phyir dang ma byung las/_/'byung phyir 'di ni rgyu ldan rigs/_/dper na skad cig gzhan bzhin 'dod/_/mo gsham gyi ni bu 
[\[15a.6\]]bzhin du/_/rgyu med phyir 'di cung zad med/_/yang na mkha' bzhin rtag gyur [#]te/_/gang phyir rnam pa gzhan med do/_/med pa yin na 'jig med phyir/_/dngos po thams cad rtag par 'gyur/_/'dus byas thams cad 'jig pa yi/_/shes pa'ang rgyu mtshan med pa can/_/rtag pa nyid 
[\[15a.7\]]na'ang 'jig pa med/_/lhan cig gnas 'gyur 'gal med phyir/_/skye ba med la'ang 'jig bshad pa/_/rigs [#]pa'i rjes 'brangs nyid ma yin/_/de lta bu la gzhan dag gis/_/ji lta bu zhig 'jig par rtog_/skad cig mar gnas chos rnams kyi/_/dngos nyid min nam 'on te na/_/
[\[15b\]]
[\[15b.1\]]dngos po'i ngo bo ldog gyur pa[#]/_/zhig myong can la de ltar brjod/_/de 'dir 'jig pa'i rang bzhin gang /_/chos rnams skad cig mar gnas pa'i/_/dngos med de ltar brjod dam ci/_/gzhan la brgal zhing brtag [#]pa mtshungs/_/'on te dngos po'i rang ngo bo/_/ldog pa 'jig pa'i ming 
[\[15b.2\]]yin na/_/snga ma la ni rtag byed pa[#]/_/'gal ba cung zad med pa nyid/_/skad cig mar gnas dngos gang la/_/'jig pa zhes ni rjod byed na/_/de ni rgyu dang rnam par 'dod/_/gzhan dngos med phyir rgyu med can/_/dngos po'i de ma thag 'byung nyid/_/de 'dra de la yod min te/_/g.yo 
[\[15b.3\]]ba dngos po'i rang ngo bo/_/dngos dang lhan cig skye phyir ro/_/de phyir 'jig pa yod pa'i phyir/_/'dus byas thams cad rtag pa min/_/'jig pa zhes pa'i blo dag kyang /_/rgyu mtshan med par thal mi 'gyur/_/zhig pa nyid ni bdag med phyir/_/de ma thag 'byung yod pa min/_/ma byung byung ba 
[\[15b.4\]]ldan pa yang /_/nam mkha'i pad+ma la sogs bzhin/_/zhig 'gyur zhes bya de ltar 'dir/_/dngos por ma gyur zhes bya yi/_/don 'di rtogs par byed yin gyi/_/bsgrub pa 'ga' yang mi 'dod do/_/mi 'ga' zhig la bong bu zhes/_/de ltar ming tsam byas pa na/_/bong bu'i chos rnams 
[\[15b.5\]]thams cad ni/_/'di las thob [#]'gyur ma yin no/_/zhig pa zhes bya'i dngos po ni/_/dngos po bcom pa zhes bya ba/_/sgrub pa na yang dngos 'di la/_/cung zad kyang ni skye med phyir/_/'di ni ji ltar ldog par 'gyur/_/'jig pa dngos po zhig bdag nyid/_/ji ltar dngos po'i ngo bo dang /_/
[\[15b.6\]]bral phyir med pa nyid 'dod kyi/_/dngos po ngo bo min phyir min/_/'di la ldog pa'i dngos nyid kyang /_/sgrub pa'i sgo nas rjod mi byed/_/dngos po yi ni rjes 'jug pa/_/skad cig las phyis [#]'gegs par byed/_/de phyir 'di yi rnam gzhag gi/_/ngo bo sgrub pa cung zad med/_/de phyir 'di la 
[\[15b.7\]]rtag [#]pa yi/_/rnam rtog byed pa gnas med do/_/nam mkha' la sogs dngos gang dang [#]/_/ma byas par ni yang dag 'dod/_/dngos po 'jug pa yod min te/_/de dag yod nus bral phyir ro/_/skad cig skad cig min sogs rtog[# ]/de tshe de la gnas med do/_/gang gis dngos nyid skad 
[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]cig ma'am/_/gal te rnam pa gzhan du 'gyur/_/skad cig gnas pa'i rang bzhin gang /_/dngos po skad cig ma ru brjod/_/brten pa'i ngo bor gnas pa yi/_/dngos nyid skad cig pa ma yin/_/skyes ma thag tu mi gnas pa'i/_/dngos po'i ngo bo gang yin pa[#]/_/
[\[16a.2\]]skad cig tu brjod de gang la/_/yod pa skad cig mar 'dod do/_/don la tha dad med na yang /_/de 'di la 'di zhes [#]gnod med/_/'dod pas sbyar ba'i brda tsam gyi/_/dngos po rjod [#]par byed pa yin/_/byung ma thag tu mi gnas pa'i/_/dngos la de ltar brjod 'dod pa/_/de la sgra ni rkyen bcas 
[\[16a.3\]]pa 'am/_/[(]rkyan [,]rkyen [)]med rab tu sbyor bar 'gyur/_/gal te nam mkha' dus la sogs/_/de dag yod 'gyur de ltar na/_/de dag kyang ni [#]byas bzhin du/_/skad cig 'jig 'di las mi 'da'/_/'di ltar gang zag [#]yod ces pas/_/de kun skad cig 'jig can de/_/dper na 'dus byas dngos po 
[\[16a.4\]]dag_/de ltar de ma thag grub bzhin/_/nam mkha' dus dang dbang phyug sogs/_/'di yang khyod kyi yod par 'dod/_/de dag skad cig ma nyid dang /_/bral na med par thal bar 'gyur/_/gang phyir brtan pa'i dngos po rnams/_/rim gyis sam ni cig car du'ang /_/don gyi bya ba byed 
[\[16a.5\]]min pa/_/de phyir de dag med par 'dod/_/rgyu ni nye ba med pa'i phyir/_/'bras bu rnams ni sdod par byed[#]/_/nus pa'i rgyu ni yod gyur na/_/de dag sdod par [#]gang gis byas/_/'on te'ang rtag la [#]rim can gyi/_/lhan cig byed rnams yod gyur [#]na/_/'di ni gang dag la ni ltos/_/'bras bu'i 
[\[16a.6\]]tshogs rim rten par byed/_/'di legs 'on kyang de la ni/_/lhan cig byed de rnams [#]yod pa/_/rung ba'i ngo bo'i rgyu nyid phyir/_/don gcig byed pas yin nam ci/_/rung ba'i ngo bo'i rgyu nyid na/_/dngos de dag gi [#]byas 'gyur na/_/de yang bya bar mi nus phyir/_/de rang bzhin du rtag gnas 
[\[16a.7\]]pa/_/'di yi rang bzhin byas na yang /_/'di yi rtag pa nyams par 'gyur/_/de las khyad par tha dad ni[#]/_/'di ni byed por ji ltar 'gyur/_/de yod na ni 'bras bu dag_/skyed pa'i phyir dang de med na/_/mi skyed phyir te de ltar na/_/de nyid rgyu nyid rnam par gnas/_/'on te de dang 'brel 
[\[16b\]]
[\[16b.1\]]pa'i phyir/_/de la'ang rgyu nyid yod pa na/_/de dag las [#]'brel gang yin 'dod/_/tha dad phyir na de bdag min/_/de ni de las 'byung ba'ang min/_/cig car bar du thal phyir ro/_/de las kyang ni 'bras bu rnams/_/cig car nyid du skyed par 'gyur/_/de la'ang gzhan la ltos pa na[#]/_/
[\[16b.2\]]thug pa med par thal bar 'gyur/_/de las 'bras bu dus gcig na'ang /_/'brel pa med phyir brtag mi bya/_/gal te 'brel pa 'du ba yin/_/de la'ang 'dir ni dpyad bya ste/_/de ni de la 'gyur ba'am/_/de lta ma yin zhes bya ba/_/'du ba can du 'dod dam ci/_/gal te rtog pa dang 
[\[16b.3\]]po 'dzin/_/de las byung ba de nyid thob/_/de yang de ma thag gsal [#]zin/_/gal te na ni de la 'di/_/phan mi byed par 'dod gyur na/_/dngos gzhan kun kyang de bzhin du/_/'du ba can 'gyur khyad par med/_/tha dad tha dad med rnam rtog_/so so dag tu bkag pa'i phyir/_/ngo bo gnyis [#]can 
[\[16b.4\]]khyad par ni/_/dngos po de la bsal ba yin/_/gang zag la sogs rtags [#]rnams su/_/phung po gnyis ni bstan pa'i phyir/_/ngo bo gnyis can khyad par med/_/gnyis med bdag nyid can yang med/_/'on te lhan cig byed pa yi/_/rkyen las [#]'jug pa mi ltos pa[#]/_/de lta na yang de bral 
[\[16b.5\]]'di/_/byed pa ma yin mtha' rgyu bzhin/_/de yi bdag gi rang bzhin 'di/_/de dag nyid ni nye ba na/_/byed po nyid yin de phyir de/_/yod na'ang kun tshe 'bras bu min/_/de lta yin na 'on kyang ni/_/tshogs pa na de [#]rang bzhin 'dod/_/gang de [(]mtshogs [,]ma tshogs [)]na 'dod na/_/der 'brel rang bzhin 
[\[16b.6\]]yod na ni/_/de dag kyang ni 'di gnas phyir/_/bral ba'i ngo bo'i gzhan yin na/_/gcig pa nyid ni nyams par 'gyur/_/de phyir de ltar ltos pas kyang /_/'bras bu rnams ni rim skye'am [#]min/_/cig car mi 'dod nyid dang ni/_/de 'bras rim gyis mthong phyir ro/_/gal te 'bras 
[\[16b.7\]]bu ma lus rnams/_/cig car byas nas nus pa yi/_/bdag nyid don de ldog 'gyur na/_/'di ni skad cig 'jig can grub/_/de dngos can ni rjes 'jug na/_/slar yang 'bras bu skye bar 'gyur/_/cung zad mi byed ngo bo ni/_/ji ltar yang ni nyid du 'dod/_/nus pa kun gyis stong 
[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]nyid phyir/_/rgyu skar lam gyi pad+ma bzhin/_/skad cig yin par thams cad med/_/gang phyir nus pa [#]dngos mtshan nyid/_/nus pa'i mtshan nyid dngos nyid 'di/_/ci [#]ste khas len mi byed na/_/gal te de ltar gzhan dag gis/_/dngos po'i mtshan nyid 
[\[17a.2\]]gzhan ston gyis[#]/_/gal te dngos po'i mtshan nyid ni/_/yod nyid dang 'brel 'dod de min/_/'du sogs ma grub phyir dang ni/_/ji ltar gzhan ni [#]gzhan mtshan nyid/_/don byed don du gnyer byed pa/_/g.yeng ba med pa'i blo can gyi/_/don la dpyod sogs bya ba kun[#]/_/rigs kyi 
[\[17a.3\]]smyon pa nyid kyis min/_/myos par gyur pas kyang ma yin/_/de la gang dag 'ga' zhig la[#]/_/la lar dus 'gar ji ltar yang /_/nye bar mkho [#]gyur de nyid la/_/yod nyid rnam [#]gzhag rig [#]pa yin/_/de yi dngos can don nyid la/_/skad cig ma nyid rab sgrub byed/_/thams cad sdud 
[\[17a.4\]]phyir khyad par ni/_/de nyid la ni brjod par bya/_/mo gsham gyi ni bu lta bu'i[#]/_/nus pa ma lus stong pa gang [#]/_/thams cad mkhyen pa'i thugs kyi yang /_/nam yang rgyu nyid ma yin pa/_/de la mkhas pas yod nyid kyi[#]/_/rnam gzhag 'di ni yod mi bya/_/rgyu med phyir ro 'bras bu dang /_/ngo bo 'bras 
[\[17a.5\]]sogs ma mthong phyir/_/de la skad cig ma nyid du/_/bsgrubs [#]pas don ni cung zad med/_/de la brgal zhing brtag [#]pa yang /_/byed pa'ang 'bras bu med pa yin[#]/_/'on te nam mkha'i pad+ma sogs/_/rang gis shes pa'i rgyur gyur phyir/_/don gyi bya ba byed nus kyang /_/de dag dngos por 
[\[17a.6\]]yod ma yin/_/nam mkha'i chu skyes pad sogs la/_/der rtogs bag chags tsam las ni/_/'byung ba dmigs pa med nyid ni/_/shes pa 'di ni rab tu [#]byed/_/med pa rgyu nyid yin na ni/_/rgyun mi 'chad par 'byung bar 'gyur/_/rtag tu khyad par med nyid phyir/_/dngos med ltos pa ci 
[\[17a.7\]]yang med/_/skad cig pa yi dngos rnams kyang /_/ji ltar don byed pa nyid yod/_/thog ma'i lhan cig byed rnams ni/_/phan tshun khyad par byed min te/_/skyes na bdag kun grub phyir dang /_/ma skyes rang bzhin med phyir ro/_/khyad par med las khyad par ni/_/'byung na [#]'bras bu cis 
[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]mi 'byung /_/gzhan la [#]khyad par can de min/_/brgal zhing brtag [#]pa mtshungs phyir ro/_/khyed la lhan cig byed pa'i tshogs/_/thug pa med par yang 'gyur ro/_/de ltar gang phyir de dag ni/_/rim dang cig car don bya med/_/mi 'gyur de phyir de dag ni/_/skad cig ma nyid rten don med/_/
[\[17b.2\]]de ltar lhan cig byed byas pas[#]/_/gang tshe la lar khyad med na/_/de tshe de phyir dus kun du/_/rgyun yang [#]khyad par med pa nyid/_/de tshe de phyir rgyun la yang /_/gal te 'jig pa rgyu med ni/_/'di ni thog ma nyid du 'gyur/_/gal te thog ma 'di med na/_/mtha' yang de ni ji ltar 'gyur/_/gal 
[\[17b.3\]]te dngos po 'jig pa la/_/rang gi rgyu las rgyu gzhan ni/_/mi 'dod na ni de dag la/_/'ga' zhig tu ni ji ltar lags/_/smras pa thog ma'i gnas skabs rnams/_/phan tshun phan pa byed nyid na/_/de dag don cig byed nyid kyis/_/lhan cig byed rnams su 'gyur te/_/phan tshun phan byed 
[\[17b.4\]]min na yang /_/'di dag khyad par yod gang phyir/_/rang gi nyer len stobs las byung /_/skye [#]byed tshogs pa'i tha dad yin/_/de [#]las brtsams nas gang skyes pa'i/_/khyad par de dag des byas pa'o[#]/_/de la dgongs pa de dag ni/_/dngos de'i rang bzhin can yin phyir/_/dngos rnams skad cig 
[\[17b.5\]]so sor ni/_/nus pa nges pa [#]bsam mi khyab/_/'byung ba brtag par bya min te/_/me [#]la sreg par byed nyid bzhin/_/rgyun chad ngo bo gang yin pa'i/_/'jig pa rgyu dang ldan pa min/_/de [#]ni mthar yang 'byung yod min/_/de ltar skye ba bkag pa yin/_/gyo mo la sogs mi 'dra ba'i/_/skye ba rgyu dang 
[\[17b.6\]]bcas pa ste[#]/_/de yang thog mar skyes min nyid/_/de tshe rgyu ni med phyir ro/_/dngos po skad cig ma rnams ni/_/rnam gnyis kha cig 'grib pa'i rgyu/_/grang ba sogs kyis [#]me sogs bzhin/_/gzhan dag de lta min pa yi/_/thog mar smras pa rgyu 'bras bu/_/yin na'ang [#]de nyid ma mthong ba'i/_/
[\[17b.7\]]'jig rten pa yis 'gal ba ni/_/rnam pa du mar mngon par zhen/_/dngos po rnams la gnod bya dang /_/gnod byed dngos po [#]don dam par/_/yod min de nyid phyir na yang /_/'gal ba rtogs [#]pa zhes bshad do/_/'o na dbang po bya ba ni/_/yod na mi g.yo gnod med can/_/de nyid 'di yin 
[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]zhes bya ba'i/_/ngo shes pa ni nye bar skye/_/de phyir skad cig gis 'jig par[#]/_/bsgrub phyir bkod pa'i gtan tshigs ni/_/kun la mngon sum gyis gnod 'di/_/bzlog dka' bar ni thal bar 'gyur/_/ngo shes par ni [#]gyur na ni/_/mngon sum yin par mi 'thad de/_/
[\[18a.2\]]gang phyir de ni rnam rtog bcas/_/dngos po'i ngo bo bstan bya min/_/ngo shes pa ni 'khrul pa ste/_/mngon sum mtshan nyid de las gzhan/_/tha dad min pa'i zhen pa yis/_/tha dad dngos la'ang 'jug phyir ro/_/mngon sum de las gzhan pa yin/_/gal te 'di ni sngar rtogs pa'i/_/rnam 
[\[18a.3\]]pa'i spyod yul can 'gyur na/_/'di ni sngar nyid skye 'gyur te/_/de don can phyir sngon blo bzhin/_/de ltar ma yin des na 'di/_/don de 'dzin byed 'dod min te/_/de shes dus su mi skye [#]phyir/_/yul gzhan gyi ni [#]blo bzhin no/_/tha dad min pa'i zhen pa yis/_/tha dad dngos la 'ang 'jug phyir 
[\[18a.4\]]ro/_/sgong gi 'khrul pa'i shes pa bzhin/_/'di ni 'khrul par [#]gnas pa yin/_/bya ba bsgrub pa'i don la yang /_/'jug pa'i phyir dran sogs bzhin te/_/byed pa'i don ni rnam nyams phyir/_/'di ni tshad mar rigs [#]ma yin/_/da ltar dus su yod pa nyid/_/snga ma'i blo yis shes pa nyid/_/
[\[18a.5\]]de phyir dran pa la med gang /_/khyad par 'di la yod min te/_/gang phyir sngar ni shes tsam la/_/de yin zhes [#]byas dran pa skye/_/de nyid 'di zhes bya ba yi[#]/_/ngo shes par ni khyad par can/_/the tshom phyin ci log gang dang /_/de dag spangs nas ngo shes pa/_/tshad ma nyid ni thob par byed/_/
[\[18a.6\]]shes par gyur la med phyir ro/_/cig shos kyis ni rtogs don yod[#]/_/mngon sum gyis [#]ni bcad pa la/_/cig shos skye ba srid pa min/_/tshad ma gtso bo brtan pa yis[#]/_/nges par bzung ba'i [#]don nyid la/_/cig shos stobs chung dag gis phyis[#]/_/bzlog par su zhig zhen par byed/_/'o 
[\[18a.7\]]na da lta yod de nyid/_/gang tshe khyod kyis tha dad 'dod/_/de tshe sngar yod dngos po las[#]/_/tha dad khyod nyid kyis [#]bstan to/_/yod pa gzhan nyid ma yin na'ang /_/ji ltar sngar [#]blos shes pa min[#]/_/de shes min na dngos po nyid/_/ma dmigs par [#]ni thal bar 'gyur/_/rjes dpag [#]la sogs 
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]kyi spyod yul/_/mngon sum gyis ni gnod pa na/_/rjes dpag la sogs tshad ma med[#]/_/gnod phyir rab rib can sogs bzhin/_/don la mi slu [#]ba nyid du/_/mtshungs na 'di cis [#]gtso bo yin/_/de yod min na rjes dpag sogs/_/tshad mar 'gyur pa [#]ma yin nyid/_/'o na gtan 
[\[18b.2\]]tshigs thams cad kyi/_/thams cad nye bsus khyab pa la/_/rjes su dpag pa 'dis gnod [#]cing /_/dam bca'i don la gnod pa yang /_/brjod 'dod nyi zla sogs pa yi/_/yul can shes pa gang yin gzhan/_/de dus dang 'brel nyi [#]sogs kyi/_/yul can du ni 'jug byed de/_/sa las 
[\[18b.3\]]byung yul can min pa/_/de shes nyid du brjod pa'i phyir/_/dper na dang po'i shes pa ni/_/rang dus nyi [#]sogs yul can bzhin/_/gzugs nyid sogs pa rten kun dang /_/de dag gi [#]ni rten gang [#]dang /_/de rnams gang la yang dag rten[#]/_/'ga' zhig de yi yul gang yin/_/'ga' zhig de ltar 
[\[18b.4\]]skye 'gyur ba/_/de dag skyes ma thag tu ni/_/'jig la rten pa nyid min te/_/gzhal bya nyid dang brjod bya nyid/_/gtan tshigs phyir ni mkha' pad bzhin/_/rnam par rtsod [#]pa'i yul gyur pa'i/_/shes pa rim gyis 'byung gang dag_/de rnams thams cad yul gcig can/_/yin no zhes ni brjod 
[\[18b.5\]]bya ste/_/gnod med rten gcig can nyid na[#]/_/brjod pa mthun [#]par sbyor ba'i phyir/_/dper na da lta'i skad cig ma[#]/_/gcig la du ma'i blo rnams bzhin/_/re zhig gtan tshigs dang po la/_/dper na bsgrub [#]byas stong pa ste/_/yul nyid rgyud [#]nyid yin pa'i phyir/_/shes pa'i rang gi dus can min/_/
[\[18b.6\]]gang tshe nyi ma sogs pa'i sgra[#]/_/'dod pa tsam gyis 'byung dag rnams/_/mar me sogs la sbyar bya ba/_/de shel dag gis 'khrul pa nyid/_/rigs sogs rang bzhin med pa'i phyir[#]/_/skad cig gis 'dod can 'dod min/_/de med par ni bsgrub [#]pa'i phyir/_/sgrub byed bstan [#]pa don med yin/_/
[\[18b.7\]]sgra mtshungs pa yis brjod bya nyid/_/mar me la sogs shes pa la'ang /_/'jug pa de phyir de yis [#]ni/_/gtan tshigs 'di ni 'khrul bar 'gyur/_/de dag la ni gzhi mthun nyid/_/gnod 'gyur du ni rtog ce [#]na/_/brjod par 'dod pa'ang gnod pa 'di/_/gsal bar ci yi phyir ma mthong /_/rtsod 
[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]pa'i gnas su gyur pa yi/_/blo rnams don zhig yul can min/_/rim gyis 'byung ba nyid kyi phyir/_/glog dang mar me sogs blo bzhin/_/don gcig las byung [#]shes rnams la/_/rim gyis [#]'byung ba 'gal ba yin/_/gzhan gyis tha dad bya min pa/_/
[\[19a.2\]]de la ltos pa 'gal phyir ro/_/gtan tshigs de rnams thams cad la/_/ldog la the tshom za nyid de/_/mi mthun phyogs la zhugs [#]pa la/_/gnod byed bstan pa med phyir ro/_/de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i rab tu byed [#]pa las brtan [#]pa'i dngos po brtag pa ste bcu gsum pa'o//_//
[\[19a.3\]]dngos po thams cad skad cig ma'i/_/mi rtag nyid dang 'brel de'i phyir/_/las dang de yi 'brel pa dang /_/rgyu dang [#]'bras sogs ji ltar 'grub[#]/_/dge rnams dag la skad cig gang /_/byed po nyid du brtag byed pa/_/'di ni 'bras bu byung dus su/_/
[\[19a.4\]]rjes su 'jug pa nyid ma yin/_/'bras bu 'byung tshe skad cig gang /_/longs spyod por ni brjod bya ba/_/de ni las byas nyid min te/_/de ni sngar mi srid phyir ro/_/las dang de yi 'bras bu dag_/byed po gcig gis ma bzung phyir/_/byas pa chud 
[\[19a.5\]]zos ma phrad gang /_/thal bar 'gyur ba shin tu 'gal/_/bdag med sgra yi phyogs la yang /_/bdag med phyir ni 'bras med pa'am/_/bdag las gzhan gyir bya bar [#]ni/_/sngar nyid du ni rtogs byed pa/_/de phyir rtog ldan 'brel 'dod pa/_/dge ba dang ni mi dge ba'i/_/bya ba rtsom 
[\[19a.6\]]la mi 'jug nyid/_/'bras bu ring du gnas pa yin/_/dngos po 'bras bu byed nus pa/_/ma 'ongs ma yin 'das pa'ang min[#]/_/da ltar ba yang de snyed kyi/_/dus su gnas pa ma yin no/_/dngos po bdag nyid ma thob pa/_/gzhan gyi yan lag phyir nus min/_/'gags pa ma yin 
[\[19a.7\]]de yi ni/_/'bras bu med nus gnas pa'ang min/_/skad cig snga ma gzhan zhig pa'ang [#]/_/rjes 'gro med par rten [#]byed na/_/phyis byung [#]skye bar 'thad min te/_/rgyu mtshan med pa nyid phyir ro/_/'jig dang skye ba dus mtshungs na'ang /_/rgyu 'bras dag ni yod pa min/_/phan tshun ltos pa med 
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]phyir te/_/de yi bya ba ma bzung phyir/_/bum pa'i gzugs ni zhig pa na/_/dri la sogs pa'ang skyes pa ni/_/ji ltar [(]da [,]de [)]'bras mi 'dod ltar/_/gzugs gzhan rnams kyang de bzhin te/_/de phyir 'bras bu 'byung ba las[#]/_/sngon du gang gi bya ba mthong /_/de nyid de yi rgyu yin gyi/_/de ma 
[\[19b.2\]]thag pa tsam ni min/_/gal te 'di ni mdor [#]bsdus na/_/rgyu zhig pa las 'bras 'byung na/_/zhig pa brjod du med pa'i phyir/_/'di ni rgyu med nyid du 'gyur/_/ma zhig pa las de 'byung na/_/skad cig du mar gnas pa'i phyir/_/de tshe dngos rnams skad cig nyid/_/ji ltar nyams par 
[\[19b.3\]]'gyur ba min/_/skad cig gnas pa'i ngang tshul can/_/bum sogs mig gis [#]mthong min te/_/gang phyir zhig pa rtogs pa [#]yin/_/yun ring 'das pa'i dngos po bzhin/_/dngos rnams rang bzhin mi dmigs phyir/_/rgyu dang 'bras bu'i dngos por yang /_/mngon sum dang ni mi 
[\[19b.4\]]dmigs pas/_/mgu bar 'gyur ba ma yin no/_/'di mthong ba las 'di mthong la/_/'di yang mthong na ma mthong bar/_/rim ldan 'gyur ba'i mtshams sbyor ba/_/byed por rnam par gnas pa gang /_/skad cig gis 'jig dngos rnams la/_/ngo shes pa yang 'brel par dka'/_/
[\[19b.5\]]skyes bu gzhan gyis mthong ba'i don/_/gzhan gyis [#]ngo shes 'gyur ba min/_/sgra [#]la sogs pa tha dad la/_/shes pa po zhig yod pa'i phyir/_/'dra ba las ni ngo shes 'gyur/_/gnyis tha dad na rgyu med can/_/gal te mtshams sbyor byed pa'i rgyu/_/don zhig yod pa ma yin na/_/gzugs 
[\[19b.6\]]mthong ba na ro sogs la/_/mngon 'dod sogs de ji ltar 'gyur/_/srid pa'i btson rar chags sogs kyis/_/'ching bas bcings pa'i skad cig gzhan/_/ma bcings gzhan ni grol 'gyur ba/_/zhes bya 'di yang rtogs ma yin/_/bcings pa grol ba nam yang ni/_/mi srid 
[\[19b.7\]]gcig tu 'jug phyir ro/_/de phyir thar pa don gnyer rnams/_/ngal ba don ni med par 'gyur/_/'ching ba sogs kyis bcings gyur pa/_/grol ba thob par byed pa mthong /_/ma bcings grol ba thob byed pa/_/zhes bya 'di 'dra mthong bas gnod/_/bcings dang grol ba 'di dag ni/_/gzhi 
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]gcig de bzhin gnas pa'i phyir/_/'jig rten pa yi de dag bzhin/_/de bzhin thams cad mdzes par gnas/_/rnam pa [(]da [,]de [)]dag nyid kyis ni/_/dngos rnams skad cig gis 'jig la/_/gzhi gcig pa ni med pa'i phyir/_/dran pa la sogs mi srid do/_/'di la mngon 
[\[20a.2\]]par brjod par bya[#]/_/bdag med rnams la rgyu 'bras nyid/_/gnas pa yin na 'di dag kun/_/gnod pa med par grub pa nyid/_/ji ltar myu gu la sogs la/_/rjes 'gro can gyi bdag med la/_/sa bon sogs kyi nus nges pa/_/de bzhin nang gi bdag nyid gnas[#]/_/dngos su 'am ni brgyud 
[\[20a.3\]]pa yis/_/'ga' la [#]kha cig nus bcas yin/_/de phyir las 'bras sogs rnams kyi/_/'brel pa nyid ni 'thad ma yin/_/byed po nyid sogs rnam gzhag ste/_/rgyu gcig nyid du brjod 'dod pas/_/rtog pas sgro btags nyid 'dod kyi/_/yang dag gnas pa'i yang dag min/_/rjes 
[\[20a.4\]]'gro med na rgyu 'bras nyid/_/khyad par can de mi 'thad de/_/rgyud gzhan las ni byung [#]rnams dang /_/gang zhig rgyu 'bras mi 'thad bzhin/_/'o na sa bon myu gu sogs/_/rgyu 'bras nges pa nyid mthong ste/_/de la rjes 'gro'i bdag nyid can/_/cha shas phra mo yod ma yin/_/sa bon myu gu 
[\[20a.5\]]sogs pa la/_/zhing sogs khyad par med yin na/_/tha dad mi rigs gcig bdag phyir/_/tha dad rjes 'gro med pa grub/_/de phyir las 'bras sogs rnams gyi[#]/_/yod dang med pa sgrub [#]don du/_/rgyu 'bras grub dang ma grub phyir/_/gzhan gyis rab tu 'bad par bya/_/de la brjod bya skad 
[\[20a.6\]]cig ma/_/gnyis pa la ni 'bras skye zhing /_/dang por rgyu ni skyes gyur pa/_/de ni de tshe zhig pa'ang min/_/de ni skad cig pa nyid phyir/_/'bras bu'i skad cig dus gnas phyir/_/gnas na'ang don med gang gi phyir/_/de la 'bras bu de 'grub [#]yin/_/slar skyes slar yang de yis ni/_/bskyed [#]par nus 
[\[20a.7\]]min skye ba [#]ni/_/ma byung ba las byung dngos phyir/_/gzhan du na ni gnas pa min/_/de phyir ma zhig pa yi rgyu/_/skad cig dang por byung ba las/_/de yis [#]skad cig ma nyid [#]phyir/_/cig car thal ba yod ma yin/_/skad cig gsum la sogs pa la/_/dper na rnam par smin pa'i rgyu/_/zhig las 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]'bras bu brjod pa bzhin[#]/_/gal te dang por de 'byung na/_/cig car du yang thal 'gyur te/_/lhan cig 'byung rgyu bzhin de yang /_/rigs pas 'thad min gang gi phyir/_/med pas sngar ni nus med phyir/_/nus pa'i tshe na 'bras 'byung phyir/_/rgyu 'bras dag ni cig car nyid/_/'gal bar 
[\[20b.2\]]gsal ba nyid yin te/_/skam pas bzung ba bzhin du ni/_/rgyu 'dis rang gi 'bras bu 'dis/_/bzung nas skyed par byed pa min/_/gang phyir cig car ba ru 'gyur/_/'dod ldan bud med la bzhin du/_/'bras bu rang bzhin la brtan par/_/'khyud nas skye ba ma yin na/_/gang gis [#]de dag ci dgar 
[\[20b.3\]]'gyur/_/cig car ba nyid 'gal ba yin/_/gang phyir rgyu de rang nyid kyis[#]/_/'bras bu sos bsdoms [#]bzhin bzung ba/_/skyes [#]pa yin na cig car bar/_/'gyur na de ni de ltar min[#]/_/'bras bu'ang rang bzhin de la ni/_/'dod ldan sdug bsngal 'khyud bzhin pas/_/skye ba yin na cig car bar[#]/_/'gyur na de yang de ltar min/_/rang gi rgyu las 'byung 
[\[20b.4\]]ba ni/_/skad cig dang por gang byung ba/_/skad cig gnyis par [#]nye ba yi/_/de ma thag tu gang byung min[#]/_/bya ba med pa'i dngos la yang /_/de ni de yi skyed byed yin/_/'dod pa tsam las byung ba yi/_/brda ni rjes su sgrub byed yin/_/skye ba las ni dus gzhan pa'i/_/bya bas 'dir ni ci zhig dgos/_/gang phyir yod las 'bras byung bas[#]/_/yod pa nyid ni de'i 
[\[20b.5\]]bya'o/_/de ma thag par gang nges pa/_/de nyid ltos pa yin zhes brjod/_/rtag tu 'bras bu 'byung ba dang /_/ldan pa'i rgyu yi bya ba'o/_/de yod pa na 'byung ba tsam/_/bya ba zhes kyang kun tu brtags[#]/_/de dang ldan pas rgyu nyid de/_/de phyir de ldan de nyid mchog_/gang phyir sa bon myu gu sogs/_/
[\[20b.6\]]yod na yod par mthong nyid kyi[#]/_/bya ba dngos po yod pa las/_/cung zad kyang ni mthong ma yin/_/nus pa ma mthong bzhin du yang /_/rgyu nyid du ni rtog [#]byed na/_/'di dang bye brag ci yod na/_/gzhan yang ci phyir rgyur mi 'dod/_/ji ltar gzhan ni med na yang /_/bya ba 'bras bu rgyur 'dod bzhin/_/
[\[20b.7\]]gzhan yang ci phyir rgyu ma yin/_/yang na dngos bcas mthong bar ni/_/'dod na'ang 'di ni kho bo cag_/mthong bar 'dod yin rgyu med na/_/de la dngos po yod pa yang /_/ci yi phyir na khas len byed/_/ji ltar skye ba can blo nyid/_/tshad ma nyid du nges smra ba/_/de bzhin dngos po thams cad la/_/
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]de rgyu nyid du cis mi [#]'dod/_/ji ltar blo ni skad cig la/_/de bzhin skye ba can gzhan yang /_/bsgrubs pa de phyir 'gro ba 'di/_/de bzhin kho na bya bral ba'o/_/rgyun gyis 'jug pa'i dri la sogs/_/phan tshun rgyur 'dod kho na ste/_/de rgyu nyid du thal 'gyur te/_/'di la gnod pa can 
[\[21a.2\]]min no/_/gang yang rung ba'i rgyu rnams kyi/_/gzhan gyi de ma thag 'byung ba'ang /_/de bzhin nges pa la 'dod pas/_/brtan par 'dod la skyon 'di yang /_/gang zhig gang la bya ba dang /_/bcas te de yi rgyur 'dod na'ang /_/gang la yang dag nges gang yin/_/de rgyu yin par 'dod pa ni[#]/_/mngon 
[\[21a.3\]]sum dang ni mi dmigs pas/_/yod na yod par grub 'di dag_/gal te rnam bcas rnam shes kyis[#]/_/dngos rnams rig par khyod 'dod dam/_/'on te rnam med pas dngos rnams/_/rnam par rig par khyed 'dod pa/_/skad cig gis 'jig phyogs la'ang de/_/mtshungs par nye bar 
[\[21a.4\]]mtshon par bya/_/snga ma'i rang gi rgyu rnams dang /_/gzugs sogs rig pa'i rang bzhin gyi/_/rnam par shes pa thams cad nyid/_/dus mnyam nyid du skye ba yin/_/gal te rnam shes rnam pa dang /_/bcas na sems ni sna tshogs 'gyur/_/rnam pas mtshan ma nyid min na'ang /_/nye ba 
[\[21a.5\]]na gnas rgyu med pa'o/_/zhe na de'i lan 'dir cung zad/_/khyed kyis kyang ni smra bar gyis[#]/_/'di la khyed cag lan gang yin/_/de ni kho bo cag gir [#]'gyur/_/gal te rgyu ni 'bras bu dag_/skyed par mi 'dod de lta na/_/byas 'jig 'gyur na 'di min [#]te/_/rgyun gyis rgyu nyid 'gyur ba yin/_/
[\[21a.6\]]gal te rgyu ni med par yang /_/la lar 'bras bu 'byung 'gyur na/_/ma byas pa thob par 'gyur na/_/de min nus pa des phyir ro/_/skad cig gis 'jig rnam rtog gis/_/byas pa 'jig tshogs phas rgol ba/_/des ni mi 'dod pa nyid du/_/gyur pa cung zad yod ma yin/_/mi 'jigs 
[\[21a.7\]]yod par [#]lta ba rnams/_/rgyun la gcig par mngon sems pas/_/skad cig gis phye'i rnam rtog gis/_/ji ltar yang ni 'jug mi 'gyur/_/skad cig re re rnam 'jig pa'i/_/de kho na nyid rnam rtog [#]shing /_/rgyu yi nges par [#]rig nas ni/_/dge ba'i las ni rtsom par 'gyur/_/'bras bu 
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]'bras bu can nyid dag_/nges pa'i khyad par can nyid kyi/_/sems gang yin pa'i dran pa sogs/_/kun la'ang gnod pa med pa yin/_/de la rgyu 'bras 'gyur ba [#]yi/_/ma rig [#]la sogs pas bcings 'dod/_/de dang bral zhing dri med pa'i/_/blo nyid thar [#]pa yin par 'dod/_/thams cad 
[\[21b.2\]]skad cig pa nyid kyi/_/bcings dang grol ba rab grags pa/_/gzhi gcig pa nyid yin par ni/_/'jig rten pa la'ang [#]grub pa min/_/bdag la bye brag thams cad du/_/med phyir byed po zab pa [#]po/_/bcings sogs 'di 'dra la gnod kyi/_/gzhan du mi rtag pa nyid do/_/de kho na nyid 
[\[21b.3\]]bsdus pa'i rab tu byed pa las las dang 'bras bu'i 'brel pa brtag pa ste bcu bzhi pa'o//_//rdzas la sogs pa'i don drug [#]ni/_/dam pa'i don du yod pa'i phyir/_/rig [#]sogs dngos med nyid du ni/_/sngar bstan rigs pa ma yin zhes/_/rkang mig pa dang 
[\[21b.4\]]gzegs zan pa/_/lung tsam pa ni smra byed pa'i/_/rdzas la sogs pa dgag pa 'di/_/mdor bsdus pa ni brjod par bya/_/sa la sogs pas phye ba yi/_/rdzas dgu tha dad par 'dod pa'i/_/sa la sogs pa grangs bzhi ni/_/rtag dang mi [#]rtag gis rnam gnyis/_/re zhig [#]sa sogs 
[\[21b.5\]]bdag nyid kyis/_/gang 'dod pa yi rdul phran rnams/_/de rnams rtag pa'o de la sogs/_/rtsol ba gang yin de 'jig 'dod/_/de la rdul phran rnams rtag pa/_/yin par rig [#]pa ma yin te/_/dngos po ma lus pa yi yul/_/skad cig gis 'jig bsgrub [#]phyir ro/_/rtag tu gong bu 
[\[21b.6\]]ma lus pa/_/cig car skye ba kho nar 'gyur/_/de rnams phrad pa la sogs pa/_/ltos min bye brag med phyir ro/_/rdul phran skyed pa por 'dod pa/_/yod pa chos su rtogs [#]min te/_/don yod la dmigs tshad ma yi[#]/_/yul 'gyur [#]ma yin zhe na ni/_/gang gis thags mkhan 
[\[21b.7\]]sogs mthong bas/_/rdul phran rgyu ni mi 'grub min/_/gang phyir snam bu sogs thams cad/_/rdul phran bdag nyid kho na'o/_/yod 'dzin tshad ma med tsam gyis/_/med pa grub pa [#]ma yin te/_/gang phyir tshad ma log pa ni/_/don med yin pa'i [#]nges pa med/_/de rnams 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]rtsom pa'i cha shas can/_/yon tan cha shas thams cad ldan/_/mthong ba min pa nyid kyis [#]na/_/des na tshad ma can grub min/_/shel gong nye bar bsgyur ba yi/_/bsgyur ba snang ba ma yin nam/_/chu skyar sogs pa'ang de gzugs su/_/'dzin pa nyid du'ang mthong min 
[\[22a.2\]]nam/_/ral ga'i nang gi skyes bu la/_/de yi gzugs sogs mi shes kyang /_/skyes bur shes pa mthong ba dang /_/gos dmar blo yang yod ma yin/_/gzugs sogs ut+pal la sogs las/_/gcig tu tha dad pa yin te/_/des na de ni nag pa'i las/_/rta bzhin rnam par 
[\[22a.3\]]gcod phyir ro/_/sa la sogs dang gzugs dri sogs/_/shin tu tha dad 'gyur ba ste/_/gcig dang snga ma'i tshig dbye bas/_/zla ba skar ma'i tha dad bzhin/_/byed pa [#]nus sogs tha dad par/_/snal ma snam bu tha dad bzhin/_/'gal ba'i chos dag sbyor bas na/_/ka ba 
[\[22a.4\]]bum pa'i dngos bzhin no/_/gong bu'i don yod ma yin na/_/shing sogs mthong ba nyid mi 'gyur/_/de yi ngo bo las gzhan pa/_/gzhan gyi bdag nyid mthong min te/_/gzhan gyi rnam pa rig [#]pa'i blo/_/mi rigs shin tu thal 'gyur phyir/_/de bzhin dkar po la sogs par/_/
[\[22a.5\]]rig pa yis [#]kyang srid par 'gyur/_/de phyir dung la ser sogs kyi/_/blo bzhin blo 'di 'khrul pa yin/_/ral ga [#]nang chud skyes pa [#]yi/_/shes pa de rjes dpag pa ste/_/de yi gtan tshigs su gyur la/_/ral ga [#]nyid ni mthong phyir ro/_/ska [#]dang gur kum sogs rnam [#]pas/_/gos kyi 
[\[22a.6\]]gzugs ni gzhan thob cing /_/[#]gos rnams skad cig pa nyid pas/_/snga ma'i gzugs ni zhig pa yin/_/slar [#]yang chu sogs ltos bcas las/_/gzugs de nyid las dkar po yi/_/gzugs gzhan du yang 'byung bar 'gyur/_/lcags sogs sngon po sogs bzhin 
[\[22a.7\]]du/_/gnas skabs de nyid kyi gzugs la/_/rang dngos rjes su 'jug phyir ro/_/drug pa'i tshig la sogs dbye ba/_/'dod pa tsam gyis 'byung ba ste/_/de phyir dngos rnams de nyid kyi/_/ngo bor rnam gnas rig ma yin/_/'di ltar drug po las gzhan pa'i/_/yod nyid tha dad 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]nyid mi 'dod/_/de rnams sde tshan gcig nyid do[#]/_gzhan yang khas blangs pa min no/_/yod pa shes pa'i tshad ma yin[#]/_/yul du de nyid 'dod pas na/_/drug po'i yod nyid yin zhe na/_/khyed la drug las gzhan thal 'gyur/_/de drug chos can du brjod de/_/chos ni de dag 
[\[22b.2\]]las gzhan par/_/'dod pa nyid yin zhe na yang /_/de dang de yi 'brel 'dod gang /_/rdzas [#]sogs nges phyir phrad pa yang /_/mi rigs gzhan yang 'du ba yang /_/'brel pa gzhan ni khyod 'dod min/_/'brel pa mi 'thad de rnams kyi[#]/_/chos su ji ltar 'gyur ba yin/_/de las 
[\[22b.3\]]byung tsam gyis she [#]na/_/gzhan yang de dang 'dra gyur la/_/de [#]yang yod pa nyid yin na/_/tha dad pa can gyis dbye ba/_/thob 'gyur de la'ang gzhan yin pas/_/mtha' med par ni thal bar 'gyur/_/chos gzhan la ni 'jug pa yis/_/der ni chos can nyid thob na/_/[#]de nyid kyi 
[\[22b.4\]]phyir rdzas sogs nyid/_/chos can nyid du 'dod mi 'gyur/_/gal te snga rol snal ma las/_/snam bu tha dad sgrub byed na/_/sgrub par byed pa don med nyid/_/de bzhin bzlog par dka' ba yin/_/snal ma de rnams gnas skabs kyi/_/bye brag gzhan ni thob pa na/_/khyad par 
[\[22b.5\]]can don byed nus pa/_/snga ma las gzhan mtshan nyid kyi/_/bya ba gcig nyid la mkho la/_/so sor shes pa po bstan na/_/[{]mangs [,]mang [}]dang nus med 'bras med pa'i/_/skyon rnams bsal bar 'dod pa yi/_/tha snyad 'dogs pa po rnams kyis/_/gang dag ma lus pa rnams 
[\[22b.6\]]la/_/tha snyad nyung ba'i bsam pa yi[#]/_/tshig gcig gis ni brjod pa yin/_/byed po nus pa tha dad la/_/yongs su gyur sogs chos ldan phyir/_/de rnams dus gcig nyid du ni/_/snam bu nyid du 'grub pa min[#]/_/rdul phran rnams ni phan tshun du/_/bstangs pas skye ba nyid 
[\[22b.7\]]yin na/_/de rnams dbang po'i spyod yul nyid/_/min pas dbang po las 'das min/_/rdul phran sngo sogs rnam pa'i dngos/_/ngo bo nyid yin brtags pa min/_/sngon po sogs su snang ba yang /_/mig sogs shes pas rig pa yin/_/gal te 'ang de rnams shar nyid dang /_/
[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]gzhan nyid du 'byed mtshan nyid min/_/de lta'ang sbyar ba'i btung [#]ba bzhin/_/gnas la mngon sum gyis gnod min/_/dngos po 'di rnams thams cad nyid/_/kun las log pa'i rang bzhin du/_/mthong ba yin yang rang bzhin du/_/rnam pa thams cad nges pa min/_/brtags min dbang 
[\[23a.2\]]rten rtogs bya ba [#]yang /_/cha shas don med rang mtshan nyid/_/gang las tha dad nges pa la/_/rgyu yod na ni de 'byung 'gyur/_/ji ltar mar me 'bar ba'i rgyun/_/'dra bar 'byung la gcig par 'khrul[#]/_/de bzhin phra ba du ma rnams/_/rgyun mi 'chad rnams blos gcig rtogs/_/dbye ba mi rtogs 
[\[23a.3\]]de rnams la/_/mngon sum du 'dod min zhe na/_/mar me sogs de [#]ji ltar mthong /_/de bzhin cha shas can cis 'dod/_/'di rnams rdul du ma nges na/_/sngon po la sogs rnam [#]pa'o zhes/_/de lta bur yang rtogs bya bar/_/'di ni ji ltar 'gyur ba yin/_/gang phyir de la'ang 
[\[23a.4\]]rgyu med de/_/shes pa de ni yul med min/_/gong bu yul gcig ma yin te/_/gcig dang gong bu nyid 'gal phyir/_/gong bu gcig dngos nyid yin na/_/sbrang ma'i rkang pa g.yogs tsam gyis/_/de dag thams cad g.yogs par ni/_/'gyur te dbye ba med phyir ro/_/cha gcig tu ni dmar ba yang /_/
[\[23a.5\]]thams cad la 'gyur dmar po bzhin/_/'gal ba'i chos dag sbyor ba yang /_/sna tshogs nyid du thal bar 'gyur/_/gcig gi dngos nyid ma yin na/_/thams cad sgra 'dir ci zhig bya/_/de yang du ma'i yul yin na/_/yan lag can ni du ma yin/_/gos dang lus dang ri sogs 
[\[23a.6\]]gang /_/'jig rten la grub ma yin nam/_/de nyid yan lag can nyid du/_/khyed cag gis ni smras pa yin/_/gos dmar kun du thams cad dang /_/lhag med de bzhin ma lus pa/_/de la thams cad rab sbyor [#]ba/_/'dod pa tsam gyis 'byung ba yin/_/de 'dra mngon par 'dod pa ni/_/
[\[23a.7\]]kho bo cag kyang smra ba ste/_/gos kun dmar por 'gyur sogs par[#]/_/smra la bar du gcod pa med/_/brtags nas de brjod yin zhe na/_/tshig gzhan du ni thal 'gyur zhing /_/gtso bo phal par 'dod dag gi_/tha dad blo yang yod ma yin/_/sbyor ba kun la khyab byed pa/_/'jug pa nyid 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]ni ma yin phyir/_/thams cad dmar por thal ba min/_/thams cad g.yogs par 'dod pa'ang min/_/rdzas ni cha shas med min nam/_/ci [#]phyir ma khyab par rnam gnas/_/rang dngos de ltar gnas na ni/_/de nyid kyi phyir tha dad grub/_/yul mang la gnas pa des na/_/gnas 
[\[23b.2\]]gcig byed pa nyid ma yin/_/de phyir snam bu la sogs pa/_/du ma'i ngo bo nyid du grub/_/de nyid don ni mi shes pa'i/_/'jig rten gong bu gcig snyam sems/_/rnam rtog de la ltos nas ni/_/phra rab rdul zhes 'dir brjod pa'am/_/rgyu mtshan rnam rtog [#]min par yang /_/brda yi rjes 
[\[23b.3\]]'gro ldan pa yi/_/dbul la'ang phyug po zhes brjod yin/_/'di ni [#]de 'dra'i ming du gnas/_/snal ma dang ni lag pa sogs/_/cha shas can gcig rjes 'jug min/_/dper na du ma nyid grub pa'i/_/re lde snam bu rtsig sogs bzhin/_/gal te'ang mngon par 'dod pa'i rdzas/_/gcig 
[\[23b.4\]]phyir yan lag du ma la/_/rten min rdul bzhin 'jug par ni/_/mi rigs gnod can tshad ma'o/_/gcig 'jug gnas de'ang cha shas kyi/_/rten gzhan la ni 'jug pa ni[#]/_/dngos de nyid kyis sam gzhan gyis/_/rnam pa gzhan yod min pas so/_/de la 'di ni de nyid kyis/_/
[\[23b.5\]]gzhan la 'jug par brtag min te/_/de dbang byas pa nyid kyis ni/_/gzhan du der 'jug yod ma yin/_/dper na khu 'jo ba'i ma ma/_/gzhan gyi dbang bya'i rten 'gyur min[#]/_/de bzhin gcig gis dbang byas pa'i/_/rdzas ni gzhan gyi rten byed min/_/gang phyir de 'brel rang 
[\[23b.6\]]bzhin no/_/de min yul la'ang 'jug pas na/_/de ni yul gcig nyid thob bam/_/gcig bdag nyid 'gyur dbyer med phyir/_/gzhan nyid bdag nyid kyis 'jug na/_/rang bzhin tha dad pa yi dngos/_/tha dad bdag nyid nyid yin pas/_/gcig ni du mar gnas 'grub min/_/

[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]rang rgyud pa'ang thal 'gyur ba'i/_/bsgrub [#]pas gang zhig 'jug pa na/_/de ni rang nyid kyis mthong ba/_/yin na 'brel par 'gyur ba yin/_/ma lus pas sam phyogs gcig gis/_/'jug pa gang du'ang ma shes nyid/_/gang gi yod pa min phyir rdzas/_/med 
[\[24a.2\]]pa'ang [#]gzhan du 'gyur ba'o/_/de ni la lar mthong ba na/_/rdzas la sogs pa gsal [#]ba bzhin/_/de bzhin der ni ma mthong na/_/tha dad par rgol mi rigs so/_/de dag tsam gyis 'jug pa nyid/_/med par brjod 'gyur zhe na'ang de/_/mi rigs 'di na 'di zhes pa'i/_/blo yis [#]mngon 
[\[24a.3\]]sum gyis grub pa'i/_/de ni mngon sum 'dod min na/_/gnod pa can 'ga' brjod pa'i rigs/_/gzhan du gzugs sogs shes pa yang /_/mngon sum min pa nyid du 'gyur/_/'di la 'jug pa de med par/_/dbyer med tshul gyis sngar bstan zin/_/'di la zhes pa'i shes pa

[\[24a.5\]]'jug pa'i/_/nag pa sogs mthong lta bu yin/_/bum pa mar me la sogs bzhin/_/'jig pa la sogs gtan tshigs kyis/_/sgra rnams 'ga' zhig la brten yin/_/de ni nam mkhar 'gyur ba yin/_/nyi sogs bya ba'i rdzas rnams las/_/tha dad pa yi rgyu mtshan ni/_/gzhan 
[\[24a.6\]]na gzhan min sogs shes pa/_/ji ltar bum sogs shes bzhin no/_/skra dkar gnyer ma lus rtsub sogs/_/gyur la sogs pa'i shes phyir 'dir/_/gang las mtshan nyid mi 'dra rgyu/_/de ni dus zhes [#]bya bar 'dod/_/shar dang gzhan sogs blo rnams las/_/phyogs nyid 
[\[24a.7\]]rjes su dpog pa yin/_/shes pa rim gyis skye ba las/_/yid kyang rjes su dpag par 'dod/_/gzugs la sogs pa'i shes pa ni/_/rim gyis skye phyir gzugs sogs las/_/gzhan pa'i rgyu la ltos pa ste/_/[(]dpar [,]dper [)]na shing rta sogs bzhin no/_/zin sogs 'byung ba chen po rnams/_/
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]rgyu nyid yin par khas len na/_/sgra rnams de rnams la brten par/_/'grub phyir snga ma sgrub byed min/_/kha cig [#]rtag par khyab [(]byad [,]byed [)]pa/_/'du ba nyid du 'grub min te/_/'di rnams rjes 'gro ma tshang phyir/_/de bzhin rim [#]min sogs thob [#]'gyur/_/gzhan dang gzhan min sogs

[\[24b.3\]]la gzhan pa/_/drug po de ma thag 'byung ba'i/_/shes pa gang yin de yid du/_/kho bo cag kyang 'dod pa yin/_/yin nyid rtag na shes pa rnams/_/cig car ba nyid thob par 'gyur/_/des 'dir sngar smras gtan tshigs su/_/'dod pa rnam par 
[\[24b.4\]]nyams byas [#]'gyur/_/sangs rgyas pa dang phyi rol pas/_/brtan [#]pa'i yid 'grub mi 'grub pa/_/gzhan gyis mdor smra A bcas dang /_/gzhan du yang ni bzlas nas so/_/de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i rab tu byed pa las rdzas kyi tshig gi don brtag pa ste bco lnga pa'o//_//

[\[25a.1\]]'jug pa ste/_/de rnams rdzas nyid min pa'i phyir/_/rnam 'byed rdzas ni gang zhig yid/_/ma 'khrul pa yi de nyid kyis/_/shes de brjod par [#]rigs min nyid/_/de ni rdzas dang 'brel pa las/_/gcig nyid yin dang don gcig la/_/'du phyir yon tan la sogs

[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]'gro po sogs dngos de'i/_/dngos yin rtag tu 'gro sogs 'gyur/_/skad cig gcig kyang mi g.yo bar/_/gcig tu gnas pa ma yin no/_/gang du 'gro sogs med pa yang /_/'dis ni rtag tu thob 'gyur na/_/snga ma'i ngo bo mi 'dor [#]phyir/_/'gro sogs

[\[27b\]]
[\[27b.1\]]las/_/log pa'i blo yi rgyu nyid de/_/de rnams de ltar gnas pa nyid/_/bye brag nyid ni kha cig gi_/log pa nyid kyi [#]rgyu rnams te/_/rtag pa'i rdzas su gang gnas rnams/_/bye brag ces ni brjod pa yin/_/gang gi stobs kyis [#]rdul phran las/_/sogs las [#]rnal 'byor pa'i [#]blo

[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]gcig gi ngo bo la rtag pa'i/_/khyad par can spyi yod spyi yi/_/skyu ru ra'i shing sogs de yod/_/de ltar shin tu tha dad kyang /_/kha cig nus pa nges pa yis/_/mtshungs par bsre ba la sogs pa'i/_/rgyu nyid thob kyi gzhan du 
[\[28a.2\]]min/_/bya ba tsam la mkho ba yi/_/brjod par 'dod pa'i sgra de yang /_/brda ru byas pa de la'am/_/yang na ci 'dod gzhan yang ngo /_/dgal dang bzho sogs ngo bo yi/_/bya ba'i dbye ba la mkho ba/_/ba lang sogs sgra'i brda ru ni/_/tha snyad 'dogs pa po rnams byed/_/de brda yin 
[\[28a.3\]]par [#]byed pa la/_/yod pa la sogs pa 'di ni/_/skye bar yang ni shes bya yi/_/dbang po'i bya ba'i rjes thogs min/_/rtog med rnam pa'i shes pa nyid/_/dang po nyid du skye 'gyur zhing /_/de nas brda rnams yid byed la/_/de las de dran de rnams las/_/gzhan du son pa'i sems 
[\[28a.4\]]la ni/_/dang [#]po'i gtan tshigs kyang 'bras med/_/phyogs de phyi rol la min te/_/bsgrub byas stong par ston pa yin/_/dngos su de yi rgyu min te/_/phyi yi lcags kyu be'u sogs [#]bzhin/_/rang gi mtshan nyid 'di la ni/_/brjod [#]dang rnam rtog 'jug yod min/_/gang gis rang 
[\[28a.5\]]gi mtshan nyid ni/_/ngag kun spyod yul las 'das dngos/_/nang tsam la ni brten nas 'di/_/kun rdzob nyid du dmigs byed de/_/lcags kyu la sogs phyi dngos por/_/nges pas 'jug par 'gyur ba yin/_/las dang yon tan tha snyad dag_/med pa rgyur ni gang smras 
[\[28a.6\]]pa/_/med pa'i shes pas [#]bye brag ni/_/de yang mi rigs nus pa yis[#]/_/rgyu nyid las ni dngos po nyid/_/gzhan yang yod sogs bye brag dag_/med las med pa'i shes pa thob/_/ril las tha dad blo yin pas/_/shes pa de dag rnams dang ni/_/mtshan nyid mi 'dra ba mi

[\[28b.1\]]dang /_/'di la dbye ba ci zhig yod/_/sngon po sogs la gcig nyid ni/_/rjes 'jug par yang snang ba yin/_/snang bzhin yin yang 'di yi ni/_/dbye ba shes 'gyur min zhe na/_/de yi stobs kyis de dag ni/_/sgra blo ji bzhin gsal 'jug 'gyur/_/spyi yi shes pa pha rol po/_/nges

[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]na/_/'tshed pa po sogs blo sgra yi/_/rgyu de ma yin med pas so/_/gal te las nyid kyi rigs dang /_/'brel pa'i las dang sbyor ba'i blo/_/de ni zhig pa'i las la yang /_/nges pa'i rgyu ni mtshon phyir ro/_/'das pa'i las ni de yi tshe/_/mtshon du zin

[\[37a.3\]]ba lang sgra yi [#]don du brjod/_/de ci yod pa'am yang na med/_/yod na'ang ba'am ba yin min[#]/_/ba lang zhe na ming rtsod min[#]/_/don du bsgrub pa'i mtshan nyid yin/_/go bya'i brjod bya'i a go nyid/_/yin na sgra'i [#]don shin tu mtshan[#]/_/med la 'di yang mi rigs te/_/don

[\[37a.6\]]pas/_/ba lang 'gro dang 'dug ces pa'i/_/gzhi mthun pa nyid du mi 'gyur/_/gang gis ba min dgag 'di la/_/ba lang zhes bya'i sgra rjod [#]byed/_/'on te 'di ni tha dad par/_/min las gzhan sel bar khyed 'dod/_/de phyir 'dis ni ba lang nyid/_/thob phyir lhag pa ci zhig smras/_/
[\[37a.7\]]dngos po re re'i sel ba 'di/_/gcig pa'am yang na du mar 'gyur/_/gcig pa ba lang mang yod na/_/'di ni ba lang nyid du 'gyur/_/du ma nyid na'ang gong bu bzhin/_/mtha' yas par ni thal bar 'gyur/_/des na bye brag bzhin du 'di/_/brjod bya nyid du rigs pa min/_/gzhan sel 
[\[37b\]]
[\[37b.1\]]ba'am brjod bya'am ci/_/brjod bya min par 'dod pa yin/_/brjod bya'ang bsgrub [#]pa'i ngo bos sam/_/yang na gzhan ni 'gegs pa yin/_/sgrub pa'i bdag nyid brjod bya na/_/gcig tu lta ba btang ba yin/_/thams cad du yang gzhan sel 'di/_/sgra yi don ces smra ba na/_/gzhan 
[\[37b.2\]]sel min pa 'jig pa yis/_/gal te gzhan sel brjod bya na/_/de la de bzhin du 'dod pas/_/thug pa med par khyed la 'gyur/_/'on te gzhan sel ba 'di nyid/_/brjod bya min par khyed 'dod na/_/des na khyed kyi gzhan sel byed/_/sgra yi tshig la gnod byed yin/_/gzhan yang gcig 
[\[37b.3\]]nyid rtag pa nyid/_/so so la ni 'du ba nyid/_/dngos med par brjod sel rnams la/_/smra ba snal med snam bu bzhin/_/de phyir sgra gang kho na la/_/dgag sbyor de la gzhan las ni/_/ldog pa'i cha ni 'ba' zhig 'gyur/_/rang nyid gzhan du go byed yin/_/gzhan sel yongs su ma shes phyir/_/

[\[37b.5\]]gtan tshigs sngar smras gang yin rnams/_/'jigs med la sogs don mtshungs pa/_/rang bzhin gyis [#]gzhan las tha dad/_/der rten shes pa'i snang ba gang /_/don gyi gzugs brnyan rtog pa can/_/don gyi bdag nyid min pa la/_/don zhes bya bar nges pa yin/_/snang ba gzhan 
[\[37b.6\]]las tha dad phyir/_/gzhan las log pa'i dngos po ni/_/thob pa'i rgyu las ma 'dres pa/_/dngos po'i sgo nas 'ongs pa ste/_/rigs mi mthun pa las ldog pa/_/de 'bras gang yin rang mtshan nyid/_/de la de yi bdag nyid du/_/nges phyir 'di ni 'khrul pa yin[#]/_/de la 'di ni gzhan sel

[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]bar sgra las rtogs [#]pa ste/_/sgra las yang dag byung ba'i blo/_/phyi yi don du zhen pa'o/_/de yi ngo bo gzugs brnyan gyi/_/sgo [#]ni sgra las skyes pa [#]ste/_/brjod bya rjod byed dngos 'di ni/_/rgyu 'bras bdag nyid can grub pa'o/_/dngos kyi rnam pa gcig nyid la/_/de ltar rab tu

[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]gzhan las 'ongs yin/_/gal te'ang tha dad min pa 'di/_/blo yi rnam pa'i ngo bo yin/_/de ltar phyi yi dngos nyid du/_/'khrul pas de ni nges ba yin/_/de ni don min mi ltos nyid/_/de ni brgyud pas 'ongs pa yin/_/des na bdag nyid dngos po nyid/_/yod nyid min par bstan

[\[38b.3\]]ltar yod pa min pa nyid/_/dam pa'i don du tha dad min/_/tha dad min pa'ang de bzhin zhes/_/gang gis rnam grangs nyid du 'gyur/_/tha dad min pa'ang gcig dngos nyid/_/dngos med la de ga la yod/_/don gcig nyid du rjod byed pas/_/rnam grangs pa ni thob par 'gyur/_/med 
[\[38b.4\]]pa'i ngo bo'ang gcig nyid du/_/rtog pas sprul [#]ba ji bzhin du/_/tha dad pa'ang de bzhin no/_/de las rnam grangs ga la yin/_/rjod byed la ni dngos su ba'i/_/rnam grangs gnyis min pa'ang med/_/brjod bya'ang de rnams la med par/_/rnam pa du mar bstan pa yin/_/'on kyang du ma'ang

[\[39a.1\]]pa dang bcas gzugs/_/bstan du yod pa yin na yang /_/dper na sgra ni brtsal ma thag_/'byung ba'i shes dang mnyan bya yin/_/de rgyu min pa'i dbye ba yis/_/kha cig la sgra 'jug pa yin/_/dper na brtsal las 'byung sgra dang /_/dper na sbrang ma'i sbrang rtsi yang /_/

[\[40b\]]
[\[40b.1\]]rang bzhin dbang phyug sogs bskyed byar/_/dngos po grub pa ma yin no/_/de phyir med pa'ang dngos po nyid/_/min phyir ngal ba ga la yin/_/de grub na yang yod pa yod/_/des kyang med pa grub pa yin/_/ba lang min las ldog pa yi[#]/_/ba lang mtshan nyid mi 'drar 'dod/_/de phyir dngos nyid ba lang

[\[41a.1\]]sngo [#]min ut+pal min sogs las/_/ldog pa yi ni gzugs brnyan no/_/gzhan sel ba dang ldan dngos po[#]/_/brjod byar kho bo cag 'dod de/_/gzhan las ldog pa'i dngos po las/_/gzhan pa'i ldog pa'i nga la med/_/de phyir rigs la [#]sogs bzhin pa'i/_/
[\[41a.2\]]gzhan dbang skyon dang 'brel pa med/_/blo gros bzang pos gsungs pa ni/_/sgra don log [#]pa min pas ni/_/bye brag byed dang bye brag tu/_/bya ba bzhin mthun pa nyid de/_/de phyir sel ba sgra yi don/_/rnam par gnas pa 'gal ba med/_/sngon po'i sgra sogs 'ba' zhig kyang /_/gzugs 
[\[41a.3\]]brnyan bye brag can yin te/_/khu byug ut+pal bung ba sogs/_/yid gnyis bcas bzhin 'jug pa ste/_/pi [#]ka stang zil sogs sel bas/_/bye brag can gyi yul la yang /_/de ni ut+pa la yi sgras[#]/_/rnam par gzhag par nges pa dang /_/gnas gzhi mthun pa la sogs pa/_/de 
[\[41a.4\]]ltar 'dir ni bsgrubs pas na/_/rnam gzhag de kun pha rol po'i/_/phyogs la bsgrub par dka' ba yin/_/'di ltar sgra gcig gis rang gis[#]/_/mtshan nyid thams cad du bstan la/_/de skad bstan pa de la yang /_/ci phyir bye brag gzhan blo med/_/gang gi don du phyi ma'i sgra/_/sbyor byed dngos

[\[41a.7\]]la'i sgra don med 'gyur/_/yon tan de'i rigs 'brel las gzhan/_/ut+pal rigs ldan yin zhe na/_/ut+pa la dang sngon po'i sgra/_/gnas [#]gzhi tha dad par 'gyur ro/_/'on te ut+pa la nyid dang /_/'brel pa'i ngo bo nyid dang ni/_/yon tan de'i rigs dang 'brel rdzas/_/sngon po'i sgra yis mi 
[\[41b\]]
[\[41b.1\]]ston na'o/_/ut+pal dang 'brel ngo bo nyid/_/de bdag min par 'gyur na yang /_/gang zhig ut+pal nyid 'brel ba/_/de gnyis dang 'brel kho na ste/_/sngon po'i sgra yis de brjod pa'i/_/sgra yi shes pa'i yul du ni/_/bdag nyid kun de cha shas min/_/ut+pal sgra de don med yin/_/
[\[41b.2\]]cha shas las ni grol ba la/_/zad dang cha shas rnam rtog med/_/ngag don yongs su ma shes phyir/_/'dir ni 'di dag brjod 'dod yin/_/dang po'i sgra nyid kho na yis/_/de ni thams cad du gsal [#]byas/_/bdag nyid ci yang ma dor bar/_/ji 'dra de 'drar de bstan te/_/rnam grangs de dag

[\[46a.1\]]pa 'di mdzad do/_/de lta'i shes pa'i ngo bo nyid/_/'di la gang tshe rtogs [#]par 'dod[#]/_/de yi bdag nyid dgag pa yi[#]/_/mngon sum nyid du nye bar brjod/_/de [#]tshe shing la sogs pa'i sgras/_/brjod bya shing sogs sgra [#]de yis/_/rtog [#]pa dang bral la gnod min/_/
[\[46a.2\]]gzhan du gzugs sgra la sogs pa/_/rtog [#]pa dang bcas nyid du 'gyur/_/gal te mngon sum zhes bya'i sgra/_/mngon sum rtog pa dang bral bar/_/shin tu gsal bar shes bya ste/_/sems ni gzhan du kun zhen kyang /_/dbang rten sngon por rtogs phyir ro/_/rnam par rtog pa 'di nyid 
[\[46a.3\]]ni/_/don de rtogs par byed pa yin/_/'das pa la sogs ming btang [#]nas/_/de yi ming gis sbyor byed 'thob/_/de tshe ming dang don 'dres pas/_/rnam rtog la yod gzhan du min/_/snang ba so sor ma dmigs pas/_/cig car gnyis snang bar mi 'dod/_/rtog [#]bcas yid kyi rnam shes 
[\[46a.4\]]dang /_/lhan cig 'byung bar shes byas te/_/de phyir dbang po'i shes pa 'di/_/rtog pa med par gsal [#]ba yin/_/rnam rtog rim gyis skye ba [#]nyid/_/yin yang mgal me'i 'khor lo bzhin/_/myur bar 'jug la cig car bar/_/mngon par sems pa yin zhe na/_/ma yin de tshe 
[\[46a.5\]]mngon gyur pa'i/_/dngos po min dang rjes 'brel par/_/ldan pa'i rnam rtog snang ba zhes/_/'di ni cung zad bstan pa med/_/'khrul pa'i mngon pa'i nga rgyal de'ang /_/don gyi shes pa de nyid dang /_/rgyun mi 'chad par lhan cig pa[#]/_/de yi phyir na gsal ba yin/_/gar mkhan la lta'i 
[\[46a.6\]]gnas skabs sogs/_/ma lus par ni cig car mthong /_/mang po rnams kyis bar bcad kyang /_/myur du 'jug pas de 'khrul pa'i/_/lcug ma'i sgra la sogs rnams kyis/_/blo ni shin tu myur 'byung ba'i/_/'di la'ang cig car 'byung ba yi/_/[#]nga rgyal ci phyir 'byung mi 'gyur/_/
[\[46a.7\]]yid kyi rtog pa 'ba' zhig la'ang /_/rim du nges par [#]bas byed min/_/blo rnams kun kyang myur 'byung zhing /_/rim du gnas pa min par mtshungs/_/de phyir yul rnams thams cad la/_/rim gyis 'dzin par mi 'gyur gyi/_/sgra la sogs pa'i blo bzhin du/_/cig car 'dzin par 
[\[46b\]]
[\[46b.1\]]snang bar 'gyur/_/mgal me la yang 'khrul pa yis/_/cig car 'khor lor snang bar 'byung /_/gsal bar so sor snang ba'i phyir/_/mthong bas [#]mtshams sbyor ma yin no/_/'di ltar mtshams rnams sbyor ba ni/_/dran par byed pas sbyor yin gyi[#]/_/mthong bas min te 'das pa 
[\[46b.2\]]yi[#]/_/yul dang 'dzin pa min phyir ro/_/'di yi yul du gang gyur pa/_/de ni zhig par [#]gsal ma yin/_/de phyir 'khor lor snang ba 'di/_/gsal ba min par 'gyur ba'i phyir/_/gzhan yang dngos po gang zhig gis/_/gang gi ngo bor gzhag pa'i rgyu/_/med phyir de ni de nyid du/_/
[\[46b.3\]]blo ldan rnams kyis gzhag mi bya/_/dung dkar po la ba lang gi/_/rang bzhin lkog shal med sogs bzhin/_/bye brag can dang [#]bye brag gi_/don mdzad [#]pa yang med pa yin/_/'di la rtog pa dang bcas pa'i/_/dngos por gzhag pa'i rgyu mtshan med/_/ba khra bo sogs mi mthun phyogs/_/
[\[46b.4\]]gzhan du ha cang thal chos [#]'gyur/_/rigs la sogs pa bkag pa yis/_/gtan tshigs ma grub pa nyid yin/_/dbye bas yongs su ma bcad phyir/_/de bzhin bye brag rnams kyang med/_/sgra yi rang gi mtshan nyid gang /_/ming dang [#]rjod byed min pa nyid/_/brjod bya rang gi mtshan nyid dang /_/
[\[46b.5\]]rjod [#]byed nyid kyang sun bton te/_/de phyir brjod bya rjod byed du/_/'dod pa de ni sgro btags yin/_/sgro btags dang bral don nyid du/_/mngon sum nyid ni shes par bya/_/rang gi mtshan nyid yod gyur la/_/mod la de ni med gyur na/_/de yang ldog par 'gyur ba ni/_/dngos po bar bcad 
[\[46b.6\]]sogs phyir ro/_/sngon po la sogs bdag nyid la/_/brda sbyor mi nus gzhan rten min/_/des na de dag rang rig ni/_/brjod dang rjes 'brel can ma yin/_/de ni gzung bya'i bye brag las/_/ming sogs sbyor ba ma yin yang /_/'on kyang gtan tshigs ma grub pa/_/gang gis rjes su 
[\[46b.7\]]'jug pa min/_/gal te don gzhan rnam bcad pa'i/_/tshul gyis 'dzin pa ma yin na/_/don tsam 'dzin par 'gyur ba 'am/_/'dzin par mi 'gyur bum pa bzhin/_/gal te bum gzhan rnam bcad pa'i/_/tshul gyis 'dzin pa ma yin na/_/bum pa tsam zhig 'dzin 'gyur ram/_/'dzin 
[\[47a\]]
[\[47a.1\]]par mi 'gyur bum nyid bzhin/_/gzung bya gzhan ni rnam bcad pa'i/_/tshul gyis 'dzin pa yin snyam na[#]/_/shing ngo zhes bya sogs blo bzhin/_/rtog [#]dang bcas pa'i rnam shes 'gyur/_/spyi dang 'brel pa ma yin na/_/bye brag gang na yang yod min te/_/'dzin 
[\[47a.2\]]tshe de la ma reg pas/_/dngos po med phyir gzung bya min/_/de phyir nam mkha'i me tog bzhin/_/dngos med 'di na'ang gzung ba [#]min/_/bye brag dag dang ma 'brel pa'i/_/bye brag can ni mi srid pas/_/bye brag can gyi yul gyi blo/_/rtogs pa min [#]zhes gya tshom smra/_/mthun dngos mi 
[\[47a.3\]]mthun dngos dag las[#]/_/ldog pa'i don ni 'dzin par 'dod/_/bye brag can gyi yul gyi blo/_/bye brag dag dang 'brel min na/_/tha dad de yang bye brag can/_/zhes brjod bye brag 'brel pas min/_/tha dad ces bya'ang de sgra dang /_/'brel pa med par shes bya ste/_/rang bzhin las gzhan 
[\[47a.4\]]dngos dag ni/_/ma lus pa las ldog pa can/_/bzung [#]bar gyur pa de nyid la/_/de lta'i rnam rtog skye bar 'gyur/_/bye brag tu ni ma bzung la/_/spyi [#]zhes pha rol po [#]'dod pa/_/rtog pa dang bcas shes pa la/_/'dzin na der yang de dang mtshungs/_/bye brag dag las bye brag can/_/
[\[47a.5\]]de la spyi ru shes bya na/_/de ni 'dzin pa yin pa'i phyir/_/rtog dang bcas pa'i rnam shes 'gyur/_/bye brag med pa 'dzin snyam na/_/ldog pa las ni spyi zhes brjod/_/des ni spyi ni bye brag las/_/bye brag can nyid du mi 'thad/_/mthun dang mi mthun ngo bo yis/_/'di dag shes bya yin 
[\[47a.6\]]'dod la/_/spyi dang bye brag tu gnas par/_/bdag nyid kyis ni rab tu brtags[#]/_/gal te mthun dang mi mthun pa'i/_/dngos ni tha dad par 'dod na/_/bye brag dag las bye brag can/_/nyid ni spyi [#]dang 'dra bar 'gyur/_/'on te 'di ni mi 'byed [#]na/_/'di ltar ma 'dres par gnas na/_/
[\[47a.7\]]phan tshun spangs te gnas pa yis/_/'gro ba gzhan ni yod ma yin/_/bye brag bdag nyid las ldog par/_/dbye bas mtshan nyid gzhan yod min/_/de yi ngo bo ma bzung [#]na/_/de la 'dzin par ji ltar brjod/_/de yi dngos la reg na'ang 'di/_/tha dad gzhan las ldog pa yi/_/'dzin 
[\[47b\]]
[\[47b.1\]]pa zhes bya'i rnam shes 'di/_/'di la rtogs [#]dang bcas par 'gyur/_/spyi ni dngos po med pas na/_/gang gis bye brag can rtogs 'gyur/_/de phyir khyod la de yang ni/_/rtogs [#]bcas shes pa'i gzung byar 'gyur/_/ming gis bye brag can yin na/_/de ci de yi bdag nyid dam/_/

[\[47b.3\]]yul can ni/_/de kun rtag med yin par 'gyur/_/dmigs pa tsam gyis shes pa ni[#]/_/rtog pa med pa yod pa yin/_/byis pa lkugs sogs mtshungs shes pa/_/bye brag ma yin spyi yang min/_/dag pa'i dngos la dmigs dus su/_/skye bar nyams su myong bar 'gyur/_/
[\[47b.4\]]de dag gi ni gzhir gyur pa'i/_/gsal ba nyid ni rtog [#]par 'gyur/_/de las gzhan du'ang rigs la sogs/_/dngos po'i chos ni ji lta bar/_/nges par byed pa'i blo gang yin/_/de yang mngon sum nyid du 'dod/_/yang dang yang du rnam rtog pa'ang /_/ji srid 'dzin par gyur pa 
[\[47b.5\]]rnams/_/de dang 'brel pa'i rjes 'brangs nas/_/thams cad mngon sum nyid du 'dod/_/snang ba las ni khyim phugs su/_/zhugs pa'i mod la don mthong min/_/byas kyang dbang pos mi rtogs sam/_/ji ltar snang ba'i mod nyid la/_/dang por rang gi ngo bor shes/_/phyi nas 
[\[47b.6\]]rigs la sogs chos kyis/_/de la nges par rtogs par byed/_/gal te dmigs nas mig btsums te/_/'ga' zhig rnam par rtog 'byung ba/_/'brel pa'i rjes 'brangs [#]de min pas/_/de ni mngon sum nyid du 'dod/_/zer ba mi rigs gal te yang /_/shes pa rang gi mtshan

[\[48a.1\]]gcig tu tha dad pa/_/nyid du pha rol po de yang /_/'dod pa yin par ji skad smras/_/gang tshe bye brag can dag las[#]/_/bye brag gcig tu tha dad pa/_/rang dang rjes mthun rang bzhin blo/_/bye brag can las ji ltar skye[#]/_/rang dang spyi yi bdag nyid can/_/shes pa 
[\[48a.2\]]gcig gis rigs mi 'thad/_/rtogs [#]bcas gzhan du gyur nas ni/_/snga ma rigs ni gzhan du 'gyur/_/gal te rigs sogs rtogs [#]pa can/_/gcig tu tha dad ma yin na/_/bcad pa'i don la 'dzin pa'i phyir/_/phyi ma dran pa'i blo dang mtshungs/_/spyi yi dngos po du ma yis/_/
[\[48a.3\]]mi gsal ba ni rtogs she [#]na/_/de dang mtshungs min nges pa la/_/phyi ma phyi ma nges pa yin/_/sgro 'dogs rnam par gcod pa yi/_/ji ltar yul la rjes dpag la/_/de bzhin nges pa pa yis kyang /_/yul la sgro 'dogs rnams [#]gcod yin/_/sgro 'dogs rnam par bcad pa yi/_/
[\[48a.4\]]yul can nyid kyis tshad ma yin/_/rjes su dpag pa tshad ma yin/_/yid la dran pa mngon sum gyi/_/rjes sogs sgro 'dogs sel bas yin/_/rtags las byung ba'i shes pa ni/_/tshad mar 'dod de khyed [#]la med/_/de nyid dang ni gzhan bdag nyid/_/rigs la sogs pa yod min

[\[48a.6\]]ldan/_/tha snyad ma lus yan lag ni[#]/_/de yi sgo nas 'byung ba yin/_/rtog med rtog bcas skyed nus min/_/yul tha dad phyir rtog med phyir/_/gzugs sogs rnam par shes pa bzhin/_/mig sogs rnam shes bzhin zhe na/_/[#]de dag dang yang rtog bcas dag[# ]/'dir ni 
[\[48a.7\]]lhan cig 'gal ba min/_/don de nyid la nges 'byed phyir/_/yul ni tha dad pa yang min/_/dngos su na ni dmigs med la/_/rtog pa yang dag 'jug pa yin/_/de la yul nyid yod min na/_/gang gis tha dad yod pa yin[#]/_/gzugs sgra la sogs blo rnams kyi/_/rgyu ni 
[\[48b\]]
[\[48b.1\]]gzhan dang gzhan yod pas/_/de phyir gong du smras pa'i dpe/_/bsgrub byas stong pas 'di mi 'grub/_/me dang du ba sogs blo rnams/_/'bras bu rgyu yi ngo bo yis/_/'khrul pa kho na yin pas [#]yang /_/'di la gsal bar mtshon par bya/_/ma 'khrul pa zhes smos pas ni/_/
[\[48b.2\]]skra shad 'dzings sogs [#]shes pa rnams/_/'khrul phyir tshad mar mi 'dod de/_/de rnams bsal phyir 'di byas so/_/kha cig yid kyi [#]'dod pa yang /_/de min dbang po yod na ni/_/yod la dbang po med min yang /_/nyams par gyur pa 'gyur ba yis/_/bsgyur ba yang ni

[\[48b.5\]]nyid yin no/_/dngos po'i rang gi mtshan nyid la/_/ngo bo nyid kyis de 'jug min/_/de bzhin de yi ldog pa yang /_/zla ba gnyis la sogs dang mtshungs/_/yod pa ldog pa ma yin te/_/spyi yi blo dang mtshungs she na/_/dbang phyug la sogs 'khrul pa rnams/_/de yi gzhung la [#]zhen 
[\[48b.6\]]rnams kyis/_/rigs pa bye ba snyed thos kyang /_/yod 'dod mi ldog slar la yang /_/de dag rigs pa ma yin zhes/_/skye ba [#]de dag rang nyid smra/_/de yod pa las yod pa nyid/_/dngos su ma grub [#]ces pa de/_/mi 'grub ma 'khrul rnams blo la/_/brgyud pa grub pa ma yin te/_/de ni shin tu med 
[\[48b.7\]]ces bya/_/sems ni gzhan du zhen gyur kyang /_/zla ba gnyis sogs snang ba yin/_/rgyun mi 'chad pa med pa'i phyir/_/brgyud pa las skye ma yin no/_/dngos po ma grub blo rnams ni/_/so sor sdud pa yod pa'i phyir/_/ldog par gyur pa yod pa nyid/_/rang 'dod dbang phyug gzhung 
[\[49a\]]
[\[49a.1\]]yang ldog_/dung la ser sogs blo rnams kyang /_/don byed sogs la mi slu bas/_/gzhan dag tshad ma yin par 'dod/_/de yang don byed nus min te/_/gzhan du ha cang thal 'gyur phyir/_/skra shad 'dzings sogs par mi mthong /_/slu [#]ba'i shes pa'ang [#]mthong bar 
[\[49a.2\]]'gyur/_/de phyir de yang tshad ma nyid/_/yin par bzlog par dka' bar 'gyur/_/kha dog las ni tha dad pa'i/_/dbyibs ni 'thad pa ma yin no/_/kha dog du ni snang ba can/_/mi slu bar ni mthong ba [#]yin/_/rnam pa la ni ma ltos par/_/gal te tshad mar rtog byed na/_/don byed pa la 
[\[49a.3\]]mi slu bas/_/de yi ngo bo don la nges/_/de skad ces sogs smra ba kun/_/ji ltar 'gal bar 'gyur ma yin/_/bag chags smin pa'i rgyus bslang ba/_/de [#]phyir mi slu ma yin no/_/yid dang dbang po'i shes pa ni/_/rtog pa med par sgrub pa la/_/bde sogs rigs pa gang yin pa/_/rtog 
[\[49a.4\]]pa med par sgrub byed yin/_/gzhan rig [#]byed pa'ang ma yin pa/_/rang rig ji ltar yin par dpyod/_/don gcig la rten [#]rnam shes ni/_/de dag rig par 'gyur zhe na/_/spyi yi dngos po la dmigs pa/_/rgyun mi 'chad par 'jug pa na/_/de tshe rgyun mi 'chad par yang /_/bde sogs 
[\[49a.5\]]ci phyir rig pa yin/_/gal te yid kyis de rig pa/_/nyid du 'dod na'ang de min te/_/rim gyis skye bar byed smra bas/_/dus der de ni yod ma yin/_/cig car skye bar mi 'dod kyi/_/gnas pa de lta min zhe na/_/skad cig gis 'jig par bsgrubs pas/_/de la gnas pa zhes bya med/_/
[\[49a.6\]]myur bar 'jug pa cig car bar/_/'khrul pa zhe na de la'ang bsal/_/dran pa'i shes pa'i spyod yul ni/_/gsal bar snang bar mi 'gyur ro/_/bde [#]dang bde min sogs ngo bo/_/de la'ang gal te 'khrul par ni/_/rtog na bde sogs de tsam du/_/yod pa'i mtshan nyid can du 'gyur/_/
[\[49a.7\]]gang tshe rnal 'byor pas gzhan gyi/_/bde la sogs pa rig pa na/_/des kyang de bzhin mngon sum du/_/nyams su myong bar mtshungs par 'gyur/_/rang nyid kyis kyang sdug bsngal la'ang /_/yod nyid nyams myong khyed mi 'dod/_/'on kyang gzhan gyi rgyud de yi/_/yul la shes pa 
[\[49b\]]
[\[49b.1\]]de yang yod/_/gzhan gyis sdug bsngal rjes dpog pa'ang /_/de ni mtshungs te 'on kyang de/_/gzhan gyi sdug bsngal sogs yul zhes[#]/_/smra ba spyod yul ma yin no/_/bde dang sdug bsngal la sogs pa/_/bde ba nyid ces go 'gyur gyi/_/shes pa nyid ces go mi 'gyur/_/
[\[49b.2\]]de ni [(]shas [,]shes [)]min bum sogs bzhin/_/gal te 'di brda gzhan yin na/_/shes pa zhes bya'i blo de yang /_/sems la yod phyir de yi min/_/khyed la de yang shes mi 'gyur/_/gsal bar snang ba dngos yin phyir/_/de ni de ltar 'gyur ba min/_/bde [#]dang sdug bsngal bar mtshungs par/_/
[\[49b.3\]]'di dag thams cad cis ma gyur/_/yang dag don bsgom las byung ba/_/rtog [#]dang 'khrul pa rnam spangs pa[#]/_/bshad pa rnal 'byor pas shes pa/_/dri med bsgrub par nus pa yi/_/yul rtogs pa'am rang rig pa/_/'di ni tshad ma'i 'bras bur 'dod/_/tshad ma nyid ni 'dra ba'am/_/reg

[\[49b.5\]]rigs so/_/de phyir tha dad par rtog na/_/gzhu la sogs bzhin yod pa yin/_/bskyed bya skyed byed nyid kyi ni/_/rnam par gzhag pa 'di mi 'dod/_/shing sogs sta gris bcad pa na/_/phye ba'i mtshams su zhugs pa la/_/chad pa zhes ni brjod pa yin/_/des na de nyid gcig 
[\[49b.6\]]nyid ldog[# ]/rnam par bzhag par gyur pa nyid/_/bskyod bya skye [#]byed nyid du ni/_/gzhan du yang ni rnam brtags kyang /_/rnam bzhag [#]de ni 'gal ba min/_/byed pa zhugs pa'i de ma thag_/chad pa 'bras bu nyid min par/_/shes pa tshad ma ma yin te/_/'bras bu gzhan du 'dod pa 
[\[49b.7\]]yin/_/rang rig 'bras bu nyid ce na/_/de ni bkag phyir mi rigs te/_/yul gyi rnam pa tshad ma ni/_/don ni tha dad 'gyur zhe na/_/thams cad ma rig [#]par 'gyur bas/_/dgag par nus pa ma yin no/_/tha dad don yang 'dir yod min/_/rang rig pa yang don rig 'dod/_/
[\[50a\]]
[\[50a.1\]]rang las tha dad bya ba byed/_/byed pa yin phyir tshad ma ste/_/ste'u la sogs bzhin zhe na/_/'bras bu med pa'ang [#]'bras gzhan phyir/_/smras pa'i rigs pas ste'u sogs la/_/'bras bu gzhan yang yod ma yin/_/byed pa po nyid kho bo la/_/ma grub brtags 
[\[50a.2\]]pas skyed byed yin/_/'jog pa po nyid brtags [#]pa yin/_/'gal ba ci yang yod min pas/_/des na gtan tshigs [#]ma nges pa'o/_/'gal bar bstan pa ma yin phyir/_/rnam pa gzung ba dang mthun min/_/de phyir rigs gzhan nyid yin pas/_/gzugs dang ro sogs bzhin

[\[50a.4\]]'gas 'dra ba yin na ni/_/kun kyang thams cad rig [#]byed 'gyur/_/'on kyang phyi [#]yi don yod par/_/smra la 'dra ba yod par ni/_/nges pa kho nar khas len par/_/bya phyir de ni bstan pa yin/_/shes pa rnams [#]bcas phyogs la yang /_/sems ni gzung ba las gzhan yang /_/de 
[\[50a.5\]]yi gzugs brnyan ngo bo yis/_/phal pa yang ni rig tu rung /_/de yang rnam pa yod min pas/_/phyi rol rig pa'i snod ma yin/_/de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i rab tu byed pa las mngon sum gyi mtshan nyid brtag pa ste nyi shu rtsa gcig [#]pa'o//_//rang dang gzhan don dbye ba 
[\[50a.6\]]yis/_/rjes dpag rnam pa gnyis su 'dod/_/rang don tshul gsum pa'i rtags las/_/dpag bya'i don ni shes pa yis/_/tshul gsum pa yi rtags brjod pa/_/gzhan gyi [#]don du'ang bstan pa yin/_/tshul re'am [#]gnyis gnyis don ldan pa/_/de phyir rtags ltar snang 
[\[50a.7\]]bar 'dod/_/gzhan du mi 'thad pa nyid ni/_/gtan tshigs gsha' mar ma mthong min/_/yan lag gsum yang yod min pa/_/de phyir mtshan nyid gsum dman [#]pa'i/_/gzhan du mi 'thad pa nyid ni/_/gang 'di gtan tshigs yin par 'dod/_/'di ni mtshan nyid gcig pa'i don/_/mtshan 
[\[50b\]]
[\[50b.1\]]nyid bzhi pa'ang ma yin no/_/dper na bu gsum yod pa la'ang /_/de yi bu gcig gces pa'i phyir/_/bu gcig pa zhes 'jig rten smra/_/de bzhin 'dir yang blta bar bya/_/med na mi 'byung 'brel ba'i phyir/_/nam yang tshul gsum la yod min/_/gzhan du mi srid pa yi char/_/gcig 
[\[50b.2\]]ni gtan tshigs la yod dmigs/_/gzhan du mi 'thad pa nyid ni/_/gang yin de nyid gtan tshigs te/_/dpe gnyis yod dam med kyang rung /_/de yi rgyu nyid ma yin no/_/gang na gzhan du mi 'thad yod/_/der ni gsum gyis ci zhig bya/_/sngo bsangs yin te pha de'i bu/_/yin phyir 
[\[50b.3\]]cig shos snang ba bzhin/_/'dir ni tshul gsum pa yin yang /_/nges pa'i phyir ni 'jug pa min/_/de yi dpe gnyis ma gtogs pa/_/mtshan nyid gcig gi gtan tshigs so/_/dngos po'am dngos med yod bdag nyid/_/ji ltar yang ni dmigs phyir ro/_/zla ba nyid ces brjod 
[\[50b.4\]]pa'i phyir/_/ri bong gis mtshan zla min min/_/'di ni mtshan nyid gnyis pa'o/_/gtan tshigs gzhan yang brjod bya ste/_/mchu sbrang babs pa'i reg pa las/_/byung ba dmigs par 'gyur ba ni/_/mchu sbrang bcas pas tshor ba 'di/_/byas pa yin zhes nges par shes/_/gzugs 'dzin pa yi bya ba la/_/
[\[50b.5\]]mig ni nus dang shin tu ldan/_/de la bya ba byed phyir dang /_/de ni mthong ba'i phyir yang ngo /_/gzhan yang bdag dang bum pa sogs/_/ji ltar yang ni med bdag nyid/_/ji ltar yang ni ma dmigs phyir/_/bong bu dang 'brel rwa [#]bzhin no/_/ri bong rwa la sogs pa yang /_/ji ltar yang ni yod bdag nyid/_/ji ltar yang ni 
[\[50b.6\]]dmigs phyir te/_/dper na bdag dang bu sogs bzhin/_/khyed kyi pha yang khyim 'di na/_/yod do zhes ni shes bya ste/_/khyed kyi pha yi skad nyid ni/_/khyim 'di na ni grag phyir ro/_/sgra dang mar me sogs dngos la/_/gzhan du mi 'thad pa nyid do/_/phyogs kyi chos dngos med na yang /_/rtogs 
[\[50b.7\]]par byed pa nyid du'ang mthong /_/des na mtshan nyid gcig pa yi/_/gtan tshigs rtogs byed gtso bo yin/_/don med phyogs chos sogs gzhan gyi/_/brtag pas khyed la ci zhig bya/_/mtshan nyid 'di ldan gtan tshigs de/_/de ci spyi yis rtogs bya 'am/_/shes par 'dod 
[\[51a\]]
[\[51a.1\]]pa'i bye brag gi_/chos gzhan yin nam 'on te dpe/_/der ni spyi yis rtogs she na/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i chos can la/_/gtan tshigs yod par bstan pa na/_/brjod 'dod 'grub pa min pa ste[#]/_/dper na mig gis gzung bya ni/_/mi rtag pa la 'khrul pa nyid/_/
[\[51a.2\]]spyi yis go bar byed de yang /_/sgra yang de ni sgrub byed min/_/de la chos can yod gyur par/_/shes bya zhe na de lta na/_/tshul gsum po ni khyed cag gi_/gzhung lugs la yang 'byung bar 'gyur/_/gzhan du mi 'thad pa nyid ni/_/log dang rjes 'gro dag tu rtogs/_/de ni chos can 
[\[51a.3\]]la yod phyir/_/phyogs kyi chos la brten pa yi/_/gzung bya'i chos la de yi chos/_/khyab pa'i gtan tshigs 'di ltar na'o/_/'di 'dra'i mdor bsdus mtshan nyid ni/_/slob dpon gyis kyang rgyas par bstan/_/'on te gtan tshigs mtshan nyid 'di/_/chos can nyid la shes bya na/_/
[\[51a.4\]]gang gis tshad mar de grub kyang /_/de las bsgrub bya'ang nges par grub/_/bsgrub bya rtogs par ma gyur na/_/gtan tshigs la yang nges pa med/_/gzhan las bsgrub bya grub na ni/_/des na gtan tshigs las nges pas/_/phan tshun rten [#]pa'i skyon yang ste/_/gnyis las cig shos 
[\[51a.5\]]ma grub pas/_/gzhan pa'ang nges pa ma yin no/_/dpe yi yin na de grub kyang /_/bsgrub bya'i chos can blor mi 'gyur/_/de yis thams cad bsdus pa la/_/khyab par nges pa ma yin no/_/gang yang ji ltar yang dmigs phyir/_/zhes bya'i gtan tshigs 'dir smras pa/_/phyi la yul

[\[51a.7\]]ni yod pa yi/_/bdag nyid nyid du ston par byed/_/gang zhig dngos por gnas pa kun/_/rang bzhin med nyid la brten pa/_/de yang de kho na nyid du/_/zhes [#]sogs bye brag la brtan [#]pa'o/_/gzhan du yang ni ji ltar yang /_/dmigs pa nyid ni mi 'grub bo/_/tha snyad nyid du bsgrub bya la/_/

[\[51b.1\]]dpe [#]ni grub par gyur pa min/_/zla ba nyid ces brjod pa na/_/mthun pa'i phyogs la'ang rjes 'jug ste/_/la lar mi 'am gzhan yang ste/_/ga pur dngul skya sogs pa la'o/_/zla ba nyid sgrub gtan tshigs la/_/thun mong min pa nyid gsungs pa/_/grags pa las ni tha dad pa/_/dngos

[\[51b.3\]]par dka' bar thal bar 'gyur/_/'bras bu nyid kyi tha snyad ni/_/brjod pa yin pas bsgrub bya na/_/gal te gong du grub pa'i dpes/_/bsgrub pa yin na tshul gsum 'gyur/_/chos can mig gi ngo bo la/_/yod pa re zhig des mig [#]nyid/_/ma grub sogs par thal 'gyur bas/_/

[\[51b.5\]]par shin tu gsal/_/bum pa la sogs ji ltar yang /_/med pa'i bdag nyid du bsgrub [#]pa/_/rang bzhin gtan tshigs 'bras med pa'am/_/shin tu ma grub par yang mthong /_/'di la'ang chos mi mthun pa'i dpe/_/gsal bar yod pa min na ni/_/de rnams nyid kyi ngo bo ni/_/mi

[\[52a.1\]]pa dag_/der ni rnams [#]pa gsal bar mthong /_/shes pa'i byed pa'i sgra yi don/_/ji ltar yang ni phyi rol min/_/gzhan yod ma yin dngos min phyir/_/brjod par 'dod pa 'di [#]nyid ni/_/go byed yin par de bsgrub na/_/mtshan nyid gsum par shin tu gsal/_/

[\[54a.1\]]pa'i phyir/_/de na rang don nyid du 'thad/_/thos dang mthong 'di rigs pa la/_/gang gtso 'di yi khyad par ci/_/gzhan don rjes su dpag pa nyid/_/tshig de thos pa ma ltos min/_/nyan pa'i rgyud kyi rnam shes kyi/_/rgyu nyid yin phyir shes byed phyir/_/dbang po ji lta

[\[55a.1\]]ro/_/chos kyi bye brag ldan chos can/_/de ni rtags can du rab nges/_/ji srid de yul ma gyur pa/_/de srid rjes su dpag pa min/_/'di la chos can du rtog gang /_/de nyid dpag bya yin na ni/_/de nyid nges par ma gyur pas[#]/_/de yi chos ni nges mi 'gyur/_/
[\[55a.2\]]gal te snga rol phyogs chos nyid/_/des na de yi 'og rol tu/_/phyogs chos sogs kyi shes bya [#]gang /_/gang gis rjes dpag nyid du 'gyur/_/gzhal bya'i don la nges rtogs pa'o/_/rjes su 'gro ba sgra la med/_/yod pa yis ni thams cad kyi/_/rjes su 'jug pa rtogs par bya[#]/_/
[\[55a.3\]]gang na du ba yod de na/_/me med rjes 'gro med par gsal/_/de bzhin gang na sgra yod pa/_/de na yod par nges min te/_/de [#]ltar sa phyogs de na med/_/de yi dus na'ang med shes bya/_/rtag cing khyab phyir yod ce na/_/sgra rnams thams cad der mtshungs pa/_/des na kun du mthong ba'i 
[\[55a.4\]]phyir/_/ldog pa'ang [#]grub par mi 'gyur te/_/sgra rnams kun gyi [#]ma lus pa'i/_/don rnams brtags par thal bar 'gyur/_/de phyir sgra las byung ba la/_/de 'dra'i yul ni med pa'i phyir/_/tshul gsum po nyid dang bral bas/_/rjes dpag ma yin mngon sum bzhin/_/me la sbyin sreg 
[\[55a.5\]{]stsogs [,]sogs [}]tshig gis/_/mi g.yo'i shes pa skye ba las/_/de ni tshad ma nyid yin pa'ang /_/'di ni bzlog par nus pa min/_/de la byed pos ma byas pa'o/_/tshig dang don dag kyang med pa/_/de phyir mi srid par bstan pa/_/sgra las byung ba'i mtshan nyid gcig_/nus dang nus min ngo bo yis/_/
[\[55a.6\]]thams cad du yang rjes 'jug phyir/_/rtag tu rnam shes de 'byung ba'am/_/nam yang 'byung bar mi 'gyur ro[#]/_/ji ltar [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]pa nyid kyi rgyu/_/zhe sdang gti mug sogs nyes pa/_/de bzhin bden pa nyid kyi rgyu/_/shes rab snying rje sogs shes bya/_/de yi rten [#]nyid med pa la/_/de dag kyang

[\[55b.1\]]na/_/rang bzhin tha dad pa de can/_/brdar [#]shes pa nyid ma yin yang /_/de las don rtogs 'gyur ba yin/_/rang bzhin gyis gsal mar me ni/_/brda la ltos pa ma yin no/_/brda nyid gzhan du 'gyur ba na/_/don kyang gzhan 'gyur sgra las min/_/brda yod gyur kyang sgron mas ni/_/

[\[55b.3\]]pa rigs pa'o/_/lam las mi g.yo 'di mi rtogs/_/yid ches pa nyid mi 'dod pas/_/sgra las byung ba gnyis pa yang /_/mi bzod 'dod na de nyid do/_/zhes bya'i rnam par nges pa med/_/gang gi tshig las mi slu bar/_/lan grangs du mar mthong ba yi/_/yid ches gyur pa de yi 
[\[55b.4\]]tshig_/lkog rtogs phyir ni gzung [#]zhe na/_/snga rol dag tu mi slu ba'ang /_/dus nyid tshe na yid ches min/_/dus gcig nyid ni [#]mi slu ste/_/thams cad du ni nges pa min/_/tshig dang phyi ma'i dngos dag las/_/'brel pa yang ni ci yod na/_/gang gis de rtogs gyur pa las/_/'di ni

[\[59a.1\]]gang /_/sreg pa la sogs rgyu gang yin/_/mi [#]la sogs pa ma nor bar/_/the tshom med par mthong ba ni/_/de la bum pa gzhan cir 'gyur/_/tha dad yin na 'bras bu la/_/de ni dgos pa yin pa'i phyir/_/dngos po byed pa min nyid 'gyur/_/dgos pa
