[\[59b\]]
[\[59b.1\]]tshig gzhan dag kyang 'dren par 'gyur/_/ji ltar nye bar 'gro ba yi/_/ngo bor der ni rtogs pa yi/_/tshad ma gzhan nyid gang yin des/_/don rtogs 'gyur ba brjod 'dod na/_/mtshan mo'i bza' ba dang 'brel ba/_/brjod 'dod mir ni shes par bya/_/nyin mo'i bza' ba dang bral 
[\[59b.2\]]ba/_/tsho phyir de la gzhan bzhin no/_/za ba yin dang tsho ba nyid/_/rjes su 'gro dang ldog pa yi[#]/_/'brel pa nges pa de yi ni/_/dngos po las ni dngos po rtogs/_/'brel pa thams cad kyis stong pa'i/_/tshig las ji ltar rtogs 'gyur te/_/gcig las gzhan kun rtogs 
[\[59b.3\]]min gyi/_/gzhan du yang ni 'gyur ba yin/_/de phyir 'brel pa med pa dang /_/yod kyang nges par [#]ma bzung ba/_/tshig 'di rtogs bzhin pa na'ang /_/tshad ma can du 'gyur ma yin/_/'brel pa tshad ma nyid gnas pa/_/gal te rgyal po'i bka' yin nam/_/mngon sum 'brel pa med 
[\[59b.4\]]bzhin du/_/ji ltar tshad ma nyid du 'gyur/_/'brel pas rtogs byed nyid yin na/_/ci phyir gzhan yang rtogs byed min/_/'brel pas stong pa nyid du ni/_/bye brag ci yang med par 'dod/_/mngon sum la yang 'brel pa nyid/_/tshad ma nyid du rnam par gnas/_/mi slu ba 
[\[59b.5\]]ni tshad ma nyid/_/don de [#]las ni bdag nyid thob/_/de rgyu min pa'am rgyu min [#]pa/_/de yang nges par mi slu bar/_/rigs pa min te ma lus pa/_/mi slu bar 'gyur gzhan du min/_/gnyis pa'i tshig [#]ni snang ba yi/_/bsam pa'i rjes su dpog pa ste/_/rjes su 'gro ba'i tshig 'di 
[\[59b.6\]]las/_/ldog par go bar 'dod pa yin/_/nye bar 'jal ba [#]tshad ma nyid/_/yin pa rgyas par bsal bas na/_/des bskyed don gyis go ba yis[#]/_/tshad ma nyid kyang bsal ba yin/_/ba lang nye bar dmigs pa nyid[#]/_/nye bar 'jal ba'i tshad mar 'dod/_/de yod pa la dmigs pa 
[\[59b.7\]]yang /_/rang nyid [#]nyes pa skyed byed pa/_/'dod phyir don gang rtogs pa la/_/ltos pa'i nus pa gang zhig yin/_/gzhan min pa nyid ma yin yang /_/lag pa bskyod sogs las bzhin du/_/sgra rnams kyis ni don rtogs par/_/'gal bar 'gyur ba ma yin no/_/ji 
[\[60a\]]
[\[60a.1\]]ltar 'dra ba'i chos dag ni/_/bsres pa rgyur gyur pa yi phyir/_/de phyir sgra rnams go byed pa/_/nyid du 'gal ba ma yin no/_/mngon sum rdzas la brten par ni/_/mthong ba'i bya ba gang yin pa/_/de rnams yi ge ji bzhin du/_/sngar mthong ngo shes rtag 
[\[60a.2\]]par 'dod/_/gsal bar byed pa med phyir yang /_/'di ni yod kyang mi dmigs nyid/_/khyed kyi skyed byed gang yin yid[#]/_/de nyid kho bo gsal byed yin/_/de min nus pa thogs med pa/_/yin na shes pa de [#]rtag 'byung /_/de dang ldan pa ma yin na/_/de ni nam yang 'byung mi 
[\[60a.3\]]'gyur/_/de phyir de rnams gsal byed pa'i/_/rgyus bskyed pa nyid mi rigs so/_/sngar mthong ngo shes pa rtag nyid/_/gong du bsal ba nyid yin no/_/nag pa khyim na med pa las/_/phyi rol yod pa mi rigs te/_/gang phyir shi bar dogs pa las/_/gzhan du na ni 'thad pa yin/_/
[\[60a.4\]]gson la khyim na med pa ni/_/phyi rol yod par rab bsgrubs [#]pa'i/_/don gyis [#]go ba 'dir smra ba/_/de min der yang ma nges phyir/_/tshu rol mthong bas nag pa ni/_/khyim du ma mthong tshad ma min/_/de ni srog bcas dang 'brel pa/_/ji [#]ltar rjes su 'jug pa yin/_/ji [#(]sta [,]ste [)]skad la 
[\[60a.5\]]sogs pa ste/_/srog dang bcas par nges yin na/_/[#]khyim na med pas med par yang /_/nges 'di tshad ma nyid yin na/_/de lta'ang khyim na med pa nyid/_/bltar rung ma mthong bar nges pa'ang /_/de phyir der ni phyi rol na/_/yod par brtags [#]nyid las nges yin/_/khyim du gang 
[\[60a.6\]]zhig ma phrad pa/_/'di ni phyi rol na yod nges/_/khyim gyi dang ra khyim sgo nas/_/yod pa mthong ba'i mi bzhin no/_/'dir ni khyim gyi khong gtogs pa/_/mi mthun phyogs na'ang yod pa'i phyir/_/de phyir don gyis go ba 'di/_/rjes dpag las ni mi phyed do/_/don gyis [#]go ba rnam 
[\[60a.7\]]par dpyad pa'o//_//dngos po gang zhig ngo bo la/_/tshad ma lnga po ma bskyed [#]pa'i/_/dngos yod rtogs phyir de la ni/_/dngos po med pa'i tshad ma nyid/_/mngon sum la sogs mi bskyed pa'am[#]/_/dngos po med pa'i tshad mar 'dod/_/bdag nyid yongs su ma gyur 
[\[60b\]]
[\[60b.1\]]pa'am[#]/_/dngos gzhan rnam par rig pa'am/_/tshad ma med pa'i dngos po rnams/_/dngos po med par rtog [#]par byed/_/snga na med pa la sogs pa/_/'di ni rnam pa bzhi ru dbye/_/zho sogs 'o ma la med gang /_/de ni snga na med par brjod/_/zho la 'o ma med pa nyid/_/zhig nas 
[\[60b.2\]]med pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/rta sogs ba lang la med gang /_/gcig la cig med par brjod de/_/de las gzhan dngos med cing de'i/_/bdag nyid gzhan la med pas so/_/mgo yi cha ni [#]dma' mo la/_/'brel [#]dang sra sogs ngo bo yi/_/ri bong rwa dag med gyur pa/_/'di 
[\[60b.3\]]ni shin tu med par 'dod/_/gal te snga ma [#]med pa sogs/_/dbye bas dngos med yod min na/_/rgyu la sogs pa rnams [#]dbye ba'i/_/tha snyad 'di yang med par 'gyur/_/de dngos min na dbyer med pa/_/des na 'di ni dngos po nyid/_/'bras bu la sogs dngos med 
[\[60b.4\]]gang /_/rgyu sogs dngos med yin bdag 'dod/_/gang 'di rjes 'jug mi 'jug gi_/blo yi bzung [#]ba gang de'i phyir/_/ba lang sogs bzhin dngos po nyid/_/gzhal bya'i phyir yang shes par bya/_/dngos med sgra yi brjod bya nyid/_/yin phyir mngon sum sogs las gzhan/_/
[\[60b.5\]]gzhal bya dngos po med pa bzhin/_/tshad ma rnams kyang dngos med pa'o/_/[#]dngos po med pa'ang tshad ma [#]yin/_/rang dang rjes mthun dpag bya ste/_/gzhal bya yin phyir [#]dngos po bzhin/_/de phyir dngos bdag nyid las gzhan/_/de 'dir rtag [#]pa'i bdag nyid kyi/_/yongs su gyur pa 
[\[60b.6\]]bsal bas na/_/de las bzlog pa'i dngos yod pa/_/res 'ga' ba ni mi rigs so/_/gal te de yongs dor tsam gyis[#]/_bdag nyid du ni 'di sems na/_/rtag tu dngos po med nyid du/_/'di ni rgyun mi 'chad ces bya/_/yongs su ma gyur 'di yod na'ang /_/de lta na yang 'khrul pa nyid/_/
[\[60b.7\]]gnyid [#]dang brgyal sogs gnas skabs su/_/don yod na yang don 'byung phyir/_/gal te rkyen gzhan yod pa la/_/des dben pa yi gzhan mthong phyir/_/bum pa shes pa la sogs pa'i/_/ngo bos de ni yin 'dod na/_/gnyis pa'i phyogs su bstan pa yin/_/'di la bye brag ci yod 
[\[61a\]]
[\[61a.1\]]na/_/gang 'di khyod kyi [#]rnam grangs kyis/_/phyogs gnyis dag tu bshad pa yin/_/dngos gzhan sgrub pa'i shes pa la/_/'jug pa thams cad med pa nyid/_/dngos med gnyis pa la brten nas/_/ma mthong ba yang go bar 'gyur/_/rgyu gzhan nye bar gyur pa na/_
[\[61a.2\]]rung bar mtshungs pa'i ngo bo de/_/des dben gzhan ni ma [#]shes pa/_/de ni med ces bya zhe na/_/blta nus dngos po snang ba ni/_/med nyid mngon sum nyid kyis te/_/de ltar yang dag bstan pa ni/_/'khrul pa can gzhan rnams kyi min/_/gang blo mngon sum mi 'dod pa/_/dngos gzhan 
[\[61a.3\]]rnam par rig pa ni/_/skye bar ji ltar shes par byed/_/gal te don gyis go ba las/_/yin na de yang shes bdag nyid/_/yin pas de yang ga la go_/don gyis go ba gzhan dgos na/_/thug pa med par thal bar 'gyur/_/dngos med tshad ma dngos yin na/_/gzhal bya dngos med 
[\[61a.4\]]kyang de bzhin/_/dngos med mngon sum nang 'dus pa/_/de bzhin khyod la ji ltar min/_/'bras bu la sogs dngos med pa/_/gang yin rgyu sogs dngos med pa'o/_/der [#]yang gzhan gyis dben bdag nyid/_/mngon sum gyis ni rtogs par byed/_/rang dngos gzhan gyis dngos bdag ni/_/
[\[61a.5\]]rtag tu yod dang med bdag nyid/_/dngos po'i ngo bo cung zad ni[#]/_/res 'ga' 'ga' zhig gis rtogs 'gyur/_/mngon sum la sogs 'jug pas kyang /_/gang tshe dngos po'i cha 'dzin pa/_/bya ba de ni mi skyed pa/_/dngos po med pa'i cha 'dzin 'dod/_/dngos po rnams kyi rang dngos nyid/_/
[\[61a.6\]]gzhan las log pa ma yin nam/_/des ni de bdag nyid yod pa'i/_/bdag nyid 'dis ni mngon sum mo/_/yod pa don byed nus pa ste/_/gzhan ni med par bstan pa yin/_/de dag gcig tu nyams gyur par/_/rigs pa ma yin 'gal phyir ro/_/gal te rang sgrub par nus pa/_/
[\[61a.7\]]gzhan sgrub mi nus ma yin nam/_/de nyid kyi phyir gnyis dngos nyid/_/dngos po gcig la yod ma yin/_/gal te nus med gzhan nyid las/_/gzhan du'ang 'dod pa de lta na/_/dngos po gnyis ni 'thob 'gyur na[#]/_/gcig ni gnyis kyi [#]dngos nyid min/_/dngos po med pa'ang dngos po 
[\[61b\]]
[\[61b.1\]]nyid/_/yin par gong du khas blangs pa/_/de nyid slar yang ngo bo nyid/_/med ces ci phyir smra bar byed/_/ngo bo med phyir shes pa yi[#]/_/ngo bo nyams pas tshad ma nyid/_/mi rigs de ni gzhal bya nyid/_/yang dag rig pa'i rang bzhin no/_/de rgyu yin phyir tshad ma ste/_/mig sogs 
[\[61b.2\]]bzhin zhes bya zhe na/_/ngo bo med pa rgyu nyid do[#]/_/'gyur ba nam yang mi srid do/_/shes pa'i ngo bos [#]shes pa na[#]/_/de dngos med par ji ltar shes/_/de yi yul dngos tshad ma nyid/_/las yin thug med ma yin nam/_/dngos po dngos med las tshad ma/_/tshad ma dngos med las dngos 
[\[61b.3\]]po/_/gal te med pa go byed nyid/_/yin [#]na phan tshun gnas par 'gyur/_/de yi gcig mthong gang yin pa/_/de ni gzhan mthong yin par brjod/_/rang gi ngo bos bems po nyid/_/min phyir de yang rang dbang 'grub/_/blo ni blo gzhan las grub pa/_/blo gzhan gyis [#]ni rtags 
[\[61b.4\]]las te/_/yang na gzhan du mi 'thad pa'am/_/thams cad du yang mi 'byung ba'o/_/brgal zhing brtag [#]pa gsum po 'di/_/re re zhing yang yod srid na/_/thug pa med pa du ma yi/_/khyod kyi 'di la khyab par 'gyur/_/rung ba'i ngo bor mtshungs pa'i phyir/_/gcig nyid nye bar dmigs 
[\[61b.5\]]pa las/_/rung ba nyid gzhan med par ni/_/nges par yang dag rtogs par byed/_/mkha' [#]la zla ba med shes par/_/gcig po gang las rig [#]pa yin/_/sgra kun gyis [#]ni dben par yang [#]/_/gang du ji ltar nges par byed/_/snang ba dang ni mun pa yi[#]/_/phung po dag las sgrub pa po/_/de nyid 
[\[61b.6\]]shes mkha'o snyam sems te/_/gang phyir gnas med bltar yang med/_/sgra kun gyis ni dben par yang /_/'bras bu ma mthong ba las shes/_/de ltar shes pa gzhan dag gi_/nyams su myong las de yang 'grub/_/'di yi [#]dngos med tshad ma ni/_/dngos po'i [#]dngos po de gnas 
[\[61b.7\]]pa/_/dngos med sgra yis brjod bya nyid/_/nang du 'dus byas [#]gnod pa med/_/dngos po med pa'i tshad ma ni/_/rang dang rjes mthun pas dpog na/_/de 'dir 'grub [#]pa las bsgrub [#]nyid/_/de 'drar [#]rjes mthun yod phyir ro/_/dngos po med pa rnam par dpyad pa'o//_//
[\[62a\]]
[\[62a.1\]]de yod par der 'di 'byung la/_/med par yang ni mi 'byung ba/_/de phyir de las 'byung ba de/_/'di ni rigs pa zhes brjod de/_/'di yod pas ni 'di 'byung gi_/'di med par ni 'di mi 'byung /_/'di ni tshad ma gzhan nyid ces/_/thub pa tsa ra ka smra ste/_/gang phyir 
[\[62a.2\]]'di dpe ma dmigs phyir/_/'di ni rjes [#]dpag ma yin no/_/dmigs pa gang gis don gang zhig_/rtogs pa de [#]dngos med pa [#]las/_/med pa nyid du rtogs pa ste/_/mi dmigs pa zhes bya bar 'dod/_/dpe la ltos pa med pa'i phyir/_/'di yang tshad ma gzhan yin no/_/
[\[62a.3\]]dpe yang med pa nyid du ni/_/'di nyid kyis ni rab tu grub/_/rgyu 'bras nyid dang dngos med dang /_/rtogs pa dang ni 'brel med kyi/_/sgrub dang bsgrub bya gang yin pa/_/de dang 'di la 'di dbyer med/_/de yod 'byung ba ma gtogs par/_/rgyu dang 'bras bu nyid gzhan med/_/snang 
[\[62a.4\]]ba mi dmigs ma gtogs par/_/med pa gzhan nyid rtogs pa min/_/dngos po de yi tha snyad du/_/rung ba nyid du rab sgrub byed/_/brda yis dngos su rtogs pa yis/_/don yod nges par mthong ba'o/_/tshogs par rnam par gnas pa yi/_/rgyu ni tshogs pa can nyid de/_/
[\[62a.5\]]stong yod 'bras bu'i rtags las ni/_/brgya sogs yod pa'i shes pa skye/_/zhes grags pa dang de bzhin du/_/mthangs kyis tshor ba la sogs pa/_/'di 'dra'i 'khrul bcas du ma dag_/tshad mar mi sbyar thal 'gyur phyir/_/gal te 'di ni gnas min pa/_/nyid la nyam [#]thag 
[\[62a.6\]]las byed na'ang /_/mngon sum dang ni lkog gyur te/_/don ni rnam pa gnyis su gnas/_/gnyis ka gnyis ka min bdag nyid/_/don gzhan rigs pa min pa nyid/_/gcig la bya dang bya min par/_/shes na gcig nyid nyams pa yin/_/dngos su rnam shes skyed byed 
[\[62a.7\]]la[#]/_/mngon sum don du brjod par bya/_/ji skad bstan pa las bzlog pa/_/lkog tu gyur ces bya bar brjod/_/re zhig sgra 'byung nyer 'jal sogs/_/'di ni dang po'i don yul min/_/mngon sum khong gtogs thob pa'am/_/'bras med nyid de dran pa bzhin/_/lkog tu gyur 
[\[62b\]]
[\[62b.1\]]pa'i yul de yang /_/de rnams yul du ji ltar 'gyur/_/gal te dngos gyur lkog gyur 'di/_/min te mngon sum dngos po bzhin/_/gzhan la rnam par brten na yang /_/'di ni de rtogs ji ltar yin/_/gzhan dang 'brel pa'am ma 'brel pa'am/_/tha dad [#]snang ba'am de bzhin 
[\[62b.2\]]min/_/ma 'brel pa las de 'byung na/_/nges pa med par thal bar 'gyur/_/gal te tha dad snang 'gyur na/_/khyab pa 'brel par mi 'gyur ro/_/gang zhig lkog tu gyur pa'i don/_/'brel pa'i don la brten pa dang /_/tha dad de gzhan mi brten te/_/rjes dpag yin par bsal [#]
[\[62b.3\]]zhes bya/_/de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i rab tu byed pa las tshad ma gzhan brtag pa ste nyi shu rtsa gsum [#]pa'o//_//gal te nor bu gzi lta bur[#]/_/dngos po du ma'i bdag nyid can/_/rang bzhin de ltar yin [#]pa 'am/_/yod la sogs pa 'gal ci yod/_/dngos po dngos gzhan 
[\[62b.4\]]mtshungs pa yin/_/nam mkha' me tog khyad par med/_/gal te mtshungs min nyid bral na/_/de dag thams cad grub pa min/_/gang phyir thams cad mtshungs min phyir/_/mngon par 'dod pa'i dngos po la/_/dngos po gzhan du nges pa ni/_/dngos po nyid ni [#]med par 'gyur/_/
[\[62b.5\]]dngos po las ni ldog pa na/_/med pa nyid ni spangs nas ni/_/gzhan du 'gro yod ci zhig mtshon/_/rgyu skar lam gyi mtsho skyes phyir/_/de phyir dngos der 'dod pa ni[#]/_/nam mkha'i me tog dang mtshungs min/_/nam mkha'i me tog mtshungs min par/_/'dod na de phyir dngos [#]
[\[62b.6\]]de ni/_/de dang mtshungs pa'i dngos po nyid/_/spyi zhes bya bar 'dod par gyis/_/rnam pa gzhan du blo de ni/_/gtor zas so brtag la 'jug ming [#]/_/dngos po rnams su nges nas so/_/'jug pa de ni gang gis byas/_/gal te 'dra bas nges 'di ni/_/de nyid spyir [#]ni bdag can 
[\[62b.7\]]'dod/_/rang bzhin rjes 'gro [#]nus pa ni/_/de nyid kyis ni bshad pa yin/_/de phyir shin tu tha dad blo/_/'ba' zhig nyid du grub pa min/_/gang phyir dngos pos ngo bo kun/_/phan tshun du ni tha dad min/_/dngos po'i mthar ni byas pa las/_/gal te 'dra min dang bral ba'i/_/dngos 
[\[63a\]]
[\[63a.1\]]po de ni tha dad min/_/de dbye med phyir de bdag bzhin/_/de las tha dad rang ngo bo/_/mi 'dra bar ni mngon par brjod/_/'dra ba min yang tha dad min/_/de ni phan tshun 'gal ba yin/_/de phyir don rnams tha dad nyid/_/ji ltar yang ni 'dod pa na/_/mi 'dra 
[\[63a.2\]]bar ni 'dod par gyis/_/de nas khyad par bdag nyid kyang /_/de nyid kyis na dngos gcig dang /_/rnam pa du mar 'dod pa yin/_/de dag rjes 'jug ldog pa yin/_/blo yi gzung bya nyid du gnas/_/thog mar gang dag rjes 'jug phyir/_/spyi zhes bya bar grags pa yin/_/de 
[\[63a.3\]]las gzhan ni log pa'i phyir/_/khyad par yang ni brjod pa yin/_/spyi dang khyad par dag gi ni/_/phan tshun rang bzhin nyid la ni/_/'dre bar gyur pas de nyid du/_/rnam pa gnyis 'di rigs ma yin/_/phan tshun rang bzhin med pa nyid/_/sna tshogs nyid du rjes su 'jug_/de lta bu 
[\[63a.4\]]yi ngo bo yang /_/rnam pa gnyis ni rigs ma yin/_/gal te gcig dngos 'di [#]yod na'ang /_/chos kyi dbye [#]ba rab grub phyir/_/gal te tha dad gnas 'gal min/_/dper na byed pa'i nus pa bzhin/_/mthong ba la yang rigs min med/_/gang phyir spyi dang khyad par la/_/gcig 
[\[63a.5\]]pu nyid dang tha dad dang [#]/_/'jig rten tha snyad rjes 'jug mthong /_/gcig pu ngo bo nyid yod na/_/tha dad chos grub ma yin nas[#]/_/gang phyir ma brtags dbye ba ni/_/sna tshogs nyid du mngon par brjod/_/nus pa sna tshogs bdag nyid ni/_/brjod par 'dod pa tsam gyis sprul/_/dngos 
[\[63a.6\]]po gcig pu'i bdag nyid ni/_/de la tha dad rigs pa min/_/gang de nyid dngos ces brjod pa/_/de nyid gcig ces bshad pa yin/_/de dngos mi 'gyur slar yang [#]de/_/sna tshogs bdag nyid ces bya'o/_/de dngos dang ni de dngos min/_/phan tshun du ni 'gal ba'i phyir/_/dngos po 
[\[63a.7\]]gcig pu nyid du ni/_/ji ltar yang ni rigs ma yin/_/sgrub pa dang ni dgag pa ni/_/phan tshun du ni 'gal ba yin/_/gang gis ma 'khrul sems kyis [#]ni/_/gcig tu bya ba nus pa [#]yin/_/rigs mthun pa dang rigs mi mthun/_/du ma las ldog dngos po la/_/de dang de las ldog 
[\[63b\]]
[\[63b.1\]]pa na/_/chos kyi tha dad rab tu brtags/_/de phyir gcig la'ang du ma yin[#]/_/ngo bo nyid brtags rigs pa min/_/dngos po gcig la rnam pa gnyis/_/yang dag par ni yod ma yin/_/gang yang mi yi seng ge sogs/_/rnam pa gnyis su nyer bshad de[#]/_/de dag la yang rnam 
[\[63b.2\]]pa gnyis/_/yang dag par ni yod ma yin/_/de ni rdul phran du ma'i tshogs/_/ngo bo gzugs gcig ldan ma yin/_/sna tshogs gang yang [#]de gcig min/_/sna tshogs rigs kyi rin [#]chen bzhin/_/gcig nyid na ni rnam gnyis pas/_/sna tshogs rnam par snang mi 'gyur/_/sbrang ma'i 
[\[63b.3\]]rkang pa tsam gyis kyang /_/bsgribs te ma bsgribs pa la ni/_/mi yi seng ge'i cha rjes 'brang /_/ngo shes pa yi rgyu dag ni/_/de yang rdul phran rang bzhin te/_/khyad par can gyi rkyen 'byung bzhin/_/de nyid kyis ni rnam pa yis/_/sna tshogs rin chen la sogs 
[\[63b.4\]]bshad/_/du ma'i bdag nyid sna tshogs te/_/gcig pu nyid dang 'gal ba yin/_/don byed par ni nus pa nyid/_/dngos po nyid du bas par brjod/_/gal te rjes 'gro nyid de ni/_/thams cad kun gyi [#]bya byed 'gyur/_/dngos po gcig pu nyid dang ni/_/skyed par nus pa dang sbyor ba/_/
[\[63b.5\]]de yang gzhan yang [#]khyad med yod/_/ji lta bur ni skyed byed min/_/sngo sogs kho na dngos po nyid/_/gal te rjes 'gror 'dod na ni/_/dkar po ser po la sogs kyang /_/sngon po'i 'bras bu skyed byed 'gyur/_/gnyis pa'i bdag nyid dang bral ba/_/gcig pu nyid ni de las skyes/_/
[\[63b.6\]]de phyir khyed kyis sna tshogs kun/_/gcig la du ma'i rnam pa med/_/gzegs zan 'dod pa'i yod pa bzhin/_/gal te de nyid dngos nyid min/_/gcig ni du ma'i ngo bo nyid/_/de ltar 'gyur min tha dad phyir/_/'gal ba'i chos dang 'dre ba yang /_/dngos po'i tha dad mtshan nyid de/_/
[\[63b.7\]]sngon po ser po'i tha dad ni[#]/_/'dod pa ji ltar yang mi 'gyur/_/rjes 'gro rnam pa gzhan dngos phyir/_/de yang spyi dang khyad par la/_/de dag gsal por tha dad du/_/yod pa de ni ji ltar med/_/ji bzhin kha dog khra bo la/_/ji ltar 'dod pa'i kha dog 'dzin/_/sna tshogs nyid phyir dngos 
[\[64a\]]
[\[64a.1\]]de bzhin/_/tha dad tha mi dad par nges/_/gang tshe dngos po khra bo ni/_/cig car du ni rtog [#]par byed/_/de tshe gzhan dang gzhan min sogs/_/dbye ba thams cad rab thim nyid/_/dngos po du ma'i ngo bo la/_/brjod par 'dod pa ngo bor 'dod/_/rim dang cig car 'jug pa 
[\[64a.2\]]yis/_/tshig rnams rnam pa gzhan yod min/_/de ma thag tu 'gag pa'i phyir/_/gcig ni sna tshogs nyid ma yin/_/du ma'i ngo bo sna tshogs te/_/gcig nyid lhan cig gnas pa min/_/ji srid rnam pa de dag ni/_/dngos po de la [#]yang dag yod/_/de srid kho nar skyed pa ste/_/
[\[64a.3\]]de srid dngos por gcig nyid ni/_/dngos po gcig las gzhan [#]nyid du/_/de yi bdag nyid dang bral 'gyur/_/nam mkha'i me tog dang 'dra min/_/de yang don byed nus pa min/_/gcig pu'i nus pa'i rang bzhin te/_/dngos po gzhan la 'jug ma yin/_/'bras bu dmigs par snang ba yi/_/
[\[64a.4\]]tha dad la sogs bshad pa'i phyir/_/dngos de'i bdag nyid min pa yang /_/de dang ma 'dres pas ni rigs/_/dngos med nyid ni khyad med min/_/dngos po'i mtshan nyid yod pa'i phyir/_/de yang don byed nus pa ru/_/de ltar dngos po zhes su brtag_/nus pa med pa la ldog pa/_/'dra ba 
[\[64a.5\]]nyid du brtags pa yin/_/des ni shin tu tha dad pa/_/mtshungs pa nyid du yod par brtags/_/dang po dngos gzhan dang mtshungs pa/_/nam mkha'i me tog de khyad yod/_/mthun dang gzhan dang dngos po las/_/gang gi bdag nyid dngos de ni/_/gal te ldog na mthun pa'i rigs/_/de nyid kyis 
[\[64a.6\]]mtshungs ma yin nam/_/de tshe mthun pa'i rigs rnams kyis/_/mtshungs pa nyid du shes par 'gyur/_/de yi bdag nyid khyad med phyir/_/de bzhin du yang de shes min/_/gang phyir rigs mthun pa yi ming /_/gang gis 'di ni 'gyur ba yin/_/de dang mthun pa'i bdag nyid kyang /_/de dang

[\[64b.1\]]sogs bzhin/_/gal te sngo sogs las min ni/_/ldog pa'i ngo bo'i rten nyid 'dod/_/sngon po la sogs shes pa ni/_/de ni ji ltar skyed yin nam/_/sngo sogs shes skyed [#]sngon po las/_/ji ltar tha dad ldan pa yi[#]/_/rna bas de ni mi skyed de/_/de bzhin mig kyang 'dod par gyis/_/

[\[64b.4\]]gzhan las de dag ni/_/ldog pa nyid du brjod pa na/_/des bskyed ngo bo nyid ma yin/_/de yang kho bo cag nyid 'dod/_/de yi skyed byed de yang ni/_/rang bzhin nyid du brjod pa yin/_/de kun de las nges par ni/_/rang gi rgyu las yang dag 'byung /_/gcig pu'i bdag nyid rjes 'gro'i phyir/_/

[\[65a.1\]]de la gnas pa nyid spyod pas/_/tha dad tha mi dad mi rtogs/_/gzhan du [#]bdag nyid tha dad la/_/log pa nyid du mtshungs nyid du/_/yod nyid dngos po'i rjes 'gro na/_/rab 'jug la sogs thal bar 'gyur/_/bram ze gcer bu ser skya bas/_/rtogs pas dpyad [#]pa nyid

[\[65b.1\]]du ni de bzhin dngos/_/'di ni rdzas ma [#]spangs pa min/_/'das dang ma 'ongs pa'i shes pa/_/gzhan du yul med par ni 'gyur/_/gnyis la brten pa'i rnam shes ni/_/skyob pas ji ltar gsungs pa yin/_/'das pa'i las kyang yod min na/_/ji ltar 'bras bu ster

[\[68a.1\]]gal te dngos po'i rjes 'gro ba/_/'dir ni cung zad yod ma yin/_/de tshe 'jig rten pha rol pa/_/med phyir 'jig rten pha rol med/_/lus dang blo dang [#]dbang po sogs/_/so sor skad cig 'jig pa'i phyir/_/'jig rten pha rol pa rigs min/_/gzhan ni khas

[\[68a.4\]]blo rnams lta bu'o/_/chags pa dang bcas 'chi ka'i sems/_/sems gzhan dang ni 'brel byed min/_/'chi ba'i sems kyi ngo bo'i phyir/_/nyon mongs bral ba de ji bzhin/_/de phyir srog rtsol sogs byin rlabs/_/lus nyid las ni shes pa ni/_/skye bar 'gyur ba rigs so 
[\[68a.5\]]zhes/_/zla ba can dang rta mchog zer/_/nur nur po sogs shes yod la/_/rigs pa med par smra ba yin/_/dbang po dag ni ma skyes phyir/_/de la rtogs bya'i don ni med/_/don rtogs pa las gzhan nyid du/_/ngo bo'i shes pa rigs pa min/_/de tshe nus pa'i ngo bo yis/_/blo zhes bya 
[\[68a.6\]]ba'ang yod pa min/_/nus pa rnams ni rten med par/_/gnas par 'thad pa ma yin te/_/shes pa'i rten gyi bdag nyid ni/_/yod na lus nyid de yi rten[#]/_/mthar ni lus ni ldog pa na/_/shes pa'i 'jug pa ci la brten/_/de yi de ma thag byung ba'i/_/gal te lus gzhan la brten na/_/
[\[68a.7\]]lus gzhan mthong ba med pas ni[#]/_/de ni yod par ji ltar shes/_/glang po rta sogs sems bzhin du/_/tha dad lus la 'jug pas na/_/shes pa de ni rgyun gcig pu/_/'brel pa ru ni ji ltar 'gyur/_/de phyir shes pa'i rten gcig ni/_/thog mtha' med pa'i skyes bu 'ga'/_/
[\[68b\]]
[\[68b.1\]]'khor ba par ni 'dod par gyis/_/yang na med pa [#]nyid khas long /_/de la 'jig rten pha rol ni/_/nye bar len dang blang bya ru/_/'gyur ba'i shes rgyun la sogs las/_/'ga' zhig gzhan ni yod ma yin/_/de yi thog mtha' med pa yi/_/sngon dang phyi ma da ltar gyi/_/'ga' zhig 
[\[68b.2\]]nges pa'i bar mtshams kyi/_/gnas skabs nyid du yongs su bshad/_/mthong ba'i bde ba tsam zhen pas/_/ji ltar de tsam brtag [#]pa yin/_/'jig rten pha rol yul gzhan sogs/_/de ltar de ni kho bo smra/_/gal te de dang tha dad pa'i/_/'jig rten pha rol bkag yin na/_/de tshe 
[\[68b.3\]]bsgrub pa don med de/_/de med nyid la rtsod med phyir/_/rgyun ni dngos med ma yin phyir/_/gnas skabs gzhan ni yod pa min/_/de la gnas pa'i 'jig rten ni/_/pha rol ji ltar de nyid kyis/_/de ltar ma yin rgyun gyi sgras/_/skad cig rgyun can de dag ni/_/zor yang phyir na nags 
[\[68b.4\]]tshogs bzhin/_/ma lus pas ni rab bstan te/_/gcig pu nyid du brtags pa ni/_/de nyid gzhan nyid sogs brjod med/_/nam mkha'i me tog phreng ba bzhin/_/rang bzhin med pa nyid du 'dod/_/de yang thog mtha' med pa ni/_/ji ltar yang dag bsgrub par 'gyur/_/gal te 
[\[68b.5\]]thog mar 'dod pa'i sems/_/de ni rgyu med 'gyur ba'am/_/yang na brtag [#]pa'i rgyu las byung /_/de yang rang bzhin nyid kyis rtag_/'byung ba tsam las byung ba'am/_/yang na gzhan shes tsam las byung /_/ma la sogs pa thog ma'i sems/_/de la rgyu med res 'ga' bar/_
[\[68b.6\]]rigs min gzhan du de yi ni/_/yod nyid rtag tu snang bar 'gyur/_/rtag [#]pa'i yid dus phyogs dang ni/_/dbang phyug bdag sogs kyis byas min/_/de nyid kyis na rtag yod min[#]/_/thal 'gyur phyir ro de med phyir/_/rnam shes gcig pu rtag pa yi/_/rang bzhin zhes smra gzu lums 
[\[68b.7\]]yin/_/gzugs dang sgra sogs kyi sems dag_/gsal bar tha dad snang ba'i phyir/_/sa dang chu dang me sogs pa'i/_/'byung ba las ni skyes pa dang /_/gsal ba min pa'i sems rnams ni/_/cig car 'byung bar thal bar 'gyur/_/'byung ba bzhi po de yang ni/_/gzhan gyis [#]
[\[69a\]]
[\[69a.1\]]bstan pa'i ngo bor 'dod/_/lhan cig byed pa'i ltos la yang /_/'on te 'di nyid skad cig mar/_/gzhan dag gis kyang brjod na ni/_/de ltar yin na de dag gi_/ji ltar grub mtha' la mi gtod[#]/_/de ni nges par gnod gyur kyang /_/rigs pa yin zhes khas 
[\[69a.2\]]len byed/_/sngar bshad rigs pas skad cig mar/_/thams cad nyid du rigs pa yin/_/rang gi phyogs la ltos med cing /_/gal te rigs pa'i rjes chags na/_/'byung ba nyid ni yod min pa'i/_/rigs pa'i mchog 'di 'dod par gyis/_/de dag yan lag can dngos med/_/
[\[69a.3\]]rdul phran dngos nyid kyang rigs min/_/rdul phran rnams ni med pa'i phyir/_/de ltar de ni 'chad par 'gyur/_/bag chags yongs su smin pas na/_/phyi rol med pa'i ngo bo ni/_/rnam shes la ni so sor snang /_/rmi lam sogs mtshungs gzhan du min/_/rnam shes nyid kyi snang ba 
[\[69a.4\]]la/_/brten nas rab tu brtags pa yin/_/gal te 'byung ba bzhi po ni/_/rmi lam sgyu [#]ma dang 'dra ste/_/de tshe de las gzhan med phyir/_/rgyu nyid du ni mi 'thad do/_/sngar gyur 'byung ba nges gnas pa/_/shes pas gzhan ni skyed byed yin/_/gal te rgyud gzhan gyi rnam 
[\[69a.5\]]shes/_/de yi rgyur ni 'dod pa na/_/de ci nye bar len yin nam/_/yang na lhan cig byed pa'am/_/gal te nye bar len 'dod na/_/bu tsha'i shes pa'i rgyun la ni/_/pha ma 'dus sogs 'dus byas la[#]/_/khyad par rjes 'gror 'gyur ba yin/_/gang phyir nyer len nyer blang ba'i/_/
[\[69a.6\]]chos 'di nyid ni rnam gnas pa/_/rjes su 'gro dang ldog pas na/_/rang gi rgyu las [#]nges pa yin/_/rang gi nyer len stobs byung na/_/rgyud gzhan pa yi [#]rnam shes ni/_/lhan cig byed nyid du brtag la/_/gang yang 'gal ba slar yang med/_/de phyir thog ma'i rnam shes ni/_/rang 
[\[69a.7\]]gi nyer len stobs las byung /_/rnam shes nyid sogs gtan tshigs kyis/_/da ltar ma [#]yin rnam shes bzhin/_/rgyu gzhan pa ni spangs pa'i phyir/_/rgyu nyid med par gnas pa la/_/rnam pa gzhan du nges pa'i chos/_/'di ni de yi thal 'gyur min/_/shi ka'i skad cig rnam 
[\[69b\]]
[\[69b.1\]]shes ni/_/rang gi nyer blang skyed nus te/_/chags pas byas pa'i 'brel pa nyid/_/sngon gyi rnam par shes pa bzhin/_/gang gi ngo bos rnam shes ni/_/skyed par byed pa yod nges yin/_/snga phyir de ni ma nyams su/_/zin kyang ci phyir rgyu ma yin/_/pha rol phyogs kyi rnam 
[\[69b.2\]]shes ni/_/lus skyes kho na yin pas na/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i chos ldan pa/_/dpe ni de dag ji ltar 'grub/_/lus ni rnam shes rgyu nyid du/_/sngar ni bkag byas ma yin nam/_/sems rnams cig car thob phyir dang /_/tha dad pa la med pa'i phyir/_/'dod na sdug pa yid 
[\[69b.3\]]byed sogs/_/dran dang chags sogs rnam shes ni/_/'byung ba nyid du rnam par nges/_/de ni dgag par mi nus so/_/goms pa'i khyad par sngar yod pa/_/'phel dang 'grib pa dag las ni/_/blo rnams 'grib dang phul du byung /_/blo la snang bar gyur pa yin/_/don gzhan 
[\[69b.4\]]la chags mi mthun sems/_/don gzhan 'dzin pa med phyir ro/_/shes pa shes pa'i rgyu nyid la/_/rtsod pa nyid du 'gyur ba min/_/de nyid phyir lus tha dad la/_/brten pa nyid du 'grub mi 'gyur/_/sems ni ltung ba'i chos can min/_/lus can rnams la ji ltar rten/_/'bab 
[\[69b.5\]]pa la ni gegs byed phyir/_/chu la sogs pa'i rten du 'gyur/_/'gro ba med pa'i sems rnams la/_/rten dag gis ni ci zhig dgos/_/gal te dag gis [#]bdag nyid gnas[#]/_/brten par 'dir ni rtog na sems/_/lus ni bdag nyid mi rigs phyir/_/khyed kyi de yang 'thad par [#]
[\[69b.6\]]min/_/ji ltar de yi bdag nyid lus/_/gzhan dag gis ni gsal bar rtogs/_/de ltar 'dod chags zhe sdang las/_/sogs pa'i sems kyang cis mi rtogs/_/sems ni bdag nyid kyis rig [#]ste/_/lus ni gzhan dag gis kyang rig_/'di ltar gang dag de tha dad/_/rgyu 
[\[69b.7\]]gser dang ni zlos gar bzhin/_/gnyis su med pa'i phyogs la ni/_/gtan tshigs 'di ni 'khrul [#]ma yin/_/rang rang gi ni snang ba ni/_/rig phyir rab rib la sogs bzhin/_/sems ni skyes ma thag 'jig pa/_/bar chad med par mtshon gyur te/_/lus ni de yi dngos yin na/_/
[\[70a\]]
[\[70a.1\]]skad cig nyid du cis mi 'dod/_/de la brten nas 'byung ba dang /_/de ni ma 'khrul pa yi phyir/_/gal te de la brtan [#]'dod na/_/de ltar na yang 'khrul [#]nyid yin/_/sngar gnas pa yi shes pa yang /_/skad cig so sor 'jig lus la/_/bstan [#]nas rgyun mi

[\[70b.1\]]dran pa ni/_/de [#]yi rnam pa ci mi 'gyur/_/de ni grub pa'i rgyu min no/_/shin tu gsal sogs med pa'i phyir/_/dran pa rab tu mi 'byung ste/_/skyes ma thag sogs sems bzhin no/_/gal te de ltar yid 'di la/_/yod nyid de dag la ci nges/_/sngar bstan gtan tshigs

[\[71a\]]
[\[71a.1\]]'byung ba dang /_/nye bar gnod las skyo bar 'gyur/_/ngu dang nu ma 'thungs sogs pa'i/_/'bras bus de ni rtogs [#]par 'gyur/_/de yang thams cad rtogs bdag nyid/_/de dang ming ni rjes 'brel can/_/ming gi [#]ngo bo goms pa ni/_/skye ba 'di la yod pa min/_/

[\[71b.5\]]song nas[#]/_/sgrub par byed na mthong 'dod gnod/_/srid pa gzhan ni sngon song nyid/_/sgrub [#]byas stong pa'i dpe yin te/_/khyad par med pas bsgrub byas na/_/gtan tshigs 'di ni 'gal ba nyid/_/de bzhin srid la gzhan goms pa'i/_/rgyud [#]nyid yongs su gyur pa'i phyir/_/spyi tsam

[\[72a\]]
[\[72a.1\]]nyid ji ltar snang ba yin/_/'on te rdul phran rang bzhin nam/_/yang na yan lag can mtshan nyid/_/re zhig phra rab rnams kyis ni/_/rnam pa so sor rig pa min/_/phra rab cha med du ma dang /_/lus can shes la mi snang phyir/_/cha shas dbye ba [#]dang bral bar/_/

[\[72a.4\]]kyis ni shes pa bsre/_/gal te skyed byed ma yin par/_/de ci mngon sum spyod yul yin/_/dngos po rnams ni skad cig mar[#]/_/rjes su dpag pas nges pa yin/_/ser po dkar po la sogs ni/_/rdul phran yin par ci las shes/_/phyi rol mig gis gzung bya ni/_/rags phyir 
[\[72a.5\]]ri la sogs bzhin du/_/phra sogs sngon song de la yang /_/rjes dpag 'di nyid yod ce na/_/rags pa nyid ni dngos chos su/_/chos can gnyis la grub pa min/_/rags pa yan lag can ni med/_/rdul phran yang ni de lta min/_/'on te yul ni rgya che bar/_/gnas pa'i 
[\[72a.6\]]gzugs ni de bzhin 'byung /_/de lta yin na 'khrul shes la/_/snang ba'i gzugs kyis the tshom za/_/gal te de [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]de ltar min/_/bye brag rab tu bsgrub par gyis/_/de dang 'di dang khyad par ci/_/gal te don byed mi slu ba/_/don byed snang ba'i rnam shes ni/_/mi slu bar ni brjod pa

[\[72b\]]
[\[72b.1\]]kyis[#]/_/mtshungs dang mi mtshungs dngos nyid kyis/_/rdul phran ngo bo gnyis su brjod/_/de la mtshungs pa'i ngo bo gang /_/de ni mig gi shes pa'i yul/_/des na rnam gcig shes pa ni/_/rdul phran rnams la 'thad pa nyid/_/mtshungs pa min pa'i [#]ngo bo gang /_/de ni rnal 'byor mngon

[\[72b.5\]]kyang /_/rdul phran rnams la the tshom za/_/'di dag la ni med pa yi[#]/_/rtog ldan nges pa ji ltar skye/_/yang dag 'byar dang bskor ba'am/_/bar med rnam par gnas kyang rung /_/dbus nas rdul phran rdul gcig la/_/bltas pa'i rang bzhin gang yin pa/_/rdul phran gzhan la

[\[73a.1\]]gtan tshigs gzhi ma grub pa min/_/gzhan gyi khas len sbyor sogs pa'i/_/stobs kyis gcig nyid sel ba yin/_/de ltar phyogs rnams thams cad la/_/bdag nyid gcig pu de mi rigs/_/gcig ni ma grub pa yi phyir/_/du ma'i rang bzhin yang

[\[82b.1\]]pas ni/_/gsal bar byed par mi nus so/_/gang zhig byed pa de la yang /_/nus pa 'bras bu la yod [#]yin/_/de lta bu [#]yang rnam pa yis/_/chu mchu la sogs nus grub yin/_/de la thog ma nyid yod min/_/khyed la 'brel pa thog mar bcas/_/gal te de la yang spyi ni/_/

[\[83a.1\]]snga ma'i grags pa yis/_/'brel pa grub ces de bzhin du/_/'brel pa thog ma med pa yin/_/zlos nas brjod par 'jug pa ni/_/mi rigs tha snyad yod phyir ro/_/'byin pa'i dang por byar med de/_/de 'dra'i dus ni 'dod pa min[#]/_/'gro ba thams cad res

[\[84a.1\]]rtogs [#]par 'gyur/_/dngos po kun la yi ge bzhin/_/la las [#]'ga' zhig gis gsal byas/_/yi ge rnams la gsal 'gyur na/_/de ni don rtogs yan lag 'gyur/_/gzhan du yang ni yod pa'i phyir/_/de yi rang bzhin rtag nyid yin/_/de

[\[85a.1\]]'bras bu yis/_/de tshe [(]rags [,]rigs [)]par mngon gsal 'gyur/_/des na rgyu las mi mthun rgyun/_/gang phyir skye bar 'gyur ba yin/_/de nyid kyis ni ma byas na'ang /_/'jig pa nges par gsal bar 'gyur/_/de ni tho bas bsnun sogs pa'i/_/brtsal [#]ma thag tu

[\[86b.5\]]ni ltos phyir ro/_/ji bzhin don rtogs rgyu nyid de[#]/_/tshad ma nyid du brtags pa yin/_/skyes bu'i bshad pa la ltos pas/_/'di la tshad mas brtags legs min/_/rig byed tshad mar mi gnas pas/_/slob dpon slob ma'i brgyud pa 'di/_/thog ma med par brtag [#]bya ba'ang /_/skyon 
[\[86b.6\]]med nyid du rigs [#]pa min/_/gang phyir de na gcig kyang ni/_/dbang po las 'das mthong 'dod min/_/de phyir thog ma med pa yi/_/dmus long brgyud pa bstan pa yin/_/long bas long ba khrid pa na/_/yang dag lam ni nges rtogs min/_/de lta min pas 'di la ni/_/thog med 
[\[86b.7\]]brtags pa 'bras med yin/_/yid dang rgyas pa la sogs kyang /_/gzhan las rig byed de nyid shes/_/de dag gis don 'di bkod min/_/zhes pa 'di la tshad ma'ang med/_/bden don rtag pa'i 'brel can gyis/_/tshad ma nyid ni yin yang bla/_/dbang po las 'das sbyor ba de/_/
[\[87a\]]
[\[87a.1\]]'dir ni 'ga' yis nges pa min/_/gang phyir don de dbang 'das pa/_/der gnas sbyor de'ang dbang po 'das/_/khyed la mi kun de dag gis/_/rang gis dbang po 'das mi mthong /_/thos pa de yi bdag gi ni/_/don 'brel 'di yin zhes brjod min/_/skyes bus brtags pas don 'brel nyid/_/
[\[87a.2\]]skyes bus byas pas tha dad min/_/de phyir ji ltar mis [#]byas pa/_/phyin ci log pa nyid du dogs/_/mi rnams kyis ni nyer btags [#]la'ang /_/de bzhin dgos [#]der cis mi 'gyur/_/rig byed tshad mar gnas na yang /_/slob dpon slob ma'i brgyud [#]pa ni/_/thog ma med par brtags pa 
[\[87a.3\]]de/_/dmus long brgyud [#]pa 'dra bar 'gyur/_/me la sbyin sreg sogs tshig gis/_/the tshom sogs med mtho ris la/_/mngon sum gyi ni blo bzhin du/_/mi g.yo blo skyes ma yin nam/_/rig byed kyis de byed pa na/_/ji ltar tshad ma nyid dmigs yin/_/gang phyir tshig des don 
[\[87a.4\]]de ni/_/'ga' zhig the tshom za rtogs yin/_/phyin ci log gang gzhan bzhin du/_/the tshom skyes phyir de bzhin min/_/rtogs pa dang ldan 'ga' zhig gis/_/de la bye brag mthong ba med/_/dbang po las 'das don la ni/_/yod med nges pa rigs pa min/_/gal te 
[\[87a.5\]]rig byed dag nges na/_/gzhan las gzhan 'dra ci ste min/_/dad pas dbang med blo can ni/_/gtsang sbra can rnams mi g.yo ba'i/_/blo nyid skye bar 'gyur ba yin/_/gzhan la [#]gzhan dag la de mtshungs/_/'di ltar bde gshegs pa sogs la/_/mchod sbyin srog bcad [#]
[\[87a.6\]]dang ldan pas/_/ngan song sdug bsngal 'byung 'gyur pa'i/_/blo ni mi g.yo skye bar 'gyur/_/blo 'di la ni 'ba' [#]zhig kyang /_/gnod pa da ltar snang 'gyur min/_/gal te la lar res 'ga' dogs/_/rig [#]byed ngag la'ang de dang mtshungs/_/gal te mi 'dod rag las pa/_/brda 
[\[87a.7\]]ni mi rtogs reg [(]byid [,]byed [)]'dis/_/rang gi [#]don ni gsal byed ni[#]/_/de tshe tshad ma nyid rigs yin/_/de tshe gti mug nga rgyal sogs/_/skyon gyis bslad pa'i blo rnams kyis/_/gzhan du bshad par byas na yang /_/rang gi don ni 'dor mi byed/_/gang phyir de yi yul can nyid/_/
[\[87b\]]
[\[87b.1\]]blo ni myur du skyed par byed/_/skyes bu rnams kyis 'dod pa yi/_/'di ni don gzhan gsal byed min/_/mi [#]'dod la ni ltos pa na/_/skyes bus byas dang bye brag med/_/de la rag las gsal ba yin/_/de yang phyin ci log par 'byung /_/gzhan yang skyes bus ma byas la/_/

[\[87b.3\]]rig byed tshad ma ni/_/gang tshe khyed la gal che na/_/de tshe skyon med byed po sogs/_/nges par bsgrub [#]la 'bad par gyis/_/skyon med pa yi byed pa pos/_/'di byas skyon med gsal byas kyis/_/gsal bar byas na 'jig rten 'dir/_/yang dag don shes sgrub byed yin/_/shes

[\[87b.5\]]mthong /_/me yi snang ba yod pa na/_/mtsho skyes sngon po'ang dmar snang 'gyur/_/gal te me sogs byas nyid kyis/_/de yi rgyu ru 'thad 'gyur na/_/don rnams kyis ni byas nyid kyis/_/log pa'i shes pa'i rgyu mtshan nam/_/de ltar du ba me la ni/_/ji bzhin gsal bar byed 
[\[87b.6\]]'gyur min/_/de ltar rig byed ma byas pa/_/de kho na nyid shes pa yi/_/rgyu mtshan gnas pa ma yin pas/_/de phyir 'di brtags don med yin/_/des na skyes bus ma byas nyid/_/gsal dang rtag nyid [(]gyis kyis [)]grub pa/_/sgra don 'brel pa brtag [#]pa yang /_/don med nyid du brjod pa

[\[88a\]]
[\[88a.1\]]la ji bzhin don/_/shes pa'i rgyu nyid 'thad pa yin/_/skyes bus ma byas yin na yang /_/de ni ma grub ces bsgrubs zin/_/phra ba'i blo yi mig can gyi[#]/_/blo rnams lam gang la dpyod pa'i/_/de yis bdag gis brlabs nas ni/_/'dir [#]ni cung zad

[\[88a.7\]]bus byas nyid rig byed la/_/rjes su dpag pas kyang rtogs 'gyur/_/gzhan du rim dngos nyid min te/_/rtag nyid phyir dang khyab byed phyir/_/gsal bas byas pa'i rim yod min/_/rtag las gsal ba mi 'thad phyir/_/lung dang nye bar 'jal ba dag_/don gyis go la tshad nyid

[\[88b.1\]]de/_/dgag par byas yin de na yang /_/'di dag nyer dgod rigs pa [#]min/_/tshad ma nyid min [#]ldog pa'i phyir/_/rigs [#]byed skyes bus ma byas nyid/_/gang 'dod de yang dngos nyid phyir/_/sgrub pa rnams kyis bsgrub bya min/_/de dag gis thos rang dbang yin/_/skyes bu'i bya

[\[89a.1\]]'jig par ni gang bshad pa/_/de la rjes su dpag pas gnod/_/gsal bar bdag med bstan zin to/_/der ni rigs sogs gzhan smras pa'ang /_/tshad mar gnod byar bsgrub byas zin/_/rjes su dpag pas [#]de yang ni/_/'brel pa nges can sngar bshad

[\[90a.1\]]sogs blo bzhin/_/drug snang la sogs dbye snang bar/_/mngon sum gyis ni nges par 'gyur/_/gsal byed kyi [#]chos 'di min te/_/zhes bya deng [#]ni brjod par 'gyur/_/snga de phyi re'i [#]zhag la ni/_/ba lang sgra zhes kun 'di dag_/don gcig pa min rim

[\[90a.5\]]'di ltar de ni rgyas pa ru/_/sngar ni bstan par byas zin to/_/ba lang nyid ni rtag pa dang /_/'brel par brtags pa'ang spangs pa yin/_/rdul phran mkha' sogs kyang spangs nas[#]/_/des na gtan tshigs kyi dpe min/_/ngo bo gcig nyid 'khrul pa yi[#]/_/shes pa rnams

[\[90a.7\]]'jig pa'i rgyu ni yod pa min/_/zhes bya 'di la [#]bstan zin to/_/de ci sgra la 'byung 'gyur yin/_/ngo shes pa ni bslad [#]pa ru/_/sngar ni rab tu bstan zin pas/_/yul dus sbyor ba po rnams kyi/_/dbye bas yi ge'ang dbye bar 'gyur/_/drug ldan drang srong sa 'dzin dang /_/lnga

[\[90b.1\]]pa la sogs rab dbye bas/_/ga yi gsal ba [#]gzhan gsal bar/_/mngon sum gyis ni rtogs par 'gyur/_/gsal bar byed pa'i dbye ba yis/_/dbye ba'i gnas pa 'di rigs min/_/rtag pa la ni gsal yod min/_/zhes bya 'og nas 'chad par 'gyur/_/ga las 
[\[90b.2\]]tha dad pa yi spyi/_/yang dag par ga nyid 'dod min/_/de yi phyir ni sbyor ba la/_/grub pa la sgrub bzlog par dka'/_/gzhan sel bdag nyid can la yang /_/ga nyid la ni rten yod min/_/rnam pa 'di nyid kyis zhe na/_/de min rten mi 'grub thob 'gyur/_/yi ge ga min 
[\[90b.3\]]las ldog pas/_/ga yig [#]rnam pa nyid du 'gyur/_/chos can gang la chos yin no/_/yang dag par ni rjes dpag kun/_/gnyis ka la grub la ltos yin/_/dpe sogs rang nyid la ltos pa/_/des dpe'i chos can ma grub 'gyur/_/khyed kyi [#]'bad pa 'di kun gyis[#]/_/ga 
[\[90b.4\]]yig gcig nyid bsgrub don du/_/de la khyed la mngon sum gyis/_/gnod pa bzlog par dka' ba'ang smras/_/snga de phyi de la sogs pa'i/_/yi ge ga zhes 'di dag ni/_/rim gyis 'byung bas don gcig min/_/gzugs dang ro sogs blo bzhin no/_/des na yi ge gcig brtan 
[\[90b.5\]]par/_/gnas pa gnyis ka la mi 'grub/_/de'i phyir nyer btags nyid 'di la/_/rtag pa nyid ni brtags [#]pa yin/_/sgra ni dmigs pa'i dus su ni/_/rna ba legs par byas pa dang /_/sna [#]bug mtha' la gnas gyur pa'i/_/rlung rnams rtogs par 'gyur ba min/_/sgra tsam legs 
[\[90b.6\]]byas rna ba yis/_/gang tshe sgra ni rtogs par 'gyur/_/de dang nyer bsres de yis ni/_/rtogs la mngon sum gyis gnod 'gyur/_/mngar ba la sogs la 'jug pa/_/kha dang ser po'i ngo bo [#]yis/_/ji ltar shes pa yul med pa/_/de bzhin sgra yang shes par 'gyur/_/myur dang

[\[91a.1\]]pa la/_/khyed la phyi'i don rnams [#]de nges/_/de yi bdag nyid phyi rol min/_/de bzhin du de ji bzhin [#]rig_/shes pa rnam bcas phyogs la yang /_/phyi rol rnam pa rjes mthun par/_/shes pa la ni snang gyur pas/_/don rtogs zhes ni brjod pa yin/_/

[\[91a.3\]]min/_/bum pa la sogs gzung [#]don du/_/ji ltar mig la mar me yi[#]/_/nus pa skyed byed de bzhin du/_/sgra dmigs phyir ni sgra tsam gyis/_/rna ba la ni nus pa bskyed/_/sgra ni rtag tu dmigs rung bar/_/rang bzhin gyis ni gnas she na[#]/_/rna ba legs par ma byas 
[\[91a.4\]]kyang /_/de ni ci yi phyir mi dmigs/_/de phyir legs byas rna ba ni/_/yod pa'i dus bzhin de yang ni/_/rung ba'i rgyu ni yod pas na/_/dmigs pa kho na yod par 'gyur/_/gal te de dmigs rung min na/_/phyogs kyang de ni ji ltar 'gyur/_/'gyur na [#]rung ba nyid ldan pa'i/_/
[\[91a.5\]]sgra gzhan skye bar 'gyur ba yin/_/yang na phyis kyang shes pa ni/_/de yi stobs las byung ba min/_/legs byas rna ba la sogs las/_/de ni skye bar brjod pa yin/_/de ltar sgra ni shes bya ru/_/mi 'gyur nye bar mkho min phyir/_/ro sogs las ni de la ni/_/khyad par cung zad 
[\[91a.6\]]kyang yod min/_/legs byas rna ba la sogs las/_/sgra la ltos med de dag gis/_/shes pa 'di ni sgra rtogs pa'i/_/rang bzhin zhes bya bskyed pa yin/_/don shes shes bya'i ngo bo 'di/_/de lta na yang sgra 'jug 'gyur/_/khyed la de yi rnam shes kyang /_/zhes bya rjes su 'jug 
[\[91a.7\]]par 'gyur/_/de lta min na rnam shes ni/_/rjes 'jug ma yin de shes bya/_/sgra de rjes su 'jug 'gyur zhes/_/khyed la de ltar 'gal bar 'gyur/_/shes dang shes bya'i rang bzhin ni/_/de dag lu gu rgyud bzhin 'brel/_/gcig yod pa na gnyis pa'i ni/_/thob 'gyur ma 'khrul pa 
[\[91b\]]
[\[91b.1\]]yi phyir/_/legs byas rna bas bskyed pa yi/_/shes dang 'brel nyid yin na ni/_/rna ba legs par ma byas kyang /_/gsal bar shes pa 'phel bar [#]'gyur/_/gal te de yi shes bya yi/_/bdag nyid sgra ni min zhe na/_/de lta na ni rna ba'i shes/_/de rtog rang bzhin 'gyur min te/_/
[\[91b.2\]]de dag [#]nyid min ro sogs bzhin/_/shes pa la ni sgra dang ni/_/'brel pa gang yod gang gis na/_/de ni sgra blo'i ngo bor 'dod/_/de dag [#]nyid ni dbye ldan min/_/de ni de las byung min te/_/sgra ni skyed byed nyid min phyir/_/de las byung ba'i nges pa'i phyir/_/de ni gtan du 'byung bar 
[\[91b.3\]]'gyur/_/de lta na de'i yul can shes/_/rtag tu 'byung ba'am yang na min/_/de ltar nges par gnas pas [#]na/_/rna ba legs byas rtags don med/_/res 'ga' legs par byas pa ni/_/yod pa nyid na res 'ga' yang /_/shes pa 'byung 'gyur zhes bya ba/_/legs byas 
[\[91b.4\]]brtags pa mdzes pa yin/_/legs byas nus pa'i mtshan nyid can/_/shes 'bras nges par byas she na/_/shes pa de yang rtag yod pa'am/_/yang na rtag tu med par 'gyur/_/rnam par shes pa res 'ga' ba[#]/_/de yi legs byas ji ltar ston/_/rna ba legs par byas 
[\[91b.5\]]phyir lan/_/de phyir de [#]ni legs smras min/_/gal te tha mi dad pa yi[#]/_/nus pa rna ba la byed na/_/de tshe nam mkha' phyogs la ni/_/rtag nyid nyams 'gyur bskyed pa'i phyir/_/dngos dang tha mi dad nyid phyir/_/legs par byas pa'ang rtag nyid yin/_/dngos po bzhin du de yis 
[\[91b.6\]]ni[#]/_/de bskyed rnam shes rtag tu na/_/tha dad na ni de'i zhe na/_/'brel pa 'thad par mi 'gyur ro/_/nus pa las zhes 'byung ba'i phyir/_/rna ba'i shes pa nyid kyang med/_/tha dad tha mi dad grol ba'i/_/phyogs ni spangs par byas zin to/_/des na rna ba legs byas phyir/_/lan ni 
[\[91b.7\]]blo dman brjod pa yin/_/de nyid kyis ni yul dang ni/_/gnyis ka legs par byas pa bkag_/de phyir rtag la gsal ba ni/_/thams cad du yang rten med yin/_/des na gsal ba la brten nas/_/ring thung rim pa sogs rnams ni/_/'ga' zhig gang dag snang gyur pa/_/
[\[92a\]]
[\[92a.1\]]de dag kun kyang rten med 'gyur/_/ma phrad tsam du mtshungs na yang /_/thams cad gzung nyid mi 'gyur te/_/nus pa nges par gnas pa'i phyir/_/khab len gyis ni lcags la bzhin/_/gal te khab len 'od phrad [#]pas/_/de yang drangs par gyur ce na/_/
[\[92a.2\]]'o na 'od 'di ma mthong bas/_/ji ltar yod par rtogs [#]par 'gyur/_/ci phyir 'od de shing sogs dang /_/phrad kyang 'dren par byed pa min/_/de nus nges nyid phyir zhe na/_/ma phrad pa la'ang de mtshungs yin/_/sbyor ba'i de ma thag tu ni/_/ring dang bar mar nyer gnas pas/_/rim 
[\[92a.3\]]pa med pas rtogs par 'gyur/_/thams cad la der shes skyed phyir/_/ring dang nye sogs dbye ba yis/_/gzugs ni gsal dang mi gsal bar/_/ji ltar snang 'gyur de bzhin du/_/sgra yang myur dang dal sogs 'gyur/_/tha dad snang ba'i blo rnams ni/_/dmigs pa gcig 
[\[92a.4\]]par ji ltar 'gyur/_/zhe na phyi rol don smra la/_/gzugs blo la de brgal brtag mtshungs/_/phrad nas 'dzin pa'i phyogs la yang /_/rna ba'i nang du sgra'i sgra bzhin/_/'brug sgra la sogs shes pa ni/_/tha dad par ni 'gyur ba min/_/des na skyed byed phyir shes pa/_/tha dad min 
[\[92a.5\]]te yid bzhin no/_/gzhan du res byas [#]la sogs bzhin/_/rna dang ma phrad byed 'gyur min/_/yid kyang phrad par byed pa yin/_/gang brjod skad cig tsam gyis ni/_/shin tu ring ba'i don la ni/_/de dag gi sems 'jug mi 'thad/_/phyi rol dbang po nyid phyir sogs/_/
[\[92a.6\]]phrad nas 'dzin byed nyid sgrub gang /_/bsgrub bya'i chos mi mthun pa la/_/'gal ba med phyir de 'khrul yin/_/ldan pa dang ni rnam dbye ba/_/tha dad pa can sngar spangs zin/_/de nyid kyis ni khyad med phyir/_/'di la rtogs pa mi 'thad do/_/de phyir shugs med pas de ni/_/ji srid shugs 
[\[92a.7\]]ni gnas pa min/_/rna ba'i nam mkhar phyin pa yis/_/de ni rna nus byed ce na/_/ci phyir lcag gis bsnun pa bzhin/_/sgra rigs [#]pa na de shes min/_/gang tshe sgra'i [#]shes pas de yi/_/rig pa so sor bkag ce na/_/lcag gis bsnun dang rlung gzhan bar/_/de tshe rigs [#]pas 
[\[92b\]]
[\[92b.1\]]brjod rigs min/_/rim gyis 'byung yang mgyogs pa na/_/cig car du ni 'khrul zhe na/_/cung zad min rnam pa [#]kun du'ang /_/rlung 'di ma dmigs pa yi phyir/_/'on kyang dbang po las 'das rlung /_/gzegs zan sogs bzhin khyed cag kyang /_/brjod na bshad pa las de yis/_/
[\[92b.2\]]reg bya thob 'gyur ci phyir byas/_/yang na rlung ni [#]dbang 'das bla/_/de dang de sbyor thob 'gyur la/_/dro dang gang [#]dang gnyis ka yis/_/reg bya rig 'gyur gzhan gyi bzhin/_/'on kyang rna mchog la de dang /_/mi [#]ldan pas na rtogs mi 'dod/_/rna ba'i nam mkhar ma phyin ces/_/
[\[92b.3\]]de ni ji ltar rtogs par 'gyur/_/de phyir gtsang sbra can mthong ba'i/_/brtags pa rgyu mtshan med yin te/_/mkhas pa rnams ni tshim byed min/_/nam mkha' cha med rna gang brjod/_/de la skyon ni sngar bstan gang /_/de dag cha med phyogs rna bar/_/'dod pa nyid la'ang mtshungs rtogs 
[\[92b.4\]]bya/_/nam mkha' gcig pu khyab phyogs la'ang /_/legs byas la brten dang gzhan gyi/_/rnam par dbye ba 'thad pa min/_/gang phyir nam mkha' de gcig yin/_/mkha' gcig bdag nyid rna ba ni/_/rna mchog legs par ma byas med/_/de phyir rten gyi dbye ba yis/_/
[\[92b.5\]]legs par byas pa nges mi gnas/_/de bas na rten [#]gcig la ni/_/dbang po legs par byas thob na/_/dbang po gcig pur smra ba la/_/lus can kun gyis rtogs byed 'gyur/_/skyes bu [#]lus kyi phyogs rnams la/_/rnam par shes pa skyed 'dod kyi/_/'on kyang khyed la mkha' 
[\[92b.6\]]gcig phyir/_/gtso bo tha dad par 'gyur min/_/de phyir bdag phyogs dang bral yang /_/ma lus par ni rtogs byed pa/_/lus nyid la gnas 'dzin pas na/_/de ltar smras kyang 'gal ba yin/_/de gnyis na ni rgyu yis ni/_/'on pa nyid sogs lta bur rtogs/_/gzugs ni me 
[\[92b.7\]]long la mngon lta/_/gzugs brnyan de ltar ma yin no/_/chu sogs nang du zhugs pa 'di/_/gzugs ni ring du gnas pa yin/_/rten gyi rjes su byed pa yis/_/gzugs brnyan che chung dbye ba dang /_/ldan gyi gzugs ni ma yin te/_/des na gtan tshigs ma grub min/_/
[\[93a\]]
[\[93a.1\]]mi mthun par ni snang ba yang /_/don shes par ni 'gyur ba na[#]/_/gzugs dang sgra sogs sems thams cad/_/don kun gyi ni yul can 'gyur/_/sor mos cung zad mnan mig la/_/gcig kyang du mar snang ba gang /_/de yang de ltar 'khrul pa 
[\[93a.2\]]ste/_/yul med nyid du 'dod pa yin/_/'dir ni gzugs brnyan 'byung ba ni/_/sngar ni gsal bar byas nyid yin/_/gcig la lhan cig gnyis mi rung /_/lus can thogs pa dang bcas phyir/_/yul rnams [#]du ma la gnas par/_/gzugs brnyan yod pa yin na yang /_/
[\[93a.3\]]che chung sogs rigs [#]dbye ba yis/_/gcig nyid yod pa ma yin no/_/skyes bu rnams la nges pa yang /_/gang la 'di ltar 'jug par 'gyur/_/bdag gi steng na nyi gnas zhes/_/de ni nges par 'khrul pa yin/_/gang phyir srog chags thams cad ni/_/cig car du ni nyi gcig 
[\[93a.4\]]nyid/_/mthong bar 'gyur ba yin pas de/_/gnyis pa nam yang ma yin no/_/des na nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni/_/gnyis pa nam yang yod ma yin/_/snang ba mi dmigs pa yi phyir/_/des na nyi ma gcig tu nges/_/de ltar sgra ni gcig nyid du/_/ji ltar yang ni nges pa min/_/
[\[93a.5\]]rim gyis sam ni cig car yang /_/de ni du ma nyid mtshon phyir/_/gang tshe rjod par byed mang po/_/ga sogs yi ge cig car du/_/sbyor bar byed pa de yi tshe/_/dbye bar gsal bar dmigs par 'gyur/_/rim gyis kyang ni sbyor ba la/_/dbye ba mngon sum 'ba' zhig gis/_/
[\[93a.6\]]rtogs 'gyur ma yin 'on kyang ni/_/rtags las kyang blo'i rim snang phyir/_/sgra tsam gsal byed rag las pa/_/'di yi dmigs bar zad 'gyur mod/_/'on kyang ji ltar dngos gnas pa/_/'di ni 'dzin par 'gyur ba yin/_/de lta min na de 'dzin 'di/_/de dngos mi snang la nges 
[\[93a.7\]]'gyur/_/nam mkha'i cha ni ma lus pa/_/sgras [#]ni khyab cing gsal 'gyur min/_/des na sgra tsam yul tha dad/_/thos pa de cis rjes su byed/_/rang bdag nyid ni gsal ba ru/_/gsal bar 'gyur te bum sogs bzhin/_/'di dag khyed gzhung khas blangs 
[\[93b\]]
[\[93b.1\]]na/_/rgyu mtshan med par brjod pa yin/_/gsal ba med phyir gsal byed rnams/_/nus pa med phyir bsgrubs zin to/_/de 'di yin zhes bshad [#]pa ni/_/mngon sum yin par bsal zin to/_/de'i phyir yul dbyes tha dad nyid/_/shes bya de ni gnod med [#]yin/_/'ga' 
[\[93b.2\]]zhig yul ni tha dad par/_/rim gyis 'gro bar byed pa ste/_/skad cig 'jig phyir tha dad 'gyur/_/de min 'gro ba dang ldan min/_/snga ma'i yul dang ma bral ba'i/_/rang bzhin rjes su 'jug pa'i phyir/_/brtan [#]pas de ni yul gzhan du/_/phyin pas rig pa ma yin no/_/nam mkha' 
[\[93b.3\]]la ni mngon gsal na[#]/_/rigs min nus pa med phyir dang /_/dbang 'das phyir ro sa khung [#]la/_/snang ba de ni de ltar snang /_/gzhan yang sgra ni nam mkha' la'ang /_/mngon par gsal ba tha dad min/_/blo ni gsal yin de yang ni/_/rtag 'byung rgyur ni nyer gnas phyir/_/gang 
[\[93b.4\]]tshe sgra la gsal byed pa/_/ji ltar yang ni rigs pa min/_/de tshe gzhan gyi khyad par can/_/sgra blo la ni 'khrul pa med/_/de phyir ri sogs thams cad ni/_/sgra chos min zhes gnas par 'gyur/_/gang phyir sgra tsam gsal byed nyid/_/yod na der 'gyur de yang bstsal/_/
[\[93b.5\]]shin tu tha dad can de dag_/mtshungs par gcig tu rtogs pa la/_/nus pa can yin sgra dang de'i/_/don ces mang du sngar dpyad zin/_/des na don dang 'brel mthong bas/_/kun rdzob tu ni sgra rjod byed/_/'gyur zhing yang dag pa ru ni/_/rjod byed de min zhes bsgrubs zin/_/sgra 
[\[93b.6\]]las byung ba'i shes de dag_/log par snang bar 'gyur ba yin/_/rigs dang rang gi mtshan nyid sogs/_/brjod bya rjod [#]byed mi gnas phyir/_/des 'khrul tha snyad 'don pa po/_/yang dag par ni ba lang sgra/_/tha dad yin yang mtshungs blo can/_/gcig nyid yin pa snyam du zin/_/
[\[93b.7\]]des na mang du ma 'brel bar/_/rjod [#]par byed pa bram ze yis[#]/_/sgra yi khyad par mdun byas pas/_/gang smras de kun gzhi med yin/_/'di ltar lag brda la sogs pa/_/gcig nyid min te myur bul sogs/_/dbye bas tha dad blo yod phyir/_/de yi gsal ba'ang bstsal

[\[94a.1\]]byas zin/_/de la spyi gcig rjes 'gro ba/_/nye bar mtshon par mi 'gyur ro/_/brda ni dgag pa la sogs pa'i/_/shes pa'i yan lag tu de [#]'gyur/_/sgra rnams dngos su phyi rol gyi/_/don rtogs min te 'brel med phyir/_/des na brjod par 'dod pa 
[\[94a.2\]]ni/_/'di dag gis ni bstan par gsungs/_/de yi 'bras bu nyid du ni/_/'di dag mngon sum mi dmigs pas/_/nges pa yin phyir des na 'dir/_/rgyu 'bras nyid du gsal bar 'gyur/_/brda yi rgyu dang 'bras ngo bo/_/gang gis nges par byas pa ni/_/de yis [#]brjod 
[\[94a.3\]]'dod sgra dag gis/_/rtogs par 'gyur te lag bskyod bzhin/_/brjod 'dod la gnas don dang ni/_/sgra rnams la ni rgyu 'bras kyi/_/bdag nyid can gyi 'brel pa de/_/brda yod na ni skye bar 'gyur/_/de rnams kyis [#]ni 'brel pa ni/_/des na brda las byung zhes brjod/_/
[\[94a.4\]]de yis [#]gsal bar byed pa 'di/_/brda yis [#]brjod ces dngos su min/_/rgyu dang 'bras bur 'gyur ba yi[#]/_/dngos po las ni de gzhan min/_/de dag nyid ni de ltar bshad/_/rgyu 'bras nyid kyi 'brel pa yin/_/de dag kyang ni re re yis[#]/_/bdag cag nyid du rmongs pas 
[\[94a.5\]]rtogs/_/des [#]na brda yi tha dad kyi[#]/_/dus kyi [#]khyad par 'dod pa yin/_/yang dag par ni gcig pu nyid/_/'brel pa 'dir ni yod rigs min/_/tha dad don rnams ma 'dres phyir/_/'brel med tha mi dad la yang /_/gzhan gyi gzhung lugs ma [#]shes pas/_/mi re re la 
[\[94a.6\]]brda re 'am/_/yang na bzlas nas brjod pa yin/_/zhes bya [#]la sogs gzhan gyis smras/_/mis [#]byas nyid phyir de dag la/_/brda 'brel yang dag par yod min/_/de ni de yis [#]gsal byas phyir/_/zhes bya 'brel pa nyid du 'gyur/_/'dir ni bzlas nas brjod pa ste/_/
[\[94a.7\]]gzhan gyis so sor rtogs pa min[#]/_/dbang phyug la sogs bkag nyid phyir/_/'byin pa'i thog mar des byas min/_/re re la ni 'brel pa gang /_/de ni tha dad skad cig gis[#]/_/'jig phyir mtshungs pa'i rtogs pa ni/_/tha dad pa la'ang 'gal ba min/_/de yi yul can

[\[94b.1\]]blo rnams ni/_/rim gyis kyang ni skye 'gyur ba/_/de la'ang rim pa gsal bar smras/_/gzhan du de dag rim med 'gyur/_/mang po rnams la cig car du/_/brda byed par [#]ni yod pa na/_/brda ni tha dad par 'dod min/_/sngo sogs skad cig ma bzhin no/_/smra ba po yi blo mi [#]

[\[94b.5\]]min/_/rten dang 'brel pa yod min te/_/rtag nyid yin pas ltos med phyir/_/des na de yi sgo nas kyang /_/'dir ni 'on pa sogs mi 'thad/_/nam mkha' cha shas med yin la[#]/_/rtag pa nyid kyis 'di mtshungs yin/_/yul der gnas par 'gyur yang ni/_/gal

[\[95a.1\]]na ni 'di rtag tu/_/'byung 'gyur rgyu rtag [#]gnas pa'i phyir/_/tha mi dad pa'i phyogs la yang /_/de 'drar 'di yang rtag par 'gyur/_/de yang bya bar 'dod pa min/_/de las shes pa rtag thal 'gyur/_/de phyir rlung la sogs pa la/_/nus pa nges pa

[\[95a.5\]]pa skyed par nus yin na/_/shes rgyu nyid du gnas pa'i phyir/_/kun gyis sgra 'di yul kun dang /_/dus kun du ni rtogs par 'gyur/_/de ni nus sam mi nus pa/_/rang bzhin gyis gcig bdag nyid gnas/_/'gal ba 'am ni lhan cig byed/_/su zhig gis don 'jig byed 
[\[95a.6\]]nus/_/'ga' zhig la ni shes skyed par/_/rung ba nyid du 'di 'gyur te/_/de phyir skyed par 'gyur ba na/_/'di ni kun gyis rtogs 'gyur min/_/'on kyang ngag la nyer gnas pa/_/sgra tsam gyis gang legs byas 'gyur/_/de rnams nyid kyis sgra thos la/_/ring na gnas

[\[95b.1\]]kyang spong bar [#]byed nus min/_/sgra las khyad par can legs byas/_/de tha mi dad can yin te/_/sgra shes bya nyid mi 'gyur phyir/_/des na sgra yang skye bar 'gyur/_/lan cig sgra ni legs byas nas[#]/_/bdag ni de ni nam du yang /_/spong bar byed min mi rtag par/_/thal 'gyur

[\[95b.4\]]na/_/de dag kyang ni thos par 'gyur/_/ldog na khyed la yig rna ba/_/'di dag gzhan thob cis [#]mi 'gyur/_/des na de dag rnams la ni/_/rtag pa nyid 'dod rgyu mtshan med/_/gang phyir gzugs brnyan blo kun ni/_/dmigs pa med par gnas pas na/_/chu la sogs par 
[\[95b.5\]]nyi ma 'od/_/gcig ni du mar snang ba min/_/ci phyir khyed kyi phyogs la yang /_/blo rnams rnam pa med par gnas/_/phyi rol don ni rnam pa dang /_/ldan pa zhes ni khas blangs yin/_/gzugs brnyan de yang chu yi ni/_/nang du snang bar 'gyur ba yin/_/nam mkha'i dkyil 
[\[95b.6\]]na snang ba yi/_/nyi ma de ltar gnas min na/_/de ni ci phyir de ltar snang /_/'khrul pas she na de nyid kyis[#]/_/de ni gsal bar dmigs med de/_/de yi ngo bo'i don med phyir/_/de nyid gzhan du rtog ce na/_/'di ni legs par smras pa yin/_/'on kyang de nyid gzhan yin

[\[96a\]]
[\[96a.1\]]dang /_/shes pa de ni 'dzin par rigs/_/brgyud nas bkod par gyur pas na/_/'og na gnas pa rtogs su chud/_/gcig nyid yin pas steng gnas pa/_/ci ste 'og na gnas snyam sems/_/gal te ji ltar nyi ma gnas/_/de ltar de ni snang 'gyur [#]na/_/
[\[96a.2\]]de rtogs [#]par ni brtag [#]bya yin/_/gzhan du min zhes bstan zin to/_/shar du song 'jug pa yis ni/_/nub phyogs song 'jug la bkod pas/_/gdong mthong ba na 'khrul pa yin/_/nub phyogs lta bar rtogs pa min[#]/_/gzugs brnyan yul can rnam par shes/_/
[\[96a.3\]]de ni rang bzhin dmigs min [#]te/_/de dang mi mthun snang ba'i phyir/_/ro dang sgra sogs sems bzhin no/_/gang phyir me long chung ngu la/_/gdong ni chung ngur snang bar 'gyur/_/shing sogs thur du [#]bltas nas ni/_/chu gang zhugs pa 'brel gzhan min/_/nyan pa po yang gzhan

[\[100b.5\]]pa'i/_/gang tshe rang bzhin dbye yod na/_/de tshe rtag dngos de dag la/_/de ni ji ltar [#]yod par 'gyur/_/'brel pa la yang rtag pa nyid/_/sngar ni bkag pa byas pa yin/_/des ni 'brel byar mi 'dod pa'i/_/rigs pas ngag rtag rigs ma yin/_/ji lta bu [#]yi ngag la ni/_/rtag pa 
[\[100b.6\]]nyid du khas blangs byas/_/yi ge tsam gyis [#]bdag nyid dam/_/yig rim yang na tha dad yin/_/rim med yi ge rnams la ni/_/brjod pa nyid med de yis ni/_/de dag de 'dra'i ngag ma yin/_/de dag la ni rim pa'ang med/_/khyab pa'i phyir dang rtag nyid phyir/_/de dag la ni yul dus 
[\[100b.7\]]rim/_/me tog 'bras bu sogs dbye bzhin/_/yig bris bzhin du 'thad ma yin/_/de dag rang bzhin rim yod pa/_/mtsho ro [#]zhes bya nyid srid na/_/phyi nas ro mtsho sogs mi 'gyur/_/brtan pa'i rim pa dang 'gal phyir/_/gang phyir yi ge rnam rtag par/_/brjod par byas pa de yi 
[\[101a\]]
[\[101a.1\]]phyir/_/rim gzhan gnas pa'i ro mtsho sogs/_/rim pa gzhan la skye mi 'gyur/_/de ltar min khyod kyis smras pa'i/_/ngo shes pa ni rtag pa dang /_/gcig nyid sgrub par byed pa po/_/'khrul pa can du 'gyur ba yin/_/tha dad pa la'ang [#]'jug pa'i phyir/_/
[\[101a.2\]]gsal ba'i rim pa'ang ngag min te/_/rtag pa'i [#]bkag pa bsal byas phyir[#]/_/ngag ni 'thad pa ma yin pas/_/de phyir rtag pa nyid mi rigs/_/ji ltar 'phel ba la sogs sgra/_/'dod pas bkod pa'i don can rnams/_/mtho ris mchod sbyin la sogs sgra/_/de bzhin du yang bsrings pa 
[\[101a.3\]]yin/_/rang nyid kyis byas gtam ngo bo'i/_/zlos gar gtam rgyud la sogs pa/_/rtag pa'i sgra dang don 'brel pa/_/brjod 'dod yang dag par yod min/_/de la'ang nus rtag nyid yod de/_/nges sbyor mi rtag nyid yin pas/_/der gnas mi rtag nyid [#]kyi phyir/_/nus la [#]'khrul 'jug 
[\[101a.4\]]'gyur zhe na/_/de la sun dbyung phyi rol don/_/med pa nyid ni ma yin nam/_/don gang la ni nus par brtags/_/rnam rtog gzugs brnyan la zhe na/_/de bzhin rig [#]byed la yang mtshungs/_/dbang po las 'das don 'brel pa'i/_/nus pa su zhig gis rtogs 'gyur/_/de phyir rig 
[\[101a.5\]]byed la nges sbyor/_/mi la rag las pa'ang rigs [#]min/_/rgan po la yang de rtogs min/_/de dag mi shes rang bzhin gnas/_/skyes bu la brten bshad pa las/_/rtogs pa de ni srid pa yin/_/byas pa'i lung la brgal brtags [#]pa/_/'di ni mtshungs pa ma yin nam/_/
[\[101a.6\]]der min de [#]ni dad pas sam/_/don la the tshom za bas 'jug_/mngon sum dang ni rjes dpag pas/_/yul ni rnam par brtags yod pas/_/rig byed sgra ni de lta min/_/gang phyir de ni rang las shes/_/byas pa'i don can gtam gyi [#]chos/_/ma mthong ngo bo thos las mtshungs/_/
[\[101a.7\]]de ltar rig byed ma 'brel pa/_/gzhan dag gis 'dir smras pa yin/_/sgra don thog [#]med spang byas nas/_/'brel pa thob [#]med rgyu mtshan nyid/_/gzhan yod ma yin des na 'di/_/rig byed la ni 'brel sogs med/_/thabs dang bral ba nyid kyis na[#]/_/'brel pa byar med rjes su dpag_/
[\[101b\]]
[\[101b.1\]]bshad pa byar med rjes dpag ni/_/mthong ba nyid kyis 'gal bar 'gyur/_/rgan po rnams kyi rtogs pa ni/_/yang dang yang du snang 'gyur ba/_/thabs yin zhes par [#]rtogs brten nas/_/de nyams pa ni mi 'grub ci/_/gal te rig byed don la nges/_/rgan po rnams las mi slu bar/_/grub 
[\[101b.2\]]par gyur na de dag kun/_/de ltar 'gyur gyi gzhan du nyams/_/thams cad bsdus pa'i khyab pa ni/_/grub phyir 'dus pa can nyid kyi/_/rtags la mi mthun sgrub pa ni/_/cung zad srid pa ma yin no/_/don bcas rnam dbye'i don can gyi/_/khyad par rim pa dang ldan pa/_/tshig dang ngag gi 
[\[101b.3\]]tshogs zhes bya/_/yi ge kho na de ltar bshad/_/don bcas rnam dbye'i don can gyi/_/khyad par rim dang ldan nyid ni/_/skyes bus ma byas la bkag zin/_/des na khyab pa gnod med yin/_/rig byed 'don pa brjod nyid la/_/de ltar khyab pa nges pa med/_/des na sgrub pa 'di la ni/_/ldog la [#]the tshom bsal [#]
[\[101b.4\]]bar yod/_/de ltar rim pa byed pa yi/_/mi ni nus med nges byas na/_/'dir ni khyab pa 'di 'grub ste/_/de ni nges par mi nus so/_/ma lus mi yi chos rnams ni/_/mngon sum du ni byas yin na/_/nges pa kho na skye ba yin/_/'di ni thams cad khyed la dad/_/rig byed skyes 
[\[101b.5\]]bus byas nyid du/_/yod par gsal ba srid pa yin/_/'dod pas log par g.yem pa dang /_/srog chags 'tshe dang [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]bshad pa/_/brjod dka' nyid dang thung brjod nyid/_/'brel med nyid dang mi snyan sogs/_/rig byed chos rnams tshad smra pa'i/_/tshig la yang ni yod par 
[\[101b.6\]]snang /_/dug sel nus pa la sogs pa[#]/_/bden pa cung zad gang snang ba/_/de ni mkha' lding bya sogs snang /_/gzhan yang rnam pa 'di yis ni/_/skyes bus byas pa cung zad med/_/gang phyir bde gshegs kyis gsungs pa'i/_/gsung la'ang [#]de ltar rjes dpag nus/_/de yis 
[\[101b.7\]]mngon gsal [#]byas dngos phyir/_/des byas pa zhes brjod par bya/_/byed pa dran pa de la yang /_/de ni don brjod rgyu mtshan nyid/_/de ltar gzhan mi 'dod ce na/_/rigs pa mtshungs phyir ces mi 'dod/_/de ltar gzhan mi 'dod par zad/_/rigs pas de bzhin dogs pa yin/_/thams 
[\[102a\]]
[\[102a.1\]]cad du ni skyes bu rnams/_/phal cher [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]ni smra ba yin/_/ji ltar da [#]ltar yid mi ches/_/de bzhin 'das pa'i don bstan la/_/de ltar de yi khyed smras pa'i/_/rigs par gzhung lugs 'ga' la yang /_/byed po cung zad grub min te/_/rang nyid byas par brjod 
[\[102a.2\]]na yang /_/des na skyes bus ma byas la/_/bden par re ba 'di dor cig_/gang phyir rig byed don 'gal ba'i/_/don ni de dag las bstan yin/_/gzhan yang thog med grub na yang /_/des kyang mis ma byas mi 'grub/_/de las ma byas par 'grub na/_/lung gzhan rnams kyang 
[\[102a.3\]]ma byas 'gyur/_/'di ltar par sig la sogs pa'i/_/tha snyad rnams ni gzhan brten pa'i/_/chad par smra ba'i grub mtha' yang /_/gzhan gyis 'dus byas pa las byung /_/de 'dra'i ma byas nyid grub kyang /_/yod la nan tan ci zhig yod/_/khyed kyi 'bad pa ma lus ni/_/yang 
[\[102a.4\]]dag don grub rgyur bcas yin/_/dus nyid skyes bu nyid sogs la/_/sngar bzhin ldog la the tshom za/_/skyes bu rnams la byed pa yi/_/nus pa med par ma bsgrubs phyir/_/gsal [#]ba bkag par byas nyid phyir/_/smra po byed por shes par gyis/_/de phyir sbyor ba gnyis po yang /_/
[\[102a.5\]]dpe ni bsgrub byas stong par smras/_/mis bstan pa la ltos phyir dang /_/byas pa nyid du bsgrub [#]byas phyir/_/rang don smra po ma ltos nyid/_/chos can dpe dag la ni med/_/ngag la rtag [#]nyid spangs pas na/_/chos can dang ni dpe dag la'ang /_/rtag pa'i ngag las byung nyid ni/_/
[\[102a.6\]]gsal bar ma grub par rtogs min/_/'di ltar bskul ba skyes bu yis/_/ma byas nyid du ma nges pas/_/gtan tshigs phyi ma rnams la yang /_/the tshom za bar [#]grub skyon 'gyur/_/skad cig mas 'jig gsal bsgrubs pas/_/re zhig thos pa brtag [#]pa yis/_/rang gi ngo bo la 
[\[102a.7\]]gnod par/_/des na [#]blo ni gang las 'gyur/_/thos [#]pa las kyang skyes bu ni/_/'jig par mi 'gyur zhes bshad pa/_/sngar ni de la gnod bstan pas/_/de ni ma grub par gsal yin/_/lag mthil skyu ru ra bzhin du/_/yul dang dus dang skyes bu bzhin/_/gang gis mngon 
[\[102b\]]
[\[102b.1\]]sum du ni gyur/_/des der [#]gnod med 'di nges 'gyur/_/mi yis mi shes don ngo bo/_/des na [#]mun pa'i dngos gnas pa'i/_/rig byed la ni dman rnams chags/_/rang gi dpyod la par sig bzhin/_/de yi don ni mi shes pa'ang /_/sdig pa'i rgyu mthun dang ldan phyir/_/srog 
[\[102b.2\]]chags 'tshe sogs sdig pa la/_/de bzhin 'di dag 'jug par 'gyur/_/de phyir chos la brten nas ni/_/bskul ba tshad ma nyid ma grub/_/sgra de dag la bdag gzhan gyis/_/de don nges par rtogs mi 'gyur/_/ye shes snang bas nang gi ni/_/mun pa'i tshogs ni 'joms byed la/_/
[\[102b.3\]]thos don rnam dben ston byed pa/_/tshad mas [#]grub pa 'dod par gyis/_/de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i tshig le'ur byas pa las [#]thos pa brtag pa ste nyi shu rtsa brgyad [#]pa'o//_//rig byed skyes bus byas nyid du/_/de ltar bsgrub [#]par byas pas na/_/'bad mi dgos par de dag 
[\[102b.4\]]la/_/rang las tshad ma nyid kyang btsal[#]/_/rang las tshad ma thams cad kyi/_/tshad ma nyid ni gzung bar gyis/_/de lta bu'i ngag de la ni/_/khyed kyi don ni ji ltar 'dod/_/de dag la ni rang bzhin gyis/_/gzhal bya rtogs sogs nus gnas yin/_/rang la med pa'i nus pa ni/_/gzhan 
[\[102b.5\]]gyis byed par nus ma yin/_/ltos pa med nyid gcig kho na/_/tshad ma nyid kyi rgyu mtshan nyid[#]/_/ltos pa dang bcas nyid rten na/_/de ni 'jig pa kho nar 'gyur/_/rigs pa smra ba su zhig ni/_/rtsa ba la gnod phyogs nges med/_/gang gis [#]ngag gi rang tshig gis[#]/_/de grub thabs kyang 
[\[102b.6\]]'jig par byed/_/ltos pa dang bcas tshad nyid ni/_/la lar yang ni gzhan [#]byas min/_/gtan tshigs thug pa med pa yis/_/bsgrub bya su zhig sgrub [#]par byed/_/de lta'i don ni 'dod ce na/_/dngos po thams cad dag la ni/_/nus pa tha mi dad pa can/_/sngar bsgrubs byas zin ma yin 
[\[102b.7\]]nam/_/gang phyir 'dod 'bras nus pa can/_/rang bzhin nus pa med ces brjod/_/de dag dngos bdag nyid min na/_/dngos de byed pa por mi 'gyur/_/rang rgyu'i stobs las byung ba can/_/de yang mi rtag de 'dra ba'i/_/nus pa tshad ma dag la ni/_/rang bzhin gyis ni yod pa ru/_/
[\[103a\]]
[\[103a.1\]]khyed cag ji ltar 'dod pa yin/_/nus pa rang bzhin can yin na/_/rtag pa 'am ni rgyu med 'gyur/_/de bdag nyid phyir tshad ma yang /_/rtag pa nyid dang rgyu med yin/_/rgyu med gzhan la mi ltos phyir/_/rtag tu yod pa'am med par 'gyur/_/des na der rag 
[\[103a.2\]]las 'bras bu/_/res 'ga' ba ni rigs pa min/_/tshad ma rnams kyi rang bzhin dang /_/'bras bu nyid ni res 'ga' bar/_/snang rung des na nus pa yis/_/rang bzhin can khyed la mi rigs/_/tshad ma rnams kyi rang dngos ni/_/gsal byed kyis gsal snang bas na[#]/_/skyon 
[\[103a.3\]]gzhan la ni ltos nas ni/_/de 'bras rtsom par byed ce na/_/rtag dngos la ni gsal ba dang /_/rgyu gzhan la ni ltos pa spangs/_/de phyir de yi ngo bo dang /_/'bras bu rtag tu dmigs par 'gyur/_/dngos la nus pa tha dad dang /_/gnyis bdag nyid na'ang de lta na'ang /_/rtag pa'i 
[\[103a.4\]]nus dang 'brel pa can/_/de shes rtag tu 'byung bar 'gyur/_/de lta [#]min na rtag mi 'gyur/_/dngos gcig rjes 'gro med pa'i phyir/_/gang phyir de shes dang nus pa/_/res 'ga' 'brel zhing [#]gzhan du min/_/rang gi rgyu las skye 'gyur ba/_/tshad ma rnams kyi nus pa ni/_/rang gi rgyu 
[\[103a.5\]]las skyes 'gyur te/_/phyis rkyen gzhan byas min zhe na/_/'dir ni bdag cag la rtsol [#]med/_/gang phyir rang rgyud [#]las skyes pa'i/_/cha shas med pa'i dngos po yi/_/nus pa phyi nas su zhig rtogs[#]/_/gzhan gyis phyis kyi dus su ni/_/ngo bo gang zhig byas pa ni/_/de ni dngos gzhan 
[\[103a.6\]]nyid yin pas/_/de ni [#]bdag nyid du brjod min/_/gang phyir 'gal ba'i chos 'dre ba/_/dngos po'i tha dad nyid du bshad/_/de grub na de mi 'grub phyir/_/nus pa la yang de yod yin/_/dngos kun skad cig 'jig pa ru/_/bsgrub par byas pa [#]de dag gis[#]/_/nus pa 'ga' zhig rkyen 
[\[103a.7\]]gzhan gyis/_/skyed par byed par mi nus so/_/gang phyir de dag gis gnas pa/_/dus gzhan du ni yod min te/_/gang gi dus na [#]rkyen gzhan gyis/_/'bras bu 'ga' zhig la nus thob/_/'di ni blo [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}#]sangs rgyas pa/_/de dag ji ltar brjod pa yin/_/shes pa 'ga' 
[\[103b\]]
[\[103b.1\]]la nus gnas kyang /_/de ni rang las rtogs nus min/_/ji ltar gnas pa'i shes bya yi/_/dngos po rtogs dang thob nus nyid/_/nyams myong bdag nyid gcig pu yis/_/su zhig nges par byed par [#]nus/_/gang phyir tshad ma ma yin yang /_/skra shad la sogs shes pa ni/_/rnam 
[\[103b.2\]]pa gsal ba snang ba yi[#]/_/nyams myong bdag nyid de yod yin[#]/_/des na nges par bya nyid phyir/_/don byed shes pa'am gzhan ltos min/_/de byed phyir ni ma yin te/_/dug la sogs pa la bzhin no/_/ji ltar [#]dug dang myos 'gyur sogs/_/de las mtshungs gzhan snang phyir 
[\[103b.3\]]dang /_/rjes thogs la ni 'bras med phyir/_/de'i bdag nyid nges gzhan ltos yin/_/brgyal dang dub [#]dang 'chol sogs pa'i/_/de yi 'bras bu nges bskyed na/_/de'i bdag nyid du rtogs 'gyur te/_/da ltar shes la de nus nges/_/gzhan yang tshad ma'i nus mtshan nyid/_/
[\[103b.4\]]rtsod pa med pa nyid du 'dod/_/tshad ma gzhan gyis nges byas zhes/_/de ltar khyed kyis smras pa yin/_/dngos po rnams kyi [#]nus pa rnams/_/'bras bu'i don gyis sgrub par byed/_/des na don gyis go bas grub/_/gzhan las mi 'grub ji ltar yin/_/rang gi rgyu las tshad nyid 
[\[103b.5\]]ni/_/cha shas med par grub yin na/_/de ltar yin dang rtsod med de/_/nges bskyed gzhan la ltos pa'i phyir/_/rang gi ngo bo grub don du/_/tshad ma gzhan la ltos pa min/_/de yi ngo bo nges don du/_/rtogs po de la ltos pa yin/_/gzhan yang tshad ma nyid min yang /_/
[\[103b.6\]]de ltar rang nyid las thal 'gyur/_/rang las tshad ma min med na/_/gzhan las kyang ni srid ma yin/_/ltos pa med pa gcig kho na/_/tshad min nyid kyi [#]rgyu mtshan yin/_/de lta bu sogs 'dir yang ni/_/ma lus brjod par nus ma yin/_/slu bar byed pa'i [#]nus pa ni/_/ji ltar gzhan 
[\[103b.7\]]las nges pa yi/_/de ltar mi slu'i [#]nus pa nyid/_/des na rnam kun gyi gnyis mtshungs/_/bzlog pa de las tshad nyid la/_/phul du byung ba ci zhig snang /_/gang gis [#]rang las de [#]nyid dang /_/gzhan las tshad ma min pa 'dod/_/'on te rang las tshad ma kun/_/tshad ma nyid du brjod [#]
[\[104a\]]
[\[104a.1\]]pa'i phyir/_/skyes nas rang gi 'bras bu la/_/rang nyid kyis ni 'jug 'gyur min/_/bdag nyid thob phyir dngos po rnams/_/rgyu la ltos phyir byed pa ni/_/bdag nyid thob nas rang 'bras la/_/rang nyid kyis ni 'jug par 'gyur/_/des na skye tsam shes pa la/_/
[\[104a.2\]]rang gi rgyu la ltos yod phyir/_/de dag rang gi yon tan la/_/nges pa bskyed phyir ltos pa min/_/gang phyir skyed la rang dbang can/_/tshad ma nyid dang don nges la/_/rang gi rgyu la ma ltos par/_/de dag 'gyur bum la sogs bzhin/_/'jim gong 'khor lo dbyug sogs la/_/bum 
[\[104a.3\]]pa skyed [#]phyir ltos pa yin/_/'di yi chu 'chu [#]ba la ni/_/de dag la ni ltos pa med/_/de ltar rang las tshad ma nyid/_/tha mar gdon mi za de yin/_/gzhan la [#]rag las tshad nyid ni/_/thug pa med par thal bar 'gyur/_/rtsa ba yi ni tshad ma nyid/_/tshad ma gzhan gyis grub byas 
[\[104a.4\]]na/_/de dang der ni der [#]'dod pas/_/mthar thug gnas rnyed mi 'gyur ro/_/ji ltar dang po'i shes pa de/_/don byed mi slu'i [#]shes la ltos/_/de ltar mi slu'i [#]shes pa yang /_/mi slu gzhan la ltos pa yin/_/gal te 'ga' zhig rang nyid las/_/tshad ma nyid du 'dod pa na/_/dang 
[\[104a.5\]]po de ltar 'gyur ba la/_/rgyu mtshan gang gis sdang bar byed/_/gal te de ltar yon tan la/_/rag las mngon sum tshad ma [#]nyid/_/tshad ma med na yon tan yang /_/nam yang yod pa ma yin no/_/des na yon tan yongs gcod phyir/_/tshad ma gzhan ni 'dod pa na/_/de la'ang gzhan 
[\[104a.6\]]gyis yongs bcad pa'i/_/yon tan rag las tshad ma nyid/_/dang po'i bzhin no des na yang /_/sngar bzhin thug pa med par 'gyur/_/[(]da [,]de [)]dang de [#]de ltar 'dod na/_/mthar thug rnyed par mi 'gyur ro/_/'on te yon tan gyis shes pa/_/yon 'dzin rag las tshad ma nyid/_/mi 'dod

[\[104b\]]
[\[104b.1\]]stobs kyis dang po ni/_/de dag la ni rag las 'gyur/_/de phyir rag las tshad ma nyid/_/kun la spyir bstan pa can gnas/_/gnod dang rgyu skyon bcas nyid kyis/_/shes pa dag gis de bsal yin/_/de ni gzhan la rag las kyang /_/thug pa med par mi 'gyur ro/_/de yi 
[\[104b.2\]]tshad ma la rag las/_/de yang rang la [#]gnas pa yin/_/gang phyir tshad ma tshad gzhan gyis/_/ji ltar sgrub par mi byed pa/_/de ltar tshad min nyid kyis ni/_/tshad min gzhan las grub pa min/_/rigs mthun la rten [#]nyid kyis ni/_/gnas pa 'thad pa ma yin te/_/rigs mi mthun 
[\[104b.3\]]ni rgyu gzhan phyir/_/rtsa ba brtan [#]par gnas pa yin/_/re zhig gnod pa'i shes pa ni/_/don gzhan nyid du nges gzung yin/_/de ni ltos med tshad nyid phyir/_/snga ma shes pas sel bar byed/_/de la yang ni gnod pa brtsal[#]/_/slar yang ltos par 'gyur ba yin/_/
[\[104b.4\]]la lar snga mar dogs skye na/_/de yang 'bad pa nyung ngus ldog_/gal te 'di la gnod pa gzhan/_/tshol [#]bas skye bar 'gyur ba na/_/des na dbus mar gnod gyur pas[#]/_/snga ma kho na tshad ma yin/_/'on te rjes mthun 'bad pa yis/_/yang dag 
[\[104b.5\]]tshol bar byed pa na/_/rtsa ba med phyir gnod can la[#]/_/gnod pa'i shes pas gnod mi 'gyur/_/des na dmigs gsal [#]med pa'i phyir/_/stobs bcas de yi dang po ni/_/gnod 'gyur de yis de nyid kyi/_/tshad ma nyid ni bsal [#]ba yin/_/de ltar rtog [#]ldan shes pa ni/_/
[\[104b.6\]]gsum po spong bar mi byed do/_/des kyang gnod pa ma skyes na/_/gnod pa slar yang dogs mi bya/_/gang zhig rmongs pas ma skyes pa'ang /_/gnod pa can du nye bar rtog_/the tshom bdag nyid can de ni/_/tha snyad thams cad nyams par 'gyur/_/'di ltar nor lha'i bu yis ni/_/
[\[104b.7\]]the tshom bdag nyid la smad yin/_/kun ta'i [#]bu skyes the tshom can/_/'jig rten 'di dang phyi ma med/_/la lar gang du ji snyed pa'i/_/mig [#]gsal rnams ni srid pa yin/_/de bdag nyid la de snyed ni/_/brtsal [#]yang ma skyes pas der med/_/'jal byed don du gnyer rnams 
[\[105a\]]
[\[105a.1\]]kyis/_/slar yang dngos po de dag ni/_/res 'ga' 'di [#]ltar srid pa yang /_/nye bar brtags [#]shing gnas mi bya/_/gang la yul dus mi'i gnas skabs/_/dbye ba tha dad kyis grub gang /_/gnod can don du gnyer ba ni/_/de dag la ni ltos pa yin/_/
[\[105a.2\]]yul thag ring na gnas pa'i phyir/_/yang dag par ni ma mthong bas/_/la lar gzhan du dogs 'gyur ba/_/der ni nye bar phyin pa tsam/_/gnod pa'i mtha' ste dus dang ni/_/mi dang gnas skabs gzhan dag la/_/de la ltos pa yod min te/_/smig rgyu la sogs blo bzhin no/_/
[\[105a.3\]]de ltar dmag dang bcas dus su/_/ba lang rta [#]sogs the tshom mam/_/'khrul pa gang yin de'i nges pa/_/gsal bar gyur pa mtha' can yin/_/de bzhin zla gnyis phyogs la 'khrul/_/rig [#]byed yi ge dbyangs sogs la'ang /_/skyes bu gzhan la dris pa na/_/rnam pa 
[\[105a.4\]]gzhan du nges 'dzin skye/_/chags dang zhe sdang rgyags dang bsnyo[#]/_/bkres skom sogs kyis dbang nyams pas/_/shes bya'i don la log shes skye/_/de med yang dag shes pa skye/_/bu lon la sogs tha snyad la'ang /_/gnyis ka rtsod par byed pa na/_/gcig ni bu lon chags

[\[123b.1\]]gang phyir de yi [#]dngos nyid la/_/gzugs sgra sogs pa'i spyod yul can/_/dran pa gsal bar brjod pa yin/_/rmi lam ngan [#]yang yid shes na/_/de kun nyams myong bdag nyid can/_/des na thams cad mkhyen pa la/_/nges byed don can yin rtog na/_/gang gi phyir na mig gis ni/_/
[\[123b.2\]]ro sogs rtogs par 'gyur ba yin/_/mig las byung nyid de rtog na/_/skyon ni 'di ltar 'gyur ba yin/_/yid las byung ba'i sems kyis ni/_/de ni ro sogs mkhyen pa yin/_/gang la yang ni phul byung mthong /_/de yang rang don ma spangs phyir/_/ring dang phra sogs mthong ba yin/_/
[\[123b.3\]]gzugs la rna ba 'jug pa min/_/des na de lta bu sogs tshig_/gzhan gyis rigs pa dang bral smras/_/gang phyir drang don ma spangs pas/_/yid la phul byung 'dod pa yin/_/ji ltar rigs can tshad ma yis/_/ji ltar rigs pa'i don snang bar/_/da ltar 'jig rten na ni mthong /_/
[\[123b.4\]]de bzhin dus gzhan dag [#]la'ang yin/_/rnam par dag pa'i shes kun nam/_/don kun spyod yul can yin te/_/'bras bu 'ga' zhig rgyu yis ni/_/byas pa'i phul byung cung zad srid/_/mya ngam [#]na ni skyu ru'i shing /_/'bras bu chung dang bcas mthong bas/_/de dag thams cad de nyid kyi/_/
[\[123b.5\]]de dngos gzhan du yang 'dug [#]min/_/sbrul rnams mig gis nyan pa yin/_/de yang de ltar grags pa yin/_/des na sems can las nus rnams/_/sna tshogs don bcas srid pa yin/_/gang las dus gsum gnas pa yi/_/dngos po thams cad de bzhin du/_/nus pa [(]ngas [,]nges [)]pa 
[\[123b.6\]]can du snang /_/des ni de ltar brjod na mdzes/_/ji lta'i rigs can tshad ma yis/_/ji ltar rigs pa'i don snang yang /_/de [#]ltar 'jig rten na ni mthong /_/de bzhin dus gzhan na yang 'gyur/_/khyod ni skye bo tsam yin pas/_/nus kun nges par ma byas phyir/_/'jig rten rnams 
[\[123b.7\]]kyis nus pa ni/_/da ltar dus [#]na'ang shes nus min/_/nus pa ma lus thams cad ni/_/shes na khyod rang kun mkhyen 'gyur/_/rnam pa gzhan du yod dogs pas/_/rjes su dpag pas kyang grub min/_/dbang 'das don shes dang ldan pas/_/skyes bu rnams la shes rab 
[\[124a\]]
[\[124a.1\]]sogs/_/yon tan ldan nyid dmigs 'gyur te/_/rigs la sogs pa'i [#]nus yod phyir/_/lta ba can sogs zhes bya ba'i/_/rigs [#]pa yod pa legs bsgoms gang /_/tshe 'di nyid la gzhan gyis sems/_/yongs su shes par byed pa dang /_/tshe 'di la 
[\[124a.2\]]ni ma mthong dang /_/rjes su dpag pa ma thos pa/_/byung dang 'byung 'gyur da ltar ba/_/de kun shes 'gyur smra byed yin/_/sna tshogs sngags dang klu dbang po/_/srin po gnod sbyin la sogs mthus/_/'dir ni skyes bu la babs na/_/mi slu ba can gsal bar smra/_/'khrul pa'i yul 
[\[124a.3\]]mthong med pa na/_/de lta na yang 'dir gnod can/_/tshad ma cung zad yod pa min/_/tshad ma cung zad med pa yis/_/de ni med par 'grub mi 'gyur/_/sngar bshad pa yi rim pa yis/_/rig byed byed pa po bsgrubs pas/_/khyed kyis dbang 'das mthong ba yis/_/gdon mi za bar khas blang dgos/_/des na shes 
[\[124a.4\]]rab can skyes bus/_/don phra rnams mthong nus pa yin/_/rang gi rigs can spangs nas kyang /_/mi gzhan zil gyis gnon pa yin/_/ji ltar 'gro dang [#]sbyor sogs pa'i/_/khyad par gyis ni sna tshogs pa'i/_/nus pa rang yul khyad can 'gyur/_/de bzhin yid kyang 'gyur ba yin/_/'di 
[\[124a.5\]]ltar bya rgod rnams kyis ni/_/gzugs ni shin tu ring nas mthong /_/grub pa'i mig sman sogs rnams kyis/_/de bzhin bsgribs pa'i gter sogs kyang /_/de ltar 'gro ba'i khyad par gyis/_/lta la sogs pa rnams kyis ni[#]/_/phra dang bsgribs sogs mthong yin te/_/rang gi rig pa rjes 
[\[124a.6\]]mthun phyir/_/rnal 'byor goms pa'i khyad par gyis/_/rnal 'byor rnams kyi [#]yod shes ni/_/de ltar phul byung dngos 'gyur bas/_/'dir ni gnod pa can yod min/_/gal te 'gro dang sbyor sogs ni/_/med pas shes la khyad spong [#]na/_/de tshe khyed kyis de rigs yin/_/rgyu med pas ni

[\[124b.1\]]mtho ris sogs ni mngon sum 'gyur/_/'on kyang shes rab brtse sogs ni/_/goms pas 'phel bar mthong ba'i phyir/_/de ni 'phel bar 'gyur bas na/_/phul byung gzhan yang rtogs par 'gyur/_/yid kyi yon tan nyid kyis ni/_/de dag rab kyi mthar thug yin/_/mi smod [#]bzhin

[\[124b.4\]]pa/_/de dag shin tu 'grib pa srid/_/gser gyi dri ma la sogs bzhin/_/de ltar nyon mongs pa dang ni/_/shes bya'i sgrib sogs 'di dag ni/_/ji skad bshad pa'i chos ldan yin/_/de med dri med blo 'byung 'gyur/_/yang na ji skad bshad chos ldan/_/de dag shin tu rtsa 'byin la/_/
[\[124b.5\]]dri ma med pa'i nus yod pa'i/_/de bzhin gnyen po srid pa yin/_/de nyid lta ba'i gegs nyid phyir/_/la lar shin tu 'grib pa can/_/phyi rol nyid kyis [#]mun pa bzhin/_/nang gi yang ni rtogs par 'gyur/_/de ni 'grib par 'gyur ba dang /_/thogs med ye shes chen po ni/_/shes bya

[\[128a.1\]]dus rtogs bzhin zhe na/_/ldog la the tshom za ba'i phyir/_/sgrub pa de ni rigs [#]ldan min/_/da ltar ba yi dus 'di ni/_/de yis [#]stong pa zhes [#]byas min/_/rgyu yi tshogs [#]pa med pa'i phyir/_/'das pa da ltar dus su med/_/'das dus dga' byed la

[\[132b.2\]]shes pa'i skad cig bcu drug gis/_/bden pa bzhi yi rang bzhin can/_/shes bya kun ni rim gyis mkhyen/_/yang na kho bo la kun mkhyen/_/de la de 'dra'i ye shes ni/_/rim gyis gtso bo las byung 'gyur[#]/_/thang cig tsam yang sdod min na/_/lo brgya smos kyang ci dgos 
[\[132b.3\](]kye[,]kyi[)]/_/rnam dbye ma byas pa'i ngo bos/_/dngos po kun ni gang gis rtogs/_/dngos po kun gyi rang bzhin nyid/_/des ni mkhyen par 'gyur ba yin/_/bdag bcas skad cig min sogs las/_/gang zhig log par rang mtshan nyid/_/rtag tu nyer rtogs rgyu mtshan phyir/_/de 
[\[132b.4\]]'dir spyi zhes brjod pa yin/_/de 'dzin byed pa'i rnam shes ni/_/bsgom [#]pa'i stobs las byung ba gang /_/rnal 'byor dbang phyug la mngon gsal/_/de ni rang mtshan spyod yul can/_/de nyid gzhan nyid sogs brjod med/_/gzhan dag gis kyang brtags pa yi/_/spyi de 
[\[132b.5\]]rnal 'byor ye shes des/_/'dzin par mdzad pa ma yin no/_/rnal 'byor dbang phyug gi yid ni/_/de ni rtog bral ma 'khrul yin/_/de 'dzin na ni rtog bcas dang /_/'khrul pa dang bcas thal bar 'gyur/_/gang gis brjod du med pa'i spyi/_/rnam rtog bdag nyid can 
[\[132b.6\]]du bshad/_/rtag pa'i rjes 'gro'i rang bzhin spyi/_/dngos po med par bstan zin to/_/ting nge 'dzin las byung ba yi/_/ye shes 'jig rten las 'das pa[#]/_/rgyu bcas 'bras bu las mkhyen pa/_/de ni bdag gis kun mkhyen 'dod/_/de ni yod pa nyid du ni/_/rjes su dpag pas sngar 
[\[132b.7\]]bsgrubs zin/_/des na de ni yod sgrub pa'i/_/tshad ma yod pas de yang yod/_/rim gyis sam ni cig car gyis/_/shes pa'i 'bras bu bsgrubs pa yin[#]/_/de la yang ni nus yod de/_/gang tshe nyer bstan mdzad pa [#]yin/_/chos la bdag med legs bsgoms pas/_/gang gis bstan 
[\[133a\]]
[\[133a.1\]]pa dri med 'di/_/bstan bcos kun gyis bsgrubs byas pas/_/tshad ma kun gyis gnod pa min/_/'khor ba mi mthun shes pa can/_/khyab 'jug sogs kyi spyod yul [(]man[,]min[)]/_/mkhas pa rnams kyis gang zhig ni/_/dad pas spyi bos len par byed/_/nyon mongs ma lus dgra bo 
[\[133a.2\]]yi/_/tshogs ni 'jig par byed pa can/_/mngon par mtho ba sna tshogs kyi/_/rdzogs rgyu [#]mya ngan 'das thob rgyu/_/thun mong min pa'i thabs rnyed [#]pa/_/skyes bu ma lus dang mi mthun/_/gcig pu de nyid kun mkhyen mgon/_/de ni tshad ma dang ldan yin/_/gang tshe 'di ltar kun mkhyen ni/_/chos 
[\[133a.3\]]la sogs pa rtogs pa'i rgyu/_/'ga' zhig 'thad par 'gyur ba na/_/de tshe skyes bus byas tshig grub/_/rnam pa med sogs dpyad pa ni/_/kun mkhyen la [#]ni nyer mkho min/_/ji ltar khyed kyi shes pa ni/_/don 'ga' la 'jug de bzhin gzhan/_/de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i tshig le'ur byas pa 
[\[133a.4\]]las gzhan gyi [#]dbang po las 'das pa'i don mthong ba can gyi skyes bu brtag pa ste sum cu [#]pa'o//_//slob dpon zhi ba 'tsho'i zhal snga nas kyis mdzad pa de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i tshig le'ur byas pa rdzogs so//_//gu ge yul gyi dben sa ri bo [#]dge gnas 
[\[133a.5\]]phun tshogs gling du/_rgya gar gyi mkhan po dpal [#]gu NA ka ra shrI b+ha dra'i [#]zhal snga nas [#]dang /_sgra bsgyur gyi lo tsA [#]ba chen po dpal lha btsan po shAkya'i dge slong lha bla ma zhi ba 'od kyi zhal snga nas kyis bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa/_grangs can [#]gcer bu bye brag pa dang rgyang pan rig [#]pa can dang sangs rgyas 
[\[133a.6\]]de dag gi phyogs ni/_/chu klung pa dang tsa ra ka dang dus smra spyod pa brda sprod zhi ba tshangs dang sna tshogs tshal/_/sgra yi tshangs pa rig byed mtha' dang bye brag smra dang mdo sde sems tsam dbu ma de dag gi_/bye brag lung bcas shes pa'i spyod yul gang yin 'di ni rtogs nas 'gro la phan phyir bdag gis bsgyur//_//
